defmodule CREMS.Agents.BeamSentinel do
  use GenServer
  require Logger
  alias CREMS.Physics.Alchemist

  @moduledoc """
  The Ghost Introspector: Monitors a BEAM process and transmutes its runtime stats into Feelings.
  """

  defstruct [
    :node_id,
    :target_pid,      # PID (BEAM PID)
    :monitor_ref,
    :sampling_rate,
    :last_metrics,
    :forensic_log
  ]

  # --- Client API ---

  def start_link(opts) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, opts)
  end

  # --- Callbacks ---

  @impl true
  def init(opts) do
    node_id = get_opt(opts, :node_id)
    target_pid = get_opt(opts, :target_pid)
    
    # Monitor to detect death immediately
    ref = Process.monitor(target_pid)
    sampling_rate = 1000

    Logger.info("BeamSentinel attached to BEAM PID #{inspect(target_pid)} for Node #{node_id}")
    
    # RIGOROUS: Enable Transfer Ratio Tracking via sys.statistics
    try do
      :sys.statistics(target_pid, true)
    rescue
      _ -> Logger.warning("BeamSentinel Warning: Target #{inspect(target_pid)} does not support sys stats.")
    end
    
    Process.send_after(self(), :grip_reality, sampling_rate)

    {:ok, %__MODULE__{
      node_id: node_id,
      target_pid: target_pid,
      monitor_ref: ref,
      sampling_rate: sampling_rate,
      last_metrics: nil,
      forensic_log: []
    }}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(:grip_reality, state) do
    # 1. Grip Reality (Process.info + sys.statistics)
    # RIGOROUS: We need both basic info and message counters
    case Process.info(state.target_pid, [:message_queue_len, :total_heap_size, :reductions, :status]) do
      nil -> {:noreply, state} # handled by DOWN
         
      info_list ->
         base_metrics = Map.new(info_list)
         sys_metrics = get_sys_stats(state.target_pid)
         
         current_metrics = Map.merge(base_metrics, sys_metrics)
         timestamp = System.monotonic_time(:millisecond)
         
         # 2. Calculate Deltas
         deltas = calculate_deltas(current_metrics, state.last_metrics, timestamp)
         
         # 3. Transmute
         feelings = Alchemist.transmute_beam_metrics(deltas)
         
         # 4. Update Node
         update_node(state.node_id, feelings, deltas)
         
         # 5. Forensic Logging
         new_log = Enum.take([feelings.effect | state.forensic_log], 10)
         
         Process.send_after(self(), :grip_reality, state.sampling_rate)
         {:noreply, %{state | last_metrics: Map.put(current_metrics, :timestamp, timestamp), forensic_log: new_log}}
    end
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info({:DOWN, ref, :process, _pid, reason}, %{monitor_ref: ref} = state) do
     Logger.warning("[BeamSentinel] Target BEAM process died. Reason: #{inspect(reason)}. Executing Necrosis Protocol.")
     
     # NECROSIS PROTOCOL: Death Signal Detected (Section 8.1)
     
     # 1. Capture Final State
     # Convert BEAM reason to a numeric exit code for the autopsy protocol
     exit_code = case reason do
       :normal -> 0
       :shutdown -> 0
       {:shutdown, _} -> 0
       _ -> -1 # Any other reason is a crash
     end

     # Final buffer from last known stats (Historical Forensic Context)
     final_buffer = state.forensic_log
     
     # 2. Trigger Autopsy via TrackingAgent (Section 8.2)
     case Registry.lookup(CMS.NodeRegistry, {:tracking_agent, state.node_id}) do
       [{tracker_pid, _}] ->
         GenServer.cast(tracker_pid, {:execute_autopsy, exit_code, final_buffer})
         Logger.info("[BeamSentinel] Autopsy triggered for Node #{state.node_id}")
       [] ->
         Logger.warning("[BeamSentinel] No TrackingAgent found for Node #{state.node_id}")
     end
     
     # 3. Sentinel Terminates (Resource Release - Section 8.2)
     {:stop, :normal, state}
  end

  # --- Internals ---
  
  defp get_sys_stats(pid) do
    try do
       {:ok, stats} = :sys.statistics(pid, :get)
       # stats list: [start_time: _, current_time: _, reductions: _, messages_in: X, messages_out: Y]
       %{
         msgs_in: Keyword.get(stats, :messages_in, 0),
         msgs_out: Keyword.get(stats, :messages_out, 0)
       }
    rescue
       _ -> %{msgs_in: 0, msgs_out: 0}
    end
  end

  defp calculate_deltas(current, nil, _ts) do
     Map.merge(current, %{
        reductions_delta: 0,
        net_in_delta: 0,
        net_out_delta: 0
     })
  end

  defp calculate_deltas(current, last, ts) do
     dt_sec = (ts - last.timestamp) / 1000.0
     dt_sec = if dt_sec <= 0, do: 1.0, else: dt_sec
     
     red_delta = (current[:reductions] - last[:reductions]) / dt_sec
     
     # Calculate Transfer Ratio components (Messages/sec)
     in_delta = (current.msgs_in - last.msgs_in) / dt_sec
     out_delta = (current.msgs_out - last.msgs_out) / dt_sec
     
     Map.merge(current, %{
        reductions_delta: red_delta,
        net_in_delta: in_delta, 
        net_out_delta: out_delta 
        # Note: net_in/out mapped to message counts for Alchemist compatibility? 
        # Alchemist expects 'net_in_delta' for Agility bonus, which works fine with msg counts.
     })
  end

  defp update_node(node_id, feelings, raw_deltas) do
    case Registry.lookup(CMS.NodeRegistry, node_id) do
       [{pid, _}] ->
          # 1. Update Physiology
          GenServer.cast(pid, {:update_physics, feelings, raw_deltas})
          
          # 2. Update Transfer Ratio (Rigorous)
          input = max(Map.get(raw_deltas, :net_in_delta, 0.0001), 0.0001)
          output = Map.get(raw_deltas, :net_out_delta, 0.0)
          ratio = output / input
          
          GenServer.cast(pid, {:update_communication_edge, "beam:link", ratio})

          # Spec 7.2: Trigger Archival on Sentinel Event (Significant Stress or Death)
          if feelings.stress > 0.8 or feelings.state == :terminated do
             case Registry.lookup(CMS.NodeRegistry, {:tracking_agent, node_id}) do
                [{t_pid, _}] -> GenServer.cast(t_pid, :trigger_archival)
                _ -> :ok
             end
          end
          
       [] -> :ok
    end
  end

  defp get_opt(opts, key) when is_list(opts), do: Keyword.fetch!(opts, key)
  defp get_opt(opts, key) when is_map(opts), do: Map.fetch!(opts, key)
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: ghost_scavenger.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CREMS.Agents.GhostScavenger do
  @moduledoc """
  The Death Reaper: Automated Ghost Node Scavenger.
  
  Periodically scans for expired ghost nodes, archives their ChronoStacks
  to EpochLog for long-term forensic storage, and gracefully terminates them.
  
  Configuration:
  - Scan interval: Default 1 hour (configurable)
  - Retention period: Default 24 hours (configurable)
  """

  use GenServer
  require Logger

  alias CREMS.Persistence.EpochLog

  @default_interval_ms 3_600_000  # 1 hour
  @default_retention_hours 24

  defstruct [
    :scan_interval_ms,
    :retention_hours,
    :metrics
  ]

  # --- Client API ---

  def start_link(opts \\ []) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, opts, name: __MODULE__)
  end

  @doc """
  Manually trigger a scavenge cycle (useful for testing).
  """
  def trigger_scavenge do
    GenServer.cast(__MODULE__, :scavenge)
  end

  @doc """
  Get scavenger metrics.
  """
  def get_metrics do
    GenServer.call(__MODULE__, :get_metrics)
  end

  # --- Callbacks ---

  @impl true
  def init(opts) do
    scan_interval = Keyword.get(opts, :scan_interval_ms, @default_interval_ms)
    retention_hours = Keyword.get(opts, :retention_hours, @default_retention_hours)

    # Schedule first scavenge
    schedule_scavenge(scan_interval)

    Logger.info("[GhostScavenger] Started. Scan interval: #{scan_interval}ms, Retention: #{retention_hours}h")

    {:ok, %__MODULE__{
      scan_interval_ms: scan_interval,
      retention_hours: retention_hours,
      metrics: %{
        total_scavenged: 0,
        total_archived: 0,
        last_scavenge_time: nil,
        errors: 0
      }
    }}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast(:scavenge, state) do
    new_state = perform_scavenge(state)
    {:noreply, new_state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_call(:get_metrics, _from, state) do
    {:reply, state.metrics, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(:scavenge_tick, state) do
    new_state = perform_scavenge(state)
    schedule_scavenge(state.scan_interval_ms)
    {:noreply, new_state}
  end

  # --- Internal Logic ---

  defp perform_scavenge(state) do
    Logger.info("[GhostScavenger] Starting scavenge cycle...")
    start_time = System.monotonic_time(:millisecond)

    # 1. Find all registered CREMS nodes
    all_nodes = Registry.select(CMS.NodeRegistry, [{{:"$1", :"$2", :"$3"}, [], [{{:"$1", :"$2"}}]}])

    # 2. Filter for ghost nodes with expired retention
    expired_ghosts = 
      all_nodes
      |> Enum.filter(fn {node_id, pid} -> is_ghost_expired?(node_id, pid, state.retention_hours) end)

    ghost_count = length(expired_ghosts)
    Logger.info("[GhostScavenger] Found #{ghost_count} expired ghost(s)")

    # 3. Archive and terminate each expired ghost
    {archived, errors} = 
      Enum.reduce(expired_ghosts, {0, 0}, fn {node_id, pid}, {arch_acc, err_acc} ->
        case scavenge_ghost(node_id, pid) do
          :ok -> {arch_acc + 1, err_acc}
          :error -> {arch_acc, err_acc + 1}
        end
      end)

    elapsed = System.monotonic_time(:millisecond) - start_time
    
    Logger.info("[GhostScavenger] Cycle complete. Archived: #{archived}, Errors: #{errors}, Time: #{elapsed}ms")

    # Update metrics
    new_metrics = %{
      total_scavenged: state.metrics.total_scavenged + ghost_count,
      total_archived: state.metrics.total_archived + archived,
      last_scavenge_time: DateTime.utc_now(),
      errors: state.metrics.errors + errors
    }

    %{state | metrics: new_metrics}
  end

  defp is_ghost_expired?(_node_id, pid, _retention_hours) do
    try do
      # Request state snapshot
      case GenServer.call(pid, :get_state_snapshot, 5000) do
        {:ok, node} ->
          # Check if it has ghost metadata
          case node do
            %{ghost_metadata: %{is_ghost: true, retention_expiry: expiry}} ->
              DateTime.compare(DateTime.utc_now(), expiry) == :gt
            _ ->
              false
          end
        _ ->
          false
      end
    rescue
      _ -> false
    catch
      :exit, _ -> false
    end
  end

  defp scavenge_ghost(node_id, pid) do
    try do
      # 1. Get full state snapshot
      case GenServer.call(pid, :get_state_snapshot, 5000) do
        {:ok, node} ->
          # 2. Archive ChronoStack to EpochLog
          metadata = %{
            node_id: node_id,
            exit_code: get_in(node, [:ghost_metadata, :exit_code]) || -1,
            death_time: get_in(node, [:ghost_metadata, :death_time]) || DateTime.utc_now(),
            tags: node.body.semantic_tags || [],
            process_role: extract_process_role(node.body.natural_language_fact)
          }

          case EpochLog.archive_ghost(node_id, node.body.data_tail.chrono_stack, metadata) do
            {:ok, archive_path} ->
              Logger.info("[GhostScavenger] Archived ghost #{node_id} to #{archive_path}")

              # 3. Gracefully terminate the NodeActor
              Process.exit(pid, :scavenged)
              
              Logger.info("[GhostScavenger] Terminated ghost #{node_id}")
              :ok

            {:error, reason} ->
              Logger.error("[GhostScavenger] Failed to archive ghost #{node_id}: #{inspect(reason)}")
              :error
          end

        _ ->
          Logger.warning("[GhostScavenger] Failed to get state for ghost #{node_id}")
          :error
      end
    rescue
      e ->
        Logger.error("[GhostScavenger] Error scavenging ghost #{node_id}: #{inspect(e)}")
        :error
    catch
      :exit, reason ->
        Logger.error("[GhostScavenger] Exit while scavenging ghost #{node_id}: #{inspect(reason)}")
        :error
    end
  end

  defp extract_process_role(natural_language_fact) when is_binary(natural_language_fact) do
    # Try to extract role from facts like "I am a postgres process..."
    case Regex.run(~r/I am (?:a |an )?([a-z_]+)/i, natural_language_fact) do
      [_, role] -> role
      _ -> "unknown"
    end
  end
  defp extract_process_role(_), do: "unknown"

  defp schedule_scavenge(interval_ms) do
    Process.send_after(self(), :scavenge_tick, interval_ms)
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: tracking_agent.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CREMS.Agents.TrackingAgent do
  use GenServer
  require Logger

  @moduledoc """
  The Vigilant Observer (Phase 7).
  While the Sentinel feels (fast loop), the Tracker remembers and learns (slow loop).
  
  Roles:
  1. Significant State Archival (Memory).
  2. Curve Fitting (Learning Physics for Phantom Manifold).
  """

  defstruct [
    :node_id, 
    :target_id, 
    :history_limit,
    :last_snapshot_hash,
    :last_state
  ]

  # --- Client API ---

  def start_link(opts) do
    node_id = Keyword.fetch!(opts, :node_id)
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, opts, name: via_tuple(node_id))
  end

  # --- Callbacks ---

  @impl true
  def init(opts) do
    node_id = Keyword.fetch!(opts, :node_id)
    target_id = Keyword.fetch!(opts, :target_id)
    history_limit = Keyword.get(opts, :history_limit, 50)

    # Vigilant Loop: 1 second wakeup
    Process.send_after(self(), :observe, 60_000)
    
    Logger.info("[TRACKER] Vigilant Observer started for Node #{node_id}")
    
    {:ok, %__MODULE__{
      node_id: node_id, 
      target_id: target_id, 
      history_limit: history_limit,
      last_snapshot_hash: nil,
      last_state: nil
    }}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast(:trigger_archival, state) do
    # Immediate wake-up on Sentinel Event (Role 1)
    new_state = observe_reality(state)
    {:noreply, new_state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:record_event, type, metadata}, state) do
    Logger.debug("[TRACKER] Event recorded for #{state.node_id}: #{type} | #{inspect(metadata)}")
    # We can expand this later to persist significant events to the ChronoStack
    {:noreply, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:execute_autopsy, exit_code, final_buffer}, state) do
    Logger.warning("[TRACKER] AUTOPSY PROTOCOL: Executing for Node #{state.node_id}. Exit Code: #{exit_code}")
    
    # Section 8.2: The Autopsy Protocol
    
    # 1. Retrieve current node state (Last Known State)
    case get_node_snapshot(state.node_id) do
      {:ok, node} ->
        # 2. Determine death classification
        is_crashed = exit_code != 0
        death_state = if is_crashed, do: :crashed, else: :terminated
        
        Logger.info("[TRACKER] Death Classification: #{death_state} (Exit Code: #{exit_code})")
        
        # 3. Update Node State via NodeActor
        state_updates = %{
          fid_state: :terminated,  # Unified state
          fid_satisfaction: if(is_crashed, do: 0.0, else: node.body.fid_satisfaction),
          fid_output: final_buffer
        }
        
        # Apply state updates
        case Registry.lookup(CMS.NodeRegistry, state.node_id) do
          [{pid, _}] ->
            # Update physiological state
            GenServer.cast(pid, {:update_death_state, state_updates})
            
            # 4. Final Archive: Push active state to ChronoStack
            # This preserves the crash context (High Stress/Strain)
            final_snapshot = %{
              id: node.id,
              head_config: %{
                urgency: node.head.urgency_threshold,
                state: node.head.cognitive_state
              },
              physiology: %{
                state: :terminated,
                satisfaction: state_updates.fid_satisfaction,
                stress: node.body.fid_stress,
                strain: node.body.fid_strain,
                agility: node.body.fid_agility,
                telemetry: node.body.fid_telemetry,
                effect: node.body.fid_effect,
                exit_code: exit_code,
                final_output: final_buffer
              },
              topology: %{
                hierarchical: node.tail.hierarchical_edges,
                communication_count: length(node.tail.communication_edges),
                dependency_count: length(node.tail.dependency_edges)
              },
              timestamp: DateTime.utc_now(),
              death_classification: death_state
            }
            
            GenServer.cast(pid, {:push_history, final_snapshot})
            
            # 5. Trigger Ghost Transition (Section 8.3)
            retention_hours = Application.get_env(:cms, :crems_ghost_retention_hours, 24)
            retention_expiry = DateTime.add(DateTime.utc_now(), retention_hours * 3600, :second)
            
            GenServer.cast(pid, {:enter_ghost_mode, exit_code, final_snapshot, retention_expiry})
            
            Logger.info("[TRACKER] Autopsy complete. Node #{state.node_id} transitioned to Ghost Mode. Expiry: #{retention_expiry}")
            
          [] ->
            Logger.error("[TRACKER] NodeActor not found during autopsy for #{state.node_id}")
        end
        
        # 6. Tracker Terminates (Resource Release)
        {:stop, :normal, state}
        
      {:error, reason} ->
        Logger.error("[TRACKER] Failed to retrieve node state during autopsy: #{inspect(reason)}")
        {:stop, :normal, state}
    end
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(:observe, state) do
    new_state = observe_reality(state)
    
    # Schedule next observation (Periodic Role 1)
    Process.send_after(self(), :observe, 60_000)
    {:noreply, new_state}
  end

  # --- Internal Logic ---

  defp observe_reality(state) do
    case get_node_snapshot(state.node_id) do
      {:ok, node} ->
        # 1. Evaluate Significance
        current_hash = compute_hash(node)
        
        if significant_change?(node, state) or current_hash != state.last_snapshot_hash do
          archive_state(node)
          learn_physics(node)
          %{state | last_snapshot_hash: current_hash, last_state: node}
        else
          state
        end
        
      _ -> state
    end
  end

  defp get_node_snapshot(node_id) do
    case Registry.lookup(CMS.NodeRegistry, node_id) do
      [{pid, _}] -> GenServer.call(pid, :get_state_snapshot)
      _ -> {:error, :not_found}
    end
  end

  defp compute_hash(node) do
    # Only hash relevant parts for noise rejection
    # We care about identity, state, and topology
    data_to_hash = {
      :ignore_state, # Patched: State ignored in hash to prevent blinking loops
      node.body.natural_language_fact,
      length(node.tail.hierarchical_edges),
      length(node.tail.communication_edges)
    }
    :crypto.hash(:sha256, :erlang.term_to_binary(data_to_hash)) |> Base.encode16()
  end

  defp significant_change?(node, state) do
    old_node = state.last_state
    
    cond do
      is_nil(old_node) -> true
      
      # 1. State Change
      normalize_state(node.body.fid_state) != normalize_state(old_node.body.fid_state) -> true
      
      # 2. Topology Change (New Child / Parent / Network Link)
      length(node.tail.hierarchical_edges) != length(old_node.tail.hierarchical_edges) or
      length(node.tail.communication_edges) != length(old_node.tail.communication_edges) -> true
      
      # 3. Satisfaction Collapse
      node.body.fid_satisfaction < 0.5 and old_node.body.fid_satisfaction >= 0.5 -> true
      
      # 4. Stress Jump (Transition from Low/Normal to Critical)
      (old_node.body.fid_stress < 0.5 and node.body.fid_stress > 0.8) -> true
      
      true -> false
    end
  end

  defp archive_state(node) do
    Logger.info("[TRACKER] Significant transition detected for #{node.id}. Archiving to ChronoStack.")
    
    # Snapshot: A deep copy of the Node state (Spec 7.1)
    # We strip the large artifacts like embeddings or chrono_stack itself to avoid recursive bloat,
    # but keep all logical state, config, relationships, and "feelings".
    snapshot_data = %{
      id: node.id,
      head_config: %{
        urgency: node.head.urgency_threshold,
        state: node.head.cognitive_state
      },
      physiology: %{
        state: :ignore_state, # Patched: State ignored in hash to prevent blinking loops
        satisfaction: node.body.fid_satisfaction,
        stress: node.body.fid_stress,
        strain: node.body.fid_strain,
        agility: node.body.fid_agility,
        telemetry: node.body.fid_telemetry,
        effect: node.body.fid_effect
      },
      topology: %{
        hierarchical: node.tail.hierarchical_edges, # Spec: Relationships (Deep copy)
        communication_count: length(node.tail.communication_edges),
        dependency_count: length(node.tail.dependency_edges)
      },
      timestamp: DateTime.utc_now()
    }
    
    case Registry.lookup(CMS.NodeRegistry, node.id) do
      [{pid, _}] -> GenServer.cast(pid, {:push_history, snapshot_data})
      _ -> :ok
    end
  end

  # --- Role 2: Curve Fitting (Learning Physics) ---

  defp learn_physics(node) do
    # Scan: Reads the local ChronoStack history (Spec 7.2)
    history = node.body.data_tail.chrono_stack.stack_layers
    
    if length(history) > 4 do
       # Correlate: Plots Input_Load vs Stress vs Agility (Spec 7.2)
       data_points = Enum.map(history, fn snap ->
         # Input_Load (Network/Message Volume) as per Spec 7.2 Role 2
         
         # Handle polymorphic snapshot structure (Nested vs Flat Initial)
         data = snap.data
         
         {physiology, topology} = 
           case Map.get(data, :physiology) do
             nil -> 
               # Flat Initial State (Fallback)
               {data, %{communication_count: 0}}
             phys -> 
               # Nested Standard State
               {phys, Map.get(data, :topology) || %{}}
           end

         telemetry = Map.get(physiology, :telemetry) || %{}
         
         # Safe access to communication_count (struct or map)
         comm_count = Map.get(topology, :communication_count, 0)
         
         load = comm_count + (Map.get(telemetry, "cpu_usage", 0.0) * 10)
         
         %{load: load, stress: Map.get(physiology, :stress, 0.0), agility: Map.get(physiology, :agility, 0.0)}
       end)

       # 1. Derive elasticity_coefficient (Spec 7.2)
       # For exponential "Hockey Stick" detection, we use log-log regression:
       # log(Stress) = elasticity * log(Load)
       # We add small epsilon to avoid log(0)
       log_points = 
         data_points 
         |> Enum.map(fn p -> {:math.log(max(p.load, 0.001)), :math.log(max(p.stress, 0.001))} end)
       
       elasticity = 
         case CMS.Core.Logic.CognitiveMath.linear_regression(log_points) do
           {:ok, %{slope: m}} -> max(1.0, m)
           _ -> 1.0
         end

       # 2. Derive saturation_point (Spec 7.2)
       # Finds the load value where Agility historically collapsed to near zero via regression projection.
       agility_points = Enum.map(data_points, fn p -> {p.load, p.agility} end)
       
       saturation_point = 
         case CMS.Core.Logic.CognitiveMath.linear_regression(agility_points) do
           {:ok, %{slope: m, intercept: b}} when m < 0 ->
             # Agility = m * Load + b. Set Agility = 0 -> Load = -b/m
             # We want the x-intercept
             x_intercept = -b / m
             # If Agility is already low in history, use the min load where it was low as a safe floor
             historical_floor = 
               data_points 
               |> Enum.filter(&(&1.agility < 0.2)) 
               |> Enum.map(& &1.load) 
               |> case do
                 [] -> x_intercept
                 loads -> Enum.min(loads)
               end
             min(x_intercept, historical_floor) |> max(1.0)

           _ -> 
             # Fallback to simple heuristic if data is flat or rising
             data_points 
             |> Enum.filter(&(&1.agility < 0.2)) 
             |> Enum.map(& &1.load) 
             |> case do
               [] -> 1.0 
               loads -> Enum.min(loads) |> max(1.0)
             end
          end

       Logger.debug("[TRACKER] Physics Derived for #{node.id}: Elasticity=#{Float.round(elasticity, 2)}, Saturation=#{Float.round(saturation_point, 2)}")
       
       # Update: Writes learned constants to fid:physics_model (Spec 7.2)
       case Registry.lookup(CMS.NodeRegistry, node.id) do
         [{pid, _}] -> 
           GenServer.cast(pid, {
             :update_model_constants, 
             %{elasticity_coefficient: elasticity, saturation_point: saturation_point}
           })
         _ -> :ok
       end
    end
  end
  defp via_tuple(node_id) do
    {:via, Registry, {CMS.NodeRegistry, {:tracking_agent, node_id}}}
  end

  # --- Patch Helpers ---
  defp normalize_state(state) when state in [:running, :runnable, :waiting, :sleeping, :active], do: :alive
  defp normalize_state(state), do: state
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: physiology_sentinel.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CREMS.Agents.PhysiologySentinel do
  use GenServer
  require Logger
  alias CREMS.Physics.Alchemist

  @moduledoc """
  The OS Alchemist: Monitors an OS process and transmutes its raw stats into Physiological Feelings.
  """

  defstruct [
    :node_id,
    :target_pid,      # Integer (OS PID)
    :sampling_rate,   # Milliseconds
    :last_metrics,    # Map: Stores previous values for delta calculation
    :forensic_log     # List: Stores last N feelings for autopsy
  ]

  # --- Client API ---

  def start_link(opts) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, opts)
  end

  # --- Callbacks ---

  @impl true
  def init(opts) do
    node_id = get_opt(opts, :node_id)
    target_pid = get_opt(opts, :target_pid)
    
    # Initial sampling rate: 1s
    sampling_rate = 1000
    
    Logger.info("PhysiologySentinel attached to OS PID #{target_pid} for Node #{node_id}")
    
    Process.send_after(self(), :grip_reality, sampling_rate)

    {:ok, %__MODULE__{
      node_id: node_id,
      target_pid: target_pid,
      sampling_rate: sampling_rate,
      last_metrics: nil,
      forensic_log: []
    }}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(:grip_reality, state) do
    # 1. Grip Reality (Read /proc)
    case read_proc_stats(state.target_pid) do
      {:ok, current_metrics} ->
        # 2. Calculate Deltas
        deltas = calculate_deltas(current_metrics, state.last_metrics, state.sampling_rate)
        
        # 3. Transmute (Physics)
        feelings = Alchemist.transmute_os_metrics(deltas)
        
        # 4. Update Node
        update_node(state.node_id, feelings, deltas)
        
        # 5. Forensic Logging: Keep last 10 feelings for autopsy
        new_log = Enum.take([feelings.effect | state.forensic_log], 10)
        
        # 6. Dynamic Loop
        Process.send_after(self(), :grip_reality, state.sampling_rate)
        {:noreply, %{state | last_metrics: current_metrics, forensic_log: new_log}}

      {:error, :process_not_found} ->
         Logger.warning("[PhysiologySentinel] Target PID #{state.target_pid} vanished. Executing Necrosis Protocol.")
         
         # NECROSIS PROTOCOL: Death Signal Detected (Section 8.1)
         # 1. Capture Final State (Historical Forensic Context)
         exit_code = determine_exit_code(state)
         final_buffer = state.forensic_log
         
         # 2. Trigger Autopsy via TrackingAgent (Section 8.2)
         case Registry.lookup(CMS.NodeRegistry, {:tracking_agent, state.node_id}) do
           [{tracker_pid, _}] ->
             GenServer.cast(tracker_pid, {:execute_autopsy, exit_code, final_buffer})
             Logger.info("[PhysiologySentinel] Autopsy triggered for Node #{state.node_id}")
           [] ->
             Logger.warning("[PhysiologySentinel] No TrackingAgent found for Node #{state.node_id}")
         end
         
         # 3. Sentinel Terminates (Resource Release - Section 8.2)
         {:stop, :normal, state}
         
      {:error, _reason} ->
         # Retry
         Process.send_after(self(), :grip_reality, state.sampling_rate)
         {:noreply, state}
    end
  end

  # --- Internals ---

  defp read_proc_stats(pid) do
    # Check if /proc/[pid] exists
    if File.exists?("/proc/#{pid}/stat") do
      try do
        # GRIP: Read raw inputs
        stat = File.read!("/proc/#{pid}/stat")
        status = File.read!("/proc/#{pid}/status")
        
        {state_code, cpu_ticks, rss_pages} = parse_stat(stat)
        
        # Basic Net simulation (since we can't easily isolate per-process net without cgroups/ns)
        # We will poll global net devs and apportion, or mock specific IO based on /proc/[pid]/io if available
        # For Phase 3 implementation fidelity, we check /proc/[pid]/io
        io_stats = read_proc_io(pid)

        # Normalize metrics
        total_mem_kb = parse_status_mem(status)
        
        {:ok, %{
          timestamp: System.monotonic_time(:millisecond),
          state: state_code,
          utime: cpu_ticks, # User time ticks
          rss: rss_pages,
          mem_kb: total_mem_kb,
          read_bytes: io_stats.read_bytes,
          write_bytes: io_stats.write_bytes
        }}
      rescue
        _ -> {:error, :read_failed}
      end
    else
      {:error, :process_not_found}
    end
  end

  defp parse_stat(content) do
    # /proc/[pid]/stat format: pid (name) state ppid ...
    parts = String.split(content)
    # 3rd field is State
    state = Enum.at(parts, 2)
    # 14th is utime, 15th is stime (0-indexed: 13, 14)
    utime = String.to_integer(Enum.at(parts, 13))
    stime = String.to_integer(Enum.at(parts, 14))
    # 24th is RSS pages (0-indexed: 23)
    rss = String.to_integer(Enum.at(parts, 23))
    
    {state, utime + stime, rss}
  end

  defp parse_status_mem(content) do
    regex = ~r/VmRSS:\s+(\d+)\s+kB/
    case Regex.run(regex, content) do
      [_, kb_str] -> String.to_integer(kb_str)
      _ -> 0
    end
  end
  
  defp read_proc_io(pid) do
    path = "/proc/#{pid}/io"
    if File.exists?(path) do
       content = File.read!(path)
       r_bytes = get_io_val(content, "read_bytes")
       w_bytes = get_io_val(content, "write_bytes")
       %{read_bytes: r_bytes, write_bytes: w_bytes}
    else
       %{read_bytes: 0, write_bytes: 0}
    end
  end
  
  defp get_io_val(content, key) do
    case Regex.run(~r/#{key}:\s+(\d+)/, content) do
      [_, val] -> String.to_integer(val)
      _ -> 0
    end
  end

  defp calculate_deltas(current, nil, _rate), do: %{
      cpu_usage: 0.0,
      ram_bytes: current.mem_kb * 1024,
      state: current.state,
      net_in_delta: 0,
      net_out_delta: 0,
      jitter_sec: 0.0
  }

  defp calculate_deltas(current, last, rate_ms) do
    # Time delta in seconds
    dt_sec = (current.timestamp - last.timestamp) / 1000.0
    dt_sec = if dt_sec <= 0, do: 1.0, else: dt_sec
    
    # Jitter Calculation: How far did we drift from the target sampling rate?
    target_interval_sec = rate_ms / 1000.0
    jitter_sec = abs(dt_sec - target_interval_sec)
    
    # CPU Delta: ticks / Hz (usually 100) / time
    # This is a Rough Estimate.
    cpu_delta = (current.utime - last.utime) 
    # Assume 100Hz clock for simplicity
    cpu_usage = (cpu_delta / 100.0) / dt_sec * 100.0
    
    # IO (Net) simulation: read_bytes -> net_in, write_bytes -> net_out for process abstraction
    in_rate = (current.read_bytes - last.read_bytes) / dt_sec
    out_rate = (current.write_bytes - last.write_bytes) / dt_sec

    %{
      cpu_usage: cpu_usage,
      ram_bytes: current.mem_kb * 1024,
      state: current.state,
      net_in_delta: in_rate,
      net_out_delta: out_rate,
      jitter_sec: jitter_sec
    }
  end

  defp update_node(node_id, feelings, raw_deltas) do
    case Registry.lookup(CMS.NodeRegistry, node_id) do
      [{pid, _}] ->
         # 1. Update Physiological Chamber (Body)
         GenServer.cast(pid, {:update_physics, feelings, raw_deltas})

         # 2. Update Transfer Ratio Edge (Tail)
         # Ratio = Output / Input
         # Handle div/0 gracefully by clamping denominator
         input = max(raw_deltas.net_in_delta, 0.0001) 
         ratio = raw_deltas.net_out_delta / input
         
         # The Spec requires updating NodeTail.communication_edges.
         # We instruct the NodeActor to upsert an edge representing this IO Ratio.
         # Target: "os:network" or similar abstraction since we don't have per-connection stats here.
         GenServer.cast(pid, {:update_communication_edge, "os:network", ratio})
         
         # 3. Signal Pain (Antenna Boost)
         # "If Stress > 0.8, trigger Antenna Boost."
         if feelings.stress > 0.8 do
            excess_stress = feelings.stress - 0.8
            boost_multiplier = 1.0 + (excess_stress * 5.0)
            GenServer.cast(pid, {:boost_antenna, boost_multiplier}) 
            
            # Spec 7.2: Trigger Archival on Sentinel Event
            case Registry.lookup(CMS.NodeRegistry, {:tracking_agent, node_id}) do
               [{t_pid, _}] -> GenServer.cast(t_pid, :trigger_archival)
               _ -> :ok
            end
         end
         
      [] -> :ok
    end
  end

  defp determine_exit_code(state) do
    # Inference Protocol: Guessing death reason from last known physiological state
    last_metrics = state.last_metrics
    
    cond do
      is_nil(last_metrics) -> -1
      
      # OOM Heuristic: High RAM and high Stress
      (Map.get(last_metrics, :mem_kb, 0) * 1024) > (500 * 1024 * 1024) -> 
        Logger.info("[PhysiologySentinel] Inferred death: OOM Kill (SIGKILL 137)")
        137
        
      # Signal SIGTERM Heuristic: Clean state but vanished
      last_metrics.state == "S" -> 
        Logger.info("[PhysiologySentinel] Inferred death: Graceful Shutdown (SIGTERM 143)")
        143
        
      # Abort/Crash Heuristic: Process was in high-stress Uninterruptible Sleep (D)
      last_metrics.state == "D" -> 
        Logger.info("[PhysiologySentinel] Inferred death: Unrecoverable IO/Kernel Error (SIGABRT 134)")
        134

      true -> -1
    end
  end

  defp get_opt(opts, key) when is_list(opts), do: Keyword.fetch!(opts, key)
  defp get_opt(opts, key) when is_map(opts), do: Map.fetch!(opts, key)
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: node_actor.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CREMS.Agents.NodeActor do
  use GenServer
  require Logger

  alias CREMS.Node
  alias CMS.Core.Logic.CognitiveMath
  alias CREMS.Physics.PhantomManifold

  @moduledoc """
  The Volatile Runtime Actor for CREMS.
  
  Behavior:
  - Ephemeral (Does not persist to disk, only Mnesia RAM).
  - Uses shared cognitive logic (CMS.Core.Logic.CognitiveMath) for relevance and activation.
  - Updates physiological state (Satisfaction) based on telemetry.
  """

  defstruct [:node, :last_activation_times, :metabolic_energy, :region_id, :last_query_context, :ghost_metadata]

  # --- Client API ---

  def start_link(%Node{} = node) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, node, name: via_tuple(node.id))
  end

  def pulse(uuid) do
    case Registry.lookup(CMS.NodeRegistry, uuid) do
      [{pid, _}] -> GenServer.cast(pid, :fire)
      _ -> {:error, :not_found}
    end
  end

  # --- Callbacks ---

  @impl true
  def init(node) do
    # 1. Subscribe to Semantic Bus
    Phoenix.PubSub.subscribe(CMS.PubSub, "semantic_gather")

    # 2. Register for Drift Index
    region_id = CMS.SemanticRegion.compute_region_hash(node.head.embedding, "all-MiniLM-L6-v2")
    CMS.Core.Logic.DriftIndex.register(node.id, node.head.embedding, region_id)

    # 3. Perform HEBBIAN PRIMING (Wiring)
    # "Must use the exact wiring logic as the CDMS"
    # We call the EdgeGenerator, which now supports CREMS nodes via polymorphism.
    wired_node = CMS.Core.Logic.EdgeGenerator.generate_semantic_edges(node)
    
    # Log the wiring result
    edge_count = length(wired_node.tail.semantic_edges)
    Logger.info("CREMS Node #{node.id} booted & wired. Region: #{region_id}. Edges: #{edge_count}")

    # INSERT THIS LINE:
    Process.send_after(self(), :late_binding_scan, 5000)

    {:ok, %__MODULE__{
      node: wired_node,
      last_activation_times: %{},
      metabolic_energy: 1.0,
      region_id: region_id,
      last_query_context: nil,
      ghost_metadata: nil
    }}
  end

  # --- Calls ---

  @impl true
  def handle_call(:get_state_snapshot, _from, state) do
    {:reply, {:ok, state.node}, state}
  end

  # --- Calls ---

  @impl true
  def handle_call(:get_phantom_snapshot, _from, state) do
     # Return lightweight snapshot for simulation
     snapshot = %{
       id: state.node.id,
       body: state.node.body,
       tail: state.node.tail
     }
     {:reply, snapshot, state}
  end

  # --- Casts ---

  # Ghost mode check: Ghosts don't fire
  @impl true
  def handle_cast(:fire, %{ghost_metadata: %{is_ghost: true}} = state) do
     Logger.debug("[NodeActor] Ghost node #{state.node.id} ignoring activation (dead)")
     {:noreply, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast(:fire, state) do
    # Primary fire starts a NEW trace
    trace = MapSet.new([state.node.id])
    ttl = 3 # Increased from 2 to allow 3-hop propagation
    handle_cast({:fire_with_config, trace, ttl}, state)
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:fire_with_config, trace, ttl}, state) do
    Logger.info("[CREMS] Node #{state.node.id} firing with trace (TTL: #{ttl})")
    
    # 1. Decay TTL
    if ttl > 0 do
       # 2. Get Edges
       tail = state.node.tail
       all_edges = (tail.semantic_edges || []) ++ 
                   (tail.hierarchical_edges || []) ++
                   (tail.communication_edges || []) ++
                   (tail.dependency_edges || []) ++
                   (tail.symbiotic_edges || [])
       
       # 3. Strength Calculation (Shared Math)
       inhibition = CMS.ActivationEngine.get_global_inhibition_factor()
       base_strength = state.node.antenna.gain * inhibition
       
       # 4. Propagate
       Enum.each(all_edges, fn edge ->
         weight = get_edge_weight(edge)
         target_strength = base_strength * weight
         case Registry.lookup(CMS.NodeRegistry, edge.target_node_id) do
            [{pid, _}] -> 
               send(pid, {:activation, state.node.id, target_strength, edge.type, trace, ttl})
            [] -> :ok
         end
       end)
    end
    
    {:noreply, state}
  end



  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:update_physics, feelings, raw_stats}, state) do
     # 1. Update Volatile history for rate-of-change calc
     # (This is separate from the ChronoStack which is for Significant transitions)
     current_stack = state.node.body.data_tail.chrono_stack.head_layer[:history] || []
     new_history = [feelings | Enum.take(current_stack, 9)]
     
     # 2. Simulate (Phantom Manifold Predictions)
     model = state.node.body.fid_physics_model
     
     # Use current strain as the load proxy for saturation prediction (Spec 7.2)
     current_saturation = PhantomManifold.predict_saturation(feelings.strain, model)
     
     # 3. Update Body & Model
     body = state.node.body
     
     # Sentinel Updates: Directly update the mutable head_layer (Spec 7.1)
     new_chrono_stack = CREMS.ChronoStack.update_head(body.data_tail.chrono_stack, %{
        state: feelings.state,
        stress: feelings.stress,
        strain: feelings.strain,
        agility: feelings.agility,
        satisfaction: feelings.satisfaction,
        saturation: current_saturation, 
        telemetry: raw_stats,
        history: new_history
     })
     
     new_data_tail = %{body.data_tail | chrono_stack: new_chrono_stack}

     new_body = %{body | 
        fid_state: feelings.state,
        fid_stress: feelings.stress,
        fid_strain: feelings.strain,
        fid_agility: feelings.agility,
        fid_satisfaction: feelings.satisfaction,
        fid_telemetry: raw_stats,
        fid_effect: feelings.effect,
        data_tail: new_data_tail
     }

     new_node = %{state.node | body: new_body}
     
     # Log significant stress - Antennal Boost is triggered by distinct cast from Sentinel, 
     # but we log Backpressure here.
     if current_saturation > 1.0 do
        Logger.warning("[BACKPRESSURE] Node #{state.node.id} Oversaturated (Sat=#{Float.round(current_saturation, 2)}).")
     end

     persist_node(new_node)
     {:noreply, %{state | node: new_node}}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:boost_antenna, multiplier}, state) do
     current_gain = state.node.antenna.gain
     new_gain = min(5.0, current_gain * multiplier) # Cap max gain
     
     new_antenna = %{state.node.antenna | gain: new_gain}
     new_node = %{state.node | antenna: new_antenna}
     
     Logger.info("[ADAPTATION] Node #{state.node.id} boosting antenna gain to #{new_gain}")
     
     {:noreply, %{state | node: new_node}}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:upsert_edge, type, target_id, metadata}, state) do
     case type do
        :communicates_with ->
          edges = state.node.tail.communication_edges || []
          ratio = Map.get(metadata, :ratio, 1.0)
          proto = Map.get(metadata, :proto, :tcp)
          
          new_edge = %{type: :communicates_with, target_node_id: target_id, protocol: proto, ratio: ratio}
          
          # Robustness logic: handle both Maps (new) and Tuples (legacy)
          new_edges = [new_edge | Enum.reject(edges, fn
            %{target_node_id: t} -> t == target_id
            {:communicates_with, t, _, _} -> t == target_id
            _ -> false
          end)]
            
          new_tail = %{state.node.tail | communication_edges: new_edges}
          new_node = %{state.node | tail: new_tail}
          persist_node(new_node)
          {:noreply, %{state | node: new_node}}

       :child_of ->
         # Hierarchy edges in CREMS are usually in head or tail depending on preference.
         # Spec says: "Creates :child_of edge".
         edges = state.node.tail.hierarchical_edges || []
         new_edges = [%{type: :child_of, target_node_id: target_id, weight: 1.0} | 
                      Enum.reject(edges, &(&1.target_node_id == target_id))]
         
         new_tail = %{state.node.tail | hierarchical_edges: new_edges}
         new_node = %{state.node | tail: new_tail}
         persist_node(new_node)
         {:noreply, %{state | node: new_node}}

       :depends_on ->
         # Spec says: "Creates :depends_on edges".
         edges = state.node.tail.dependency_edges || []
         new_edges = [%{type: :depends_on, target_node_id: target_id, weight: 0.8} | 
                      Enum.reject(edges, &(&1.target_node_id == target_id))]
         
         new_tail = %{state.node.tail | dependency_edges: new_edges}
         new_node = %{state.node | tail: new_tail}
         persist_node(new_node)
         {:noreply, %{state | node: new_node}}

        :semantically_related_to ->
          # Support for semantic edge updates
          edges = state.node.tail.semantic_edges || []
          weight = Map.get(metadata, :score, 0.5)
          new_edge = %{type: :semantically_related_to, target_node_id: target_id, weight: weight}
          
          # Robustness: reject existing by ID, handling both map and tuple formats
          new_edges = [new_edge | Enum.reject(edges, fn
            %{target_node_id: t} -> t == target_id
            {:semantically_related_to, t, _} -> t == target_id
            _ -> false
          end)]
          
          new_tail = %{state.node.tail | semantic_edges: new_edges}
         new_node = %{state.node | tail: new_tail}
         persist_node(new_node)
         {:noreply, %{state | node: new_node}}

         :defined_by ->
           # Bridge edge to CDMS
           edges = state.node.tail.symbiotic_edges || []
           new_edge = %{type: :defined_by, target_node_id: target_id, weight: 1.0}
           
           new_edges = [new_edge | Enum.reject(edges, fn
             %{target_node_id: t} -> t == target_id
             {:defined_by, t} -> t == target_id
             {:defines, t} -> t == target_id
             _ -> false
           end)]
          
          new_tail = %{state.node.tail | symbiotic_edges: new_edges}
          new_node = %{state.node | tail: new_tail}
          persist_node(new_node)
          {:noreply, %{state | node: new_node}}

       _ ->
         Logger.warning("[NodeActor] Unhandled edge type: #{type}")
         {:noreply, state}
     end
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:push_history, snapshot_data}, state) do
     # Evolve: Push current head_layer to stack_layers. Update head_layer with new reality.
     # (Ref: Spec 7.2 Evolve)
     new_stack = CREMS.ChronoStack.evolve(state.node.body.data_tail.chrono_stack, snapshot_data)
     
     new_data_tail = %{state.node.body.data_tail | chrono_stack: new_stack}
     new_body = %{state.node.body | data_tail: new_data_tail}
     new_node = %{state.node | body: new_body}
     
     persist_node(new_node)
     {:noreply, %{state | node: new_node}}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:update_model_constants, constants}, state) do
     elasticity = Map.get(constants, :elasticity_coefficient, 1.0)
     saturation = Map.get(constants, :saturation_point, 1.0)
     
     new_model = %{state.node.body.fid_physics_model | 
        elasticity_coefficient: elasticity, 
        saturation_point: saturation
     }
     new_body = %{state.node.body | fid_physics_model: new_model}
     new_node = %{state.node | body: new_body}
     
     persist_node(new_node)
     {:noreply, %{state | node: new_node}}
  end

  # Keep alias for backward compatibility or direct sentinel calls
  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:update_communication_edge, target_id, ratio}, state) do
     handle_cast({:upsert_edge, :communicates_with, target_id, %{ratio: ratio}}, state)
  end

  # --- Ghost Mode (Section 8.3) ---

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:enter_ghost_mode, exit_code, _final_snapshot, retention_expiry}, state) do
     Logger.warning("[NodeActor] GHOST TRANSITION: Node #{state.node.id} entering Ghost Mode. Exit: #{exit_code}, Expiry: #{retention_expiry}")
     
     # Section 8.3: Ghost Transition
     # The CREMS.Node actor does not die. It enters Ghost Mode.
     
     # 1. Generate Forensic Natural Language Fact
     death_time = DateTime.utc_now()
     exit_reason = interpret_exit_code(exit_code)
     death_classification = if exit_code == 0, do: "terminated gracefully", else: "crashed"
     
     original_fact = state.node.body.natural_language_fact
     forensic_fact = "DECEASED: #{original_fact} | Death: #{death_classification} (exit code #{exit_code} - #{exit_reason}) at #{DateTime.to_iso8601(death_time)}. Available for forensic analysis until #{DateTime.to_iso8601(retention_expiry)}."
     
     # 2. Generate new embedding for forensic discoverability
     new_embedding = 
       case CMS.Tool.Embedder.generate(forensic_fact, "all-MiniLM-L6-v2") do
         {:ok, tensor} -> tensor
         {:error, reason} ->
           Logger.error("[NodeActor] Failed to re-embed ghost node: #{inspect(reason)}")
           state.node.head.embedding  # Fallback to original
       end
     
     # 3. Add forensic tags
     forensic_tags = generate_forensic_tags(exit_code, death_classification)
     new_tags = (state.node.body.semantic_tags || []) ++ forensic_tags |> Enum.uniq()
     
     # 4. Update Node with forensic context
     new_head = %{state.node.head | embedding: new_embedding}
     new_body = %{state.node.body | 
       natural_language_fact: forensic_fact,
       semantic_tags: new_tags
     }
     new_node = %{state.node | head: new_head, body: new_body}
     
     # 5. Persist forensic node state
     persist_node(new_node)
     
     # 5.5 Update DriftIndex for semantic consistency (Discovery)
     CMS.Core.Logic.DriftIndex.register(state.node.id, new_embedding, state.region_id)
     
     # 6. Ghost Metadata
     ghost_metadata = %{
       is_ghost: true,
       death_time: death_time,
       retention_expiry: retention_expiry,
       exit_code: exit_code
     }
     
     # 7. Low Memory Footprint: Clear large volatile buffers
     cleared_state = %{state |
       node: new_node,
       last_activation_times: %{},
       metabolic_energy: 0.0,  # No longer metabolically active
       last_query_context: nil,
       ghost_metadata: ghost_metadata
     }
     
     Logger.info("[NodeActor] Ghost Mode active. Node #{state.node.id} re-embedded with forensic context. Queryable until #{retention_expiry}")
     {:noreply, cleared_state}
  end
  
  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:update_death_state, updates}, state) do
     # Update node body with death-related state
     new_body = %{state.node.body |
       fid_state: Map.get(updates, :fid_state, state.node.body.fid_state),
       fid_satisfaction: Map.get(updates, :fid_satisfaction, state.node.body.fid_satisfaction),
       fid_output: Map.get(updates, :fid_output, state.node.body.fid_output)
     }
     
     new_node = %{state.node | body: new_body}
     persist_node(new_node)
     {:noreply, %{state | node: new_node}}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:apply_hebbian_updates, adjustments}, state) do
    new_tail = Enum.reduce(adjustments, state.node.tail, fn {target_id, delta}, acc_tail ->
      update_edge_weight(acc_tail, target_id, delta)
    end)

    if new_tail != state.node.tail do
      # Logger.debug("Node #{state.node.id} applied #{length(adjustments)} Hebbian updates.")
      new_node = %{state.node | tail: new_tail}
      persist_node(new_node)
      {:noreply, %{state | node: new_node}}
    else
      {:noreply, state}
    end
  end

  # --- Hebbian Helpers ---

  defp update_edge_weight(tail, target_id, delta) do
    # Try semantic_edges first
    case update_weight_in_list(tail.semantic_edges || [], target_id, delta) do
      {:ok, new_list} -> %{tail | semantic_edges: new_list}
      :not_found ->
        # Try dependency_edges
        case update_weight_in_list(tail.dependency_edges || [], target_id, delta) do
           {:ok, new_list} -> %{tail | dependency_edges: new_list}
           :not_found -> tail
        end
    end
  end

  defp update_weight_in_list(list, target_id, delta) do
    case Enum.find_index(list, fn e -> e.target_node_id == target_id end) do
      nil -> :not_found
      idx ->
        edge = Enum.at(list, idx)
        current_weight = Map.get(edge, :weight, 1.0)
        new_weight = CMS.Core.Logic.CognitiveMath.calculate_new_hebbian_weight(current_weight, delta)
        new_edge = Map.put(edge, :weight, new_weight)
        {:ok, List.replace_at(list, idx, new_edge)}
    end
  end
  
  # Forensic Analysis Helpers
  
  defp interpret_exit_code(0), do: "Clean Exit"
  defp interpret_exit_code(1), do: "General Error"
  defp interpret_exit_code(137), do: "OOM Kill (SIGKILL)"
  defp interpret_exit_code(143), do: "Graceful Shutdown (SIGTERM)"
  defp interpret_exit_code(139), do: "Segmentation Fault"
  defp interpret_exit_code(134), do: "Abort Signal (SIGABRT)"
  defp interpret_exit_code(-1), do: "Unknown (Process Already Gone)"
  defp interpret_exit_code(code), do: "Exit Code #{code}"
  
  defp generate_forensic_tags(exit_code, classification) do
    base_tags = ["deceased", "ghost", classification]
    
    specific_tags = case exit_code do
      137 -> ["oom", "killed", "memory_exhaustion"]
      143 -> ["sigterm", "shutdown"]
      139 -> ["segfault", "crashed"]
      134 -> ["abort", "crashed"]
      0 -> ["clean_exit"]
      _ -> []
    end
    
    base_tags ++ specific_tags
  end


  # --- Infos ---

  def handle_info(:late_binding_scan, state) do
    candidates = CMS.Core.Logic.EdgeGenerator.prime_candidates(state.node.head.embedding, state.node.id)
    Enum.each(candidates, fn {target_id, score} ->
       GenServer.cast(self(), {:upsert_edge, :semantically_related_to, target_id, %{score: score}})
    end)
    {:noreply, state}
  end

  # Backward compatibility: Old activation messages without trace/TTL
  @impl true
  def handle_info({:activation, source_id, strength, type}, state) do
    # Default: empty trace, TTL = 1
    handle_info({:activation, source_id, strength, type, MapSet.new(), 1}, state)
  end

  # Backwards compatibility for untagged activations
  @impl true
  def handle_info({:activation, source_id, strength}, state) do
    handle_info({:activation, source_id, strength, :semantic}, state)
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info({:pulse, source_id, strength, type}, state) do
    handle_info({:activation, source_id, strength, type}, state)
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info({:pulse, source_id, strength}, state) do
    handle_info({:activation, source_id, strength, :semantic}, state)
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(:delayed_scatter_gather, state) do
    # Usually meaningless for volatile nodes but good for parity
    {:noreply, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(:metabolic_decay, %{ghost_metadata: %{is_ghost: true}} = state) do
    # Ghosts don't decay or recover energy. They are metabolically inert.
    {:noreply, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(:metabolic_decay, state) do
    # CREMS nodes also maintain high activity/energy
    Process.send_after(self(), :metabolic_decay, 600_000)
    {:noreply, %{state | metabolic_energy: 1.0}}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info({:hebbian_reinforce, target_id, amount}, state) do
     # SHARED LOGIC: Route to buffer
     CMS.RegionalHebbianBuffer.buffer_update(state.node.id, [{target_id, amount}], state.region_id)
     {:noreply, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info({:query, query_context}, state) do
    # 1. Relevance Score ($R$)
    node_text = state.node.body.natural_language_fact
    query_text = Map.get(query_context, :query_text, "")
    
    # SHARED LOGIC: CognitiveMath
    relevance = CognitiveMath.calculate_relevance_score(
      state.node.head.embedding, 
      query_context.query_vector, 
      node_text, 
      query_text
    )

    # 2. Adjusted Threshold ($T_{adj}$) - Use Urgency Override if stressed?
    # Spec says: "EffectiveThreshold = BaseThreshold * (1.0 - CurrentStress)"
    # We map "CurrentStress" to GlobalInhibition for now, or internal stress if available.
    inhibition = CMS.ActivationEngine.get_global_inhibition_factor()
    
    # SHARED LOGIC: CognitiveMath
    # Note: internal_state logic might differ slightly if mapped differently, but Math is same.
    t_adj = CognitiveMath.calculate_adjusted_threshold(state.node.head.urgency_threshold, inhibition, state.node.head.cognitive_state)

    if relevance >= t_adj do
       Logger.info("[CREMS] Node #{state.node.id} responding to query. R=#{relevance}")
       CMS.QueryCoordinator.node_fired(
         query_context.query_id,
         state.node.id,
         relevance,
         state.node
       )
       
       # Trigger Spreading Activation
       GenServer.cast(self(), :fire)
       
       {:noreply, %{state | last_query_context: query_context}}
    else
       {:noreply, %{state | last_query_context: query_context}}
    end
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info({:activation, _source_id, _strength, _type, _trace, _ttl}, %{ghost_metadata: %{is_ghost: true}} = state) do
     # Ghost nodes are inert and do not propagate activation. 
     # They remain queryable but are no longer part of the active spreading activation network.
     {:noreply, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info({:activation, source_id, strength, type, trace, ttl}, state) do
    # Replicate Spreading Activation logic using Shared Math
    
    # 1. Loop Prevention
    if MapSet.member?(trace, state.node.id) do
       {:noreply, state}
    else
       # SHARED LOGIC: CognitiveMath (Associative Boost)
       boost = CognitiveMath.calculate_associative_boost(strength, 1.0, type, :default)
       
       # Local Relevance from context
       local_relevance = if state.last_query_context do
          node_text = state.node.body.natural_language_fact
          query_text = Map.get(state.last_query_context, :query_text, "")
          CognitiveMath.calculate_relevance_score(state.node.head.embedding, state.last_query_context.query_vector, node_text, query_text)
       else
          0.0
       end
       
       total_score = local_relevance + boost
       inhibition = CMS.ActivationEngine.get_global_inhibition_factor()
       t_adj = CognitiveMath.calculate_adjusted_threshold(state.node.head.urgency_threshold, inhibition, state.node.head.cognitive_state)

       if total_score >= t_adj do
          # Fire!
          new_trace = MapSet.put(trace, state.node.id)
          CMS.LogAppender.append_node_event(state.node.id, :node_fired, %{strength: total_score})
          
          # Hebbian Feedback (Shared Physics)
          region_id = state.region_id
          delta = boost * 0.1
          CMS.RegionalHebbianBuffer.buffer_update(source_id, [{state.node.id, delta}], region_id)
          
          # Propagate - Decrement TTL here (once per hop)
          GenServer.cast(self(), {:fire_with_config, new_trace, ttl - 1})
          
          {:noreply, state}
       else
          {:noreply, state}
       end
    end
  end

  # --- Internal ---

  defp get_edge_weight(%{weight: w}), do: w
  defp get_edge_weight(%{ratio: r}), do: r
  defp get_edge_weight(_), do: 1.0

  defp persist_node(new_node) do
    # For CREMS, we persist to Mnesia nodes_table as well for observability.
    # But often we can use a separate volatile_nodes table if disk I/O is a concern.
    # Given the spec requirement for unified cognitive model, we use nodes_table.
    try do
      if :mnesia.system_info(:is_running) == :yes and :nodes_table in :mnesia.system_info(:tables) do
        # CREMS specific mapping for nodes_table
        path_hash = UUID.uuid5(:oid, "crems:#{new_node.id}")
        parent_uuid = 
          case Enum.find(new_node.tail.hierarchical_edges, fn e -> e.type == :child_of end) do
            %{target_node_id: id} -> id
            _ -> nil
          end
        
        :mnesia.dirty_write({:nodes_table, 
          new_node.id, 
          path_hash, 
          parent_uuid, 
          new_node.head, 
          new_node.body, 
          new_node.tail, 
          new_node.body.data_tail.chrono_stack,
          %{}, # Provenance (Empty for volatile)
          new_node.antenna
        })
      end
    rescue
      _ -> :ok
    end
  end

  defp via_tuple(id), do: {:via, Registry, {CMS.NodeRegistry, id}}
end


================================================================================
Folder: lib/crems/persistence
================================================================================

------------------------------------------------------------
File: epoch_log.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CREMS.Persistence.EpochLog do
  @moduledoc """
  Long-term cold storage for CREMS Ghost Node forensic data.
  
  Stores archived ChronoStacks from terminated/crashed processes,
  enabling forensic queries like:
  - "Show me all nodes that died with Exit Code 137 (OOM) in the last hour"
  - "Retrieve the full history of node X after it crashed"
  
  Storage Strategy:
  - File-based in priv/data/crems_epoch_logs/
  - Daily directories for organization
  - ETS index for fast lookups
  - Binary serialization via :erlang.term_to_binary/1
  """

  use GenServer
  require Logger

  @default_base_path "priv/data/crems_epoch_logs"

  defstruct [
    :base_path,
    :index_table  # ETS table for metadata index
  ]

  # --- Client API ---

  def start_link(opts \\ []) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, opts, name: __MODULE__)
  end

  @doc """
  Archives a ghost node's ChronoStack to cold storage.
  
  Metadata should include:
  - node_id: String
  - exit_code: Integer
  - death_time: DateTime.t()
  - tags: List of semantic tags
  - process_role: String
  """
  def archive_ghost(node_id, chrono_stack, metadata) do
    GenServer.call(__MODULE__, {:archive, node_id, chrono_stack, metadata})
  end

  @doc """
  Query archived nodes by exit code within a time range.
  
  Returns list of %{node_id, exit_code, death_time, archive_path, tags}
  """
  def query_by_exit_code(exit_code, time_range \\ :all) do
    GenServer.call(__MODULE__, {:query_exit_code, exit_code, time_range})
  end

  @doc """
  Retrieve a specific node's archived ChronoStack by node_id.
  """
  def query_by_node_id(node_id) do
    GenServer.call(__MODULE__, {:query_node, node_id})
  end

  @doc """
  Query all archived nodes within a time range.
  """
  def query_by_time_range(start_time, end_time) do
    GenServer.call(__MODULE__, {:query_time_range, start_time, end_time})
  end

  # --- Callbacks ---

  @impl true
  def init(opts) do
    base_path = Keyword.get(opts, :base_path, @default_base_path)
    
    # Ensure directory structure exists
    archives_path = Path.join(base_path, "archives")
    File.mkdir_p!(archives_path)
    
    # Create ETS index table (persistent across restarts via disk-based recovery)
    index_table = :ets.new(:crems_epoch_log_index, [:set, :public, :named_table])
    
    # Load existing index from disk if it exists
    load_index_from_disk(base_path, index_table)
    
    Logger.info("[EpochLog] Initialized at #{base_path}")
    
    {:ok, %__MODULE__{
      base_path: base_path,
      index_table: index_table
    }}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_call({:archive, node_id, chrono_stack, metadata}, _from, state) do
    result = perform_archive(node_id, chrono_stack, metadata, state)
    {:reply, result, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_call({:query_exit_code, exit_code, time_range}, _from, state) do
    results = perform_query_by_exit_code(exit_code, time_range, state)
    {:reply, {:ok, results}, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_call({:query_node, node_id}, _from, state) do
    result = perform_query_by_node_id(node_id, state)
    {:reply, result, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_call({:query_time_range, start_time, end_time}, _from, state) do
    results = perform_query_by_time_range(start_time, end_time, state)
    {:reply, {:ok, results}, state}
  end

  # --- Internal Logic ---

  defp perform_archive(node_id, chrono_stack, metadata, state) do
    death_time = Map.get(metadata, :death_time, DateTime.utc_now())
    exit_code = Map.get(metadata, :exit_code, -1)
    tags = Map.get(metadata, :tags, [])
    process_role = Map.get(metadata, :process_role, "unknown")
    
    # Generate archive path
    date_dir = Calendar.strftime(death_time, "%Y-%m-%d")
    archives_dir = Path.join([state.base_path, "archives", date_dir])
    File.mkdir_p!(archives_dir)
    
    # Filename: node_<short_id>_exit_<code>_<timestamp>.bin
    short_id = String.slice(node_id, 0..7)
    timestamp = DateTime.to_unix(death_time)
    filename = "node_#{short_id}_exit_#{exit_code}_#{timestamp}.bin"
    archive_path = Path.join(archives_dir, filename)
    
    # Serialize and write
    try do
      binary_data = :erlang.term_to_binary(%{
        node_id: node_id,
        chrono_stack: chrono_stack,
        metadata: metadata
      }, [:compressed])
      
      File.write!(archive_path, binary_data)
      
      # Update index
      index_entry = %{
        node_id: node_id,
        exit_code: exit_code,
        death_time: death_time,
        archive_path: archive_path,
        tags: tags,
        process_role: process_role
      }
      
      :ets.insert(state.index_table, {node_id, index_entry})
      
      # Persist index to disk
      persist_index_to_disk(state.base_path, state.index_table)
      
      Logger.info("[EpochLog] Archived node #{node_id} (exit: #{exit_code}) to #{archive_path}")
      {:ok, archive_path}
    rescue
      e ->
        Logger.error("[EpochLog] Failed to archive node #{node_id}: #{inspect(e)}")
        {:error, :archive_failed}
    end
  end

  defp perform_query_by_exit_code(exit_code, time_range, state) do
    :ets.tab2list(state.index_table)
    |> Enum.map(fn {_key, entry} -> entry end)
    |> Enum.filter(fn entry -> entry.exit_code == exit_code end)
    |> apply_time_filter(time_range)
  end

  defp perform_query_by_node_id(node_id, state) do
    case :ets.lookup(state.index_table, node_id) do
      [{^node_id, entry}] ->
        # Load full archive from disk
        load_archive_from_disk(entry.archive_path)
      [] ->
        {:error, :not_found}
    end
  end

  defp perform_query_by_time_range(start_time, end_time, state) do
    :ets.tab2list(state.index_table)
    |> Enum.map(fn {_key, entry} -> entry end)
    |> Enum.filter(fn entry ->
      DateTime.compare(entry.death_time, start_time) in [:gt, :eq] and
      DateTime.compare(entry.death_time, end_time) in [:lt, :eq]
    end)
  end

  defp apply_time_filter(entries, :all), do: entries
  defp apply_time_filter(entries, {start_time, end_time}) do
    Enum.filter(entries, fn entry ->
      DateTime.compare(entry.death_time, start_time) in [:gt, :eq] and
      DateTime.compare(entry.death_time, end_time) in [:lt, :eq]
    end)
  end

  defp load_archive_from_disk(path) do
    if File.exists?(path) do
      try do
        binary = File.read!(path)
        archive = :erlang.binary_to_term(binary)
        {:ok, archive}
      rescue
        e ->
          Logger.error("[EpochLog] Failed to load archive from #{path}: #{inspect(e)}")
          {:error, :load_failed}
      end
    else
      {:error, :not_found}
    end
  end

  defp persist_index_to_disk(base_path, table) do
    index_path = Path.join(base_path, "index.bin")
    entries = :ets.tab2list(table)
    binary = :erlang.term_to_binary(entries, [:compressed])
    File.write!(index_path, binary)
  end

  defp load_index_from_disk(base_path, table) do
    index_path = Path.join(base_path, "index.bin")
    
    if File.exists?(index_path) do
      try do
        binary = File.read!(index_path)
        entries = :erlang.binary_to_term(binary)
        Enum.each(entries, fn entry -> :ets.insert(table, entry) end)
        Logger.info("[EpochLog] Loaded #{length(entries)} index entries from disk")
      rescue
        e ->
          Logger.warning("[EpochLog] Failed to load index from disk: #{inspect(e)}")
      end
    end
  end
end

------------------------------------------------------------
File: alchemist.ex

defmodule CREMS.Physics.Alchemist do
  @moduledoc """
  The Alchemist (The Observer): Pure functions converting telemetry into normalized feelings.
  Reference: Spec 4.0 The Physics Engines.
  """

  @doc """
  1. Stress (The Struggle)
  Formula: Sigmoid((cpu_load * 0.5) + (error_rate * 2.0))
  """
  def calculate_stress(cpu_load, error_rate) do
    # Input domains: cpu_load 0.0-1.0, error_rate 0.0-1.0
    x = (cpu_load * 0.5) + (error_rate * 2.0)
    # Sigmoid centered? Standard sigmoid 1 / (1 + e^-x)
    # For inputs 0.0 (idle), result is 0.5 (Neutral).
    # Spec implies "normalized feelings 0.0 - 1.0".
    # Usually 'Stress' 0 is good. Sigmoid(0) = 0.5. 
    # To map 0.0 input to 0.0 stress, we might shift? 
    # But sticking to "Formula: Sigmoid(...)" strictly.
    sigmoid(x)
  end

  @doc """
  2. Strain (The Capacity Limit)
  Formula: (ram_usage / max_ram)^2 + io_wait_factor
  """
  def calculate_strain(ram_usage, max_ram, io_wait_factor) do
    # Cap inputs?
    ratio = ram_usage / max_ram
    (ratio * ratio) + io_wait_factor
    |> min(1.0) # Normalized cap
  end

  @doc """
  3. Agility (The Responsiveness)
  Formula: ops_sec / (cpu_usage + epsilon)
  """
  def calculate_agility(ops_sec, cpu_usage) do
    epsilon = 0.0001
    efficiency = ops_sec / (cpu_usage + epsilon)
    
    # Normalize: "Efficiency" is unbounded. We need 0.0-1.0.
    # We'll use a tanh or similar to squash, or just clamp if ops/cpu is expected roughly 1:1 normalized.
    # Assuming ops and cpu are normalized or this is a relative metric.
    # For now, we clamp at 1.0 to satisfy "normalized".
    min(1.0, efficiency)
  end

  @doc """
  4. Satisfaction (Work Done)
  If total == 0: 1.0 (Idle is satisfied).
  Else: success / total.
  """
  def calculate_satisfaction(success_events, failure_events, state) do
     cond do
       state in [:zombie, :terminated, :error] -> 0.0
       (success_events + failure_events) == 0 -> 1.0
       true -> success_events / (success_events + failure_events)
     end
  end

  @doc """
  5. Feeling Description
  Output: String.
  """
  def describe(stress, strain, agility, satisfaction) do
    stress_desc = cond do
      stress > 0.8 -> "panicked"
      stress > 0.5 -> "stressed"
      stress > 0.2 -> "focused"
      true -> "calm"
    end

    strain_desc = cond do
      strain > 0.9 -> "critical overload"
      strain > 0.7 -> "significant strain"
      strain > 0.4 -> "moderate strain"
      true -> "light load"
    end

    agility_desc = cond do
      agility > 0.8 -> "blazing efficiency"
      agility > 0.5 -> "solid responsiveness"
      agility > 0.2 -> "steady pace"
      true -> "sluggish throughput"
    end

    sat_desc = cond do
      satisfaction > 0.9 -> "highly satisfied"
      satisfaction > 0.6 -> "performing well"
      satisfaction > 0.3 -> "struggling"
      true -> "dissatisfied"
    end
    
    "Feeling #{sat_desc} while #{stress_desc} under #{strain_desc} with #{agility_desc}"
  end

  # --- Transmutation Helpers (Mapping Telemetry to Physics) ---

  def transmute_os_metrics(metrics) do
    # Map OS telemetry to Physics inputs
    
    # 1. Stress
    # cpu_usage is 0-100. Normalize to 0-1.
    cpu_load = metrics.cpu_usage / 100.0
    # Error rate: Hard to know. Use 'State D' (Uninterruptible) as error proxy? 
    # Or keep 0.0 if unknown.
    error_rate = if metrics.state == "D", do: 0.5, else: 0.0
    stress = calculate_stress(cpu_load, error_rate)

    # 2. Strain
    # Soft limit 512MB
    max_ram = 2048 * 1024 * 1024 # Recalibrated to 2GB
    io_wait = if metrics.state == "D", do: 0.5, else: 0.0
    strain = calculate_strain(metrics.ram_bytes, max_ram, io_wait)

    # 3. Agility
    # Ops: Net packets/bytes. Normalize? 
    # CPU: usage 0-1.
    # Let's map normalized IO activity to 'ops'.
    # 100KB/s = 1.0 ops?
    ops = (metrics.net_in_delta + metrics.net_out_delta) / 102400.0
    agility = calculate_agility(ops, cpu_load)

    # 4. Satisfaction
    # Success = Out. Failure = (In - Out).
    # If In < Out, Failure = 0.
    success = metrics.net_out_delta
    failure = max(0, metrics.net_in_delta - metrics.net_out_delta)
    state_sym = if metrics.state in ["Z", "X", "T", "t"], do: :zombie, else: :running
    satisfaction = calculate_satisfaction(success, failure, state_sym)

    effect = describe(stress, strain, agility, satisfaction)

    %{
      stress: stress,
      strain: strain,
      agility: agility,
      satisfaction: satisfaction,
      state: metrics.state,
      effect: effect
    }
  end

  def transmute_beam_metrics(metrics) do
    # Map BEAM telemetry to Physics inputs

    # 1. Stress
    # CPU Load ~ Reductions/sec? No, let's use Queue as Error/Struggle proxy.
    # Spec: "Struggle".
    # We map Queue Length to "Error Rate" for this formula?
    # Or CPU? BEAM doesn't give explicit CPU % easily per actor.
    # Let's assume cpu_load = 0.1 (nominal) and map Queue to Error Rate.
    cpu_load = 0.1
    # Queue > 1000 = 1.0 error rate.
    error_rate = min(1.0, metrics.message_queue_len / 1000.0)
    stress = calculate_stress(cpu_load, error_rate)

    # 2. Strain
    # Heap. Max 10MB approx 1.3M words.
    max_heap = 1_300_000
    strain = calculate_strain(metrics.total_heap_size, max_heap, 0.0)

    # 3. Agility
    # Ops = Messages Out? Or Reductions?
    # Agility Spec: "Efficiency". High ops with low CPU.
    # Let's use Reductions as 'ops' and 'cpu' as time-cost? No, Reductions IS cost.
    # Let's use Msgs Out as Ops, Reductions as Cost (CPU).
    if Map.has_key?(metrics, :net_out_delta) do
       ops = metrics.net_out_delta # Messages out / sec
       cost = metrics.reductions_delta / 2000.0 # Normalized "CPU" cost
       calculate_agility(ops, cost)
    else
       # Fallback if no rigorous stats
       0.5
    end
    |> then(fn ag -> %{agility: ag} end)
    
    # 4. Satisfaction
    success = Map.get(metrics, :net_out_delta, 0)
    failure = max(0, Map.get(metrics, :net_in_delta, 0) - success)
    status_sym = if metrics.status == :suspended, do: :zombie, else: :running
    satisfaction = calculate_satisfaction(success, failure, status_sym)
    
    # Re-merge agility
    agility = 
       if Map.has_key?(metrics, :net_out_delta) do
          ops = metrics.net_out_delta
          # cost 0.0-1.0
          cost = max(0.0001, metrics.reductions_delta / 10000.0) 
          calculate_agility(ops, cost)
       else
          0.5
       end

    effect = describe(stress, strain, agility, satisfaction)

    %{
      stress: stress,
      strain: strain,
      agility: agility,
      satisfaction: satisfaction,
      state: metrics.status,
      effect: effect
    }
  end

  def transmute_beam_death(reason) do
     # Special case for death: strictly mapped
     satisfaction = 
       case reason do
         :normal -> 1.0
         :shutdown -> 1.0
         {:shutdown, _} -> 1.0
         _ -> 0.0
       end
       
     %{
       stress: 0.0, 
       strain: 0.0, 
       agility: 0.0, 
       satisfaction: satisfaction,
       state: :terminated,
       effect: "Terminated with reason: #{inspect(reason)}"
     }
  end

  # --- Internal ---

  defp sigmoid(x), do: 1.0 / (1.0 + :math.exp(-x))
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: phantom_engine.ex

defmodule CREMS.Physics.PhantomEngine do
  require Logger

  @moduledoc """
  The Phantom Engine (The Simulator): Enables predictive "What-If" simulations without affecting live nodes.
  Reference: Spec 4.2 Module: CREMS.Physics.PhantomEngine.
  """

  # --- Data Structures ---

  defmodule SimulationRequest do
    defstruct [:node_id, :force_vector]
  end

  defmodule ForceVector do
    defstruct [:type, :magnitude]
  end

  defmodule PhantomNode do
    defstruct [:id, :body, :tail, :outcome, :satisfaction]
  end

  # --- Client API ---

  @doc """
  Runs a simulation based on a SimulationRequest.
  """
  def simulate(%SimulationRequest{} = request) do
    simulate_scenario(request.node_id, request.force_vector)
  end

  # --- Simulation Logic ---

  @doc """
  Algorithm: simulate_scenario(node_uuid, force_vector)
  """
  def simulate_scenario(node_id, %ForceVector{} = force_vector) do
    # 1. Clone (The Phantom): Synchronously fetch snapshot
    case fetch_snapshot(node_id) do
      {:error, _} -> 
        %{node_id: node_id, outcome: :error, reason: :node_not_found, children: []}
        
      snapshot ->
        # 2. Create in-memory PhantomNode
        phantom = %PhantomNode{
          id: snapshot.id,
          body: snapshot.body,
          tail: snapshot.tail,
          outcome: :ok,
          satisfaction: snapshot.body.fid_satisfaction
        }

        # 3. Extract fid:physics_model and fid:telemetry
        physics = phantom.body.fid_physics_model || %{elasticity_coefficient: 1.0, saturation_point: 1.0}
        
        # 4. Apply Physics (Elasticity Logic)
        input_force = force_vector.magnitude
        # Spec: Elasticity = Phantom.body.fid:physics_model.elasticity_coefficient
        elasticity = Map.get(physics, :elasticity_coefficient, 1.0)
        
        # FIX: Pull from top-level body field (CREMS.NodeBody.fid_stress)
        current_stress = phantom.body.fid_stress || 0.0

        # Formula: Predicted_Stress = Current_Stress * (Input_Force ^ Elasticity)
        predicted_stress = current_stress * :math.pow(input_force, elasticity)

        # 5. Check Saturation (Crash Prediction)
        # FIX: Pull from :cpu_usage inside telemetry (what Sentinels actually send)
        telemetry = phantom.body.fid_telemetry || %{}
        current_load = Map.get(telemetry, :cpu_usage, 0.0)
        
        predicted_load = current_load * input_force
        saturation_point = Map.get(physics, :saturation_point, 1.0)

        # Check for crash
        {phantom, outcome} = 
          if predicted_load > saturation_point do
             # Set Phantom.outcome = :crashed. Set Phantom.satisfaction = 0.0.
             {%{phantom | outcome: :crashed, satisfaction: 0.0}, :crashed}
          else
             {phantom, :ok}
          end

        # 6. Propagate (The Dark Matter Push)
        children = 
          if outcome != :crashed do
             # Iterate Phantom.tail.communication_edges
             propagate(phantom.tail.communication_edges, input_force)
          else
             []
          end

        # Aggregate Result
        %{
          node_id: node_id,
          outcome: outcome,
          predicted_stats: %{
            stress: predicted_stress,
            load: predicted_load,
            satisfaction: phantom.satisfaction
          },
          children: children
        }
    end
  end

  # --- Helpers ---

  defp fetch_snapshot(node_id) do
    case Registry.lookup(CMS.NodeRegistry, node_id) do
      [{pid, _}] -> 
         try do
           GenServer.call(pid, :get_phantom_snapshot, 5000)
         catch
           :exit, _ -> {:error, :timeout}
         end
      [] -> {:error, :not_found}
    end
  end

  defp propagate(nil, _), do: []
  defp propagate(edges, input_force) when is_list(edges) do
     Enum.map(edges, fn 
        {:communicates_with, target_id, _proto, transfer_ratio} ->
             # Downstream_Force_Mag = Input_Force * transfer_ratio
             downstream_mag = input_force * transfer_ratio
             
             # Recursively call simulate_scenario
             simulate_scenario(target_id, %ForceVector{type: :propagation, magnitude: downstream_mag})
        _ -> nil
     end)
     |> Enum.reject(&is_nil/1)
  end
  defp propagate(_, _), do: []
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: phantom_manifold.ex
