defmodule CREMS.Physics.PhantomManifold do
  @moduledoc """
  The Phantom Manifold (The Simulator): Pure functions converting history into Predictions.
  Reference: Spec 4.0 The Physics Engines (4.2).
  """

  @doc """
  Calculates Elasticity Coefficient (Brittleness) for the Phantom Engine.
  Formula: 1.0 / Resilience
  Where Resilience = 1.0 - (Strain_Rate_of_Change * Stress_Level)
  
  Interpretation:
  - 1.0: Perfectly Elastic (Linear response).
  - > 1.0: Brittle (Exponential response).
  """
  def calculate_elasticity_coefficient(history_buffer) do
    # History buffer is a list of physiological snapshots (maps)
    # We need at least 2 points to calculate rate of change.
    if length(history_buffer) < 2 do
      1.0 # Default Linear
    else
      [latest | rest] = history_buffer
      [previous | _] = rest
      
      # Strain Delta per tick
      strain_delta = latest.strain - previous.strain
      
      # Rate of Change: Positive slope implies stiffening/brittleness.
      rate_of_change = max(0.0, strain_delta)
      
      stress_level = latest.stress
      
      # Resilience (0.0 to 1.0)
      resilience = 1.0 - (rate_of_change * stress_level)
      clamped_resilience = max(0.1, resilience) # Avoid div/0, max brittleness 10.0
      
      1.0 / clamped_resilience
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Predicts Saturation (The Limit).
  Formula: Current_Load / (Predicted_Max_Capacity * 0.9)
  
  Concepts:
  - Current Load: Approximated by Strain (normalized capacity usage).
  - Predicted Max Capacity: Ideally 1.0 (since Strain is normalized).
  """
  def predict_saturation(current_strain, _history_buffer) do
    # Predicted Max Capacity. 
    # Since Strain is normalized 0.0-1.0, Max is 1.0. 
    # We ensure a sane floor to avoid zero-saturation math errors.
    predicted_max = 1.0
    
    # Saturation = Load / (Max * 0.9)
    current_strain / (predicted_max * 0.9)
  end
end

------------------------------------------------------------
File: definition_linker.ex

defmodule CREMS.Symbiosis.DefinitionLinker do
  use GenServer
  require Logger

  alias CMS.SymbolicIndex

  @moduledoc """
  The Bridge between Anatomy (CDMS) and Physiology (CREMS).
  Resolves "What file defines this process?"
  """

  # --- Client API ---

  def start_link(opts \\ []) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, opts, name: __MODULE__)
  end

  @doc """
  Triggers a linkage check for a specific CREMS Node.
  Usually called during ModellingAgent genesis.
  """
  def link_node(node_id) do
    GenServer.cast(__MODULE__, {:link_node, node_id})
  end

  # --- Callbacks ---

  @impl true
  def init(_opts) do
    # Periodic sweep every 30 seconds
    Process.send_after(self(), :periodic_sweep, 30_000)
    {:ok, %{}}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:link_node, node_id}, state) do
    perform_linkage(node_id)
    {:noreply, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(:periodic_sweep, state) do
    # Scan all CREMS nodes in Registry
    # This is slightly expensive, but exhaustive O(N_runtime).
    Registry.select(CMS.NodeRegistry, [{{:"$1", :"$2", :"$3"}, [], [{{:"$1", :"$2", :"$3"}}]}])
    |> Enum.each(fn {crems_id, _pid, _} -> 
       # We only link if it's a pid-node- string
       if is_binary(crems_id) and String.starts_with?(crems_id, "pid-node-") do
          perform_linkage(crems_id)
       end
    end)

    Process.send_after(self(), :periodic_sweep, 30_000)
    {:noreply, state}
  end

  # --- Internal ---

  defp perform_linkage(crems_id) do
    case Registry.lookup(CMS.NodeRegistry, crems_id) do
      [{pid, _}] ->
        # Retrieve CREMS Snapshot
        case GenServer.call(pid, :get_state_snapshot) do
          {:ok, crems_node} ->
            path = crems_node.body.executable_path
            resolve_linkage(crems_id, path)
          _ -> :ok
        end
      _ -> :ok
    end
  end

  defp resolve_linkage(_crems_id, nil), do: :ok
  defp resolve_linkage(crems_id, path) do
    case SymbolicIndex.get_node_by_path(path) do
      {:ok, cdms_pid} ->
        # Retrieve CDMS ID
        case GenServer.call(cdms_pid, :get_state_snapshot) do
          {:ok, cdms_node} ->
            establish_bridge(crems_id, cdms_node.id, cdms_pid)
          _ -> :ok
        end
      {:error, :not_found} ->
        :ok
    end
  end

  defp establish_bridge(crems_id, cdms_id, cdms_pid) do
    # 1. Update CREMS Node: {:defined_by, cdms_id}
    # Using existing upsert_edge in CREMS.NodeActor
    case Registry.lookup(CMS.NodeRegistry, crems_id) do
      [{pid, _}] -> 
        GenServer.cast(pid, {:upsert_edge, :defined_by, cdms_id, %{}})
      _ -> :ok
    end

    # 2. Update CDMS Node: {:defines, crems_id}
    # CDMS NodeActor uses handle_cast({:add_edge, edge}, state)
    edge = CMS.Edge.new(crems_id, :defines, 1.0)
    GenServer.cast(cdms_pid, {:add_edge, edge})
    
    Logger.info("[SYMBIOIS] Bridge established: #{crems_id} <---> #{cdms_id} (#{crems_id} is defined by #{cdms_id})")
  end
end

------------------------------------------------------------
File: identity_generator.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CREMS.Core.Logic.IdentityGenerator do
  @moduledoc """
  Generates immutable identity facts for CREMS Nodes.
  Strictly template-based (No LLM).
  """

  @doc """
  Generates a natural language fact string from node metadata.
  
  ## Examples
  
      iex> metadata = %{role: "postgres_worker", pid: 4096, user: "postgres", port: 5432}
      iex> CREMS.Core.Logic.IdentityGenerator.generate_fact(metadata)
      "The Postgres Worker process [PID 4096] owned by user 'postgres' listening on port 5432."
  """
  def generate_fact(metadata) do
    role = Map.get(metadata, :role, "Unknown Agent")
    pid = Map.get(metadata, :pid, "0")
    user = Map.get(metadata, :user, "system")
    
    base_fact = "The #{snake_case_to_title(to_string(role))} process [PID #{pid}] owned by user '#{user}'"
    
    case Map.get(metadata, :port) do
      nil -> "#{base_fact}."
      port -> "#{base_fact} listening on port #{port}."
    end
  end

  defp snake_case_to_title(str) do
    str
    |> String.split("_")
    |> Enum.map(&String.capitalize/1)
    |> Enum.join(" ")
  end
end

defmodule CREMS.NodeAntenna do
  @moduledoc """
  The Synaptic Transceiver of a CREMS Node.
  Controls signal gain based on physiological stress.
  """

  defstruct [
    :gain,          # Float: Signal amplification factor
    :spatial_coord, # Tuple: {x, y, z}
    :error_conduit  # PID: Channel to the Passive Consciousness
  ]

  @type t :: %__MODULE__{
    gain: float(),
    spatial_coord: {float(), float(), float()},
    error_conduit: pid()
  }

  @doc """
  Calculates Gain based on Node Stress.
  Logic: Gain = Base_Gain + (NodeStress * 2.0).
  High Stress = High Gain ("The Shout").
  """
  def calculate_gain(base_gain, node_stress) do
    base_gain + (node_stress * 2.0)
  end
end

defmodule CREMS.NodeTail do
  @moduledoc """
  The Functional Connectome of a CREMS Node.
  Defines wiring with hierarchical, communication, dependency, and semantic edges.
  """

  defstruct [
    :hierarchical_edges,  # List of Maps: %{type: :parent_of | :child_of, target_node_id: uuid, weight: 1.0}
    :communication_edges, # List of Maps: %{type: :communicates_with, target_node_id: uuid, protocol: proto, ratio: score}
    :dependency_edges,    # List of Maps: %{type: :depends_on, target_node_id: uuid, weight: score}
    :semantic_edges,      # List of Maps: %{type: :semantically_related_to, target_node_id: uuid, weight: score}
    :symbiotic_edges      # List of Maps: %{type: :defined_by | :defines, target_node_id: uuid, weight: 1.0}
  ]

  @type t :: %__MODULE__{
    hierarchical_edges: list(),
    communication_edges: list(),
    dependency_edges: list(),
    semantic_edges: list(),
    symbiotic_edges: list()
  }
end

defmodule CREMS.NodeBody do
  @moduledoc """
  The Vessel of State for a CREMS Node.
  Strictly partitioned into three sub-chambers: Identity, Physiology, and Reality.
  """

  defstruct [
    # --- Chamber 1: Identity ---
    :natural_language_fact, # String: Template-based description
    :semantic_tags,         # List[String]: derived from binary path and arguments

    # --- Chamber 2: Physiology (The "Feelings") ---
    :fid_state,          # Atom: :active | :sleeping | :uninterruptible | :zombie | :terminated
    :fid_telemetry,      # Map: %{cpu_load, ram_bytes, io_wait, net_in, net_out}
    
    # Alchemy Metrics
    :fid_satisfaction,   # Float (0.0 - 1.0): "Work Done" metric
    :fid_stress,         # Float (0.0 - 1.0): CPU/Queue backlog pressure
    :fid_strain,         # Float (0.0 - 1.0): RAM/Heap memory pressure
    :fid_agility,        # Float (0.0 - 1.0): Speed of thought/IO variation

    :fid_effect,         # String: Natural language description of physical sensation
    :fid_physics_model,  # Map: %{elasticity_coefficient: float(), saturation_point: float()} [Phantom Manifold]
    :fid_output,         # List: Circular buffer of last N lines of STDOUT/STDERR

    # --- Chamber 3: Reality Link ---
    :symbolic_link,      # Tuple: {:os_pid, 1234} or {:beam_pid, pid}
    :executable_path,    # String: Absolute path to the binary
    :data_tail           # %CREMS.DataTail{}: Ownership and Memory
  ]

  @type t :: %__MODULE__{
    natural_language_fact: String.t(),
    semantic_tags: [String.t()],
    fid_state: :active | :sleeping | :uninterruptible | :zombie | :terminated,
    fid_telemetry: map(),
    fid_satisfaction: float(),
    fid_stress: float(),
    fid_strain: float(),
    fid_agility: float(),
    fid_effect: String.t(),
    fid_physics_model: map(),
    fid_output: list(),
    symbolic_link: {atom(), any()},
    executable_path: String.t() | nil,
    data_tail: CREMS.DataTail.t()
  }
end

File: node.ex

defmodule CREMS.Node do
  @moduledoc """
  The Fundamental Unit of the CREMS system.
  Ephemeral node, tied to a specific execution lifecycle.
  """

  defstruct [
    :id,      # String: Content-Addressable Hash (SHA-256)
    :head,    # CREMS.NodeHead
    :body,    # CREMS.NodeBody
    :tail,    # CREMS.NodeTail
    :antenna  # CREMS.NodeAntenna
  ]

  @type t :: %__MODULE__{
    id: String.t(),
    head: CREMS.NodeHead.t(),
    body: CREMS.NodeBody.t(),
    tail: CREMS.NodeTail.t(),
    antenna: CREMS.NodeAntenna.t()
  }

  @doc """
  Derives the Node ID.
  Formula: SHA256(Host_ID + Process_Role + Launch_Timestamp).
  """
  def derive_id(host_id, process_role, launch_timestamp) do
    plain_text = "#{host_id}#{process_role}#{launch_timestamp}"
    :crypto.hash(:sha256, plain_text) |> Base.encode16(case: :lower)
  end
end

defmodule CREMS.DataTail do
  @moduledoc """
  The Anatomical Ledger and Memory of a CREMS Node.
  Hosts ownership, capabilities, and the ChronoStack.
  """

  defstruct [
    :ownership,     # Map: %{uid: integer(), user: string(), gid: integer(), group: string()}
    :capabilities,  # List[string]: Linux capabilities or similar
    :chrono_stack   # %CREMS.ChronoStack{}
  ]

  @type t :: %__MODULE__{
    ownership: map(),
    capabilities: [String.t()],
    chrono_stack: CREMS.ChronoStack.t()
  }

  @doc """
  Creates a new DataTail.
  """
  def new(uid, user, gid, group) do
    %__MODULE__{
      ownership: %{uid: uid, user: user, gid: gid, group: group},
      capabilities: [],
      chrono_stack: CREMS.ChronoStack.new()
    }
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: chrono_stack.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CREMS.ChronoStack do
  @moduledoc """
  A persistent, immutable data structure for CREMS node history.
  Rejects the noise of constant metric fluctuation in favor of Significant State Transitions.
  """

  defstruct [
    :head_layer,   # Map: The current, mutable state map.
    :stack_layers  # List: [%Snapshot{}].
  ]

  @type t :: %__MODULE__{
    head_layer: map(),
    stack_layers: list()
  }

  defmodule Snapshot do
    @moduledoc """
    A deep copy of the Node state at the moment of transition.
    """
    defstruct [:timestamp, :hash, :data]
    
    @type t :: %__MODULE__{
      timestamp: DateTime.t(),
      hash: String.t(),
      data: map()
    }
  end

  @doc """
  Creates a new empty ChronoStack.
  """
  def new(initial_state \\ %{}) do
    # Initial state should ideally be a snapshot of the genesis state
    %__MODULE__{
      head_layer: initial_state,
      stack_layers: []
    }
  end

  @doc """
  Evolves the stack: Pushes the CURRENT head_layer to stack_layers,
  and updates head_layer with the new reality snapshot.
  (Ref: Spec 7.2 Evolve)
  """
  def evolve(stack, new_reality) do
    timestamp = DateTime.utc_now()
    # Hash the old head_layer before pushing it to the stack
    hash = :crypto.hash(:sha256, :erlang.term_to_binary(stack.head_layer)) |> Base.encode16()
    
    snapshot = %Snapshot{
      timestamp: timestamp,
      hash: hash,
      data: stack.head_layer # Push the "old" reality
    }
    
    %{stack | 
      stack_layers: [snapshot | Enum.take(stack.stack_layers, 49)],
      head_layer: new_reality # Update with the "new" reality
    }
  end

  @doc """
  Directly updates the mutable head_layer (called by Sentinel).
  (Ref: Spec 7.1 head_layer)
  """
  def update_head(stack, updates) when is_map(updates) do
    %{stack | head_layer: Map.merge(stack.head_layer, updates)}
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: node_head.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CREMS.NodeHead do
  @moduledoc """
  The Cognitive Nucleus of a CREMS Node.
  Manages semantic self-representation and the autonomous firing decision.
  """

  defstruct [
    :embedding,          # Nx.Tensor[384] - Compatible with all-MiniLM-L6-v2
    :urgency_threshold,  # Float (0.0 - 1.0)
    :cognitive_state     # Atom: :genesis | :steady | :distressed | :dying
  ]

  @type t :: %__MODULE__{
    embedding: Nx.Tensor.t(),
    urgency_threshold: float(),
    cognitive_state: :genesis | :steady | :distressed | :dying
  }

  @doc """
  Autonomous Firing Decision.
  Decides whether to respond to a system-wide broadcast query.
  
  Logic:
  1. Semantic Match: Cosine Similarity between QueryVector and SelfEmbedding.
  2. Urgency Override: EffectiveThreshold = BaseThreshold * (1.0 - CurrentStress).
  3. Returns TRUE if Sim > EffectiveThreshold.
  """
  def should_fire?(%__MODULE__{} = head, query_vector, current_stress) do
    similarity = calculate_cosine_similarity(head.embedding, query_vector)
    effective_threshold = head.urgency_threshold * (1.0 - current_stress)

    similarity > effective_threshold
  end

  # Helper for Cosine Similarity (Placeholder for CDMS standard math if strictly separate)
  # Ideally checks CMS.Core.Math or similar if available, otherwise implementation here:
  defp calculate_cosine_similarity(vec_a, vec_b) do
    # Assuming Nx is available as per spec mentioning Nx.Tensor
    # Use standard Cosine Similarity: (A . B) / (||A|| * ||B||)
    
    # We will assume vectors are already normalized for optimization as is common in CDMS
    # If not, full calculation:
    Nx.dot(vec_a, vec_b) 
    |> Nx.divide(Nx.multiply(Nx.LinAlg.norm(vec_a), Nx.LinAlg.norm(vec_b)))
    |> Nx.to_number()
  end
end

------------------------------------------------------------
File: identity_factory.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CREMS.Ingestion.IdentityFactory do
  @moduledoc """
  Generates deterministic natural_language_facts and metadata for modelled processes.
  """

  @doc """
  Generates a fact string based on process metadata.
  """
  def generate_fact(%{binary_name: bin, user: user, args: args, ports: ports}) do
    args_str = if args == "", do: "with no arguments", else: "with arguments [#{args}]"
    port_str = if Enum.empty?(ports), do: "no open ports", else: "listening on ports #{inspect(ports)}"
    "Runtime process #{bin} owned by #{user}, running #{args_str}, #{port_str}."
  end

  def generate_fact(%{binary_name: bin, user: user}) do
    "Runtime process #{bin} owned by #{user}."
  end

  def generate_fact(%{is_erlang: true, registered_name: name, initial_call: call}) do
    name_str = if name, do: "registered as :#{name}", else: "unregistered"
    "Erlang process #{name_str}, initial call: #{inspect(call)}."
  end

  @doc """
  Generates semantic tags.
  """
  def generate_tags(%{is_erlang: true, registered_name: name}) do
    tags = ["erlang", "runtime_node", "process"]
    if is_atom(name) and name != nil, do: [Atom.to_string(name) | tags], else: tags
  end

  def generate_tags(%{binary_name: bin, user: user}) do
    [bin, user, "runtime_node", "process"]
    |> Enum.map(&String.downcase/1)
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: edge_forger.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CREMS.Ingestion.EdgeForger do
  require Logger

  @moduledoc """
  Specialized logic for OS-level introspection and relationship mapping.
  """

  @doc """
  Scans for network communication edges.
  Logic: netstat -taupn or lsof -i.
  """
  def forge_communication_edges(pid) do
    # Rigorous Implementation: Use lsof to find all active connections
    # We resolve local destinations to their respective PIDs to link nodes directly.
    try do
      {out, 0} = System.cmd("lsof", ["-nP", "-i", "-a", "-p", to_string(pid)])
      
      out
      |> String.split("\n", trim: true)
      |> Enum.drop(1) # Header
      |> Enum.map(fn line ->
        # Parse lsof -i line: COMMAND PID USER FD TYPE DEVICE SIZE/OFF NODE NAME
        # Name column: IP:PORT->IP:PORT (ESTABLISHED) or *:PORT (LISTEN)
        parts = String.split(line, ~r/\s+/, trim: true)
        name = List.last(parts)
        
        cond do
          # Established connection: IP:PORT->IP:PORT
          String.contains?(name, "->") ->
            [_local, remote] = String.split(name, "->")
            [remote_ip, remote_port] = parse_addr(remote)
            
            # If remote is local, try to find the PID
            target_id = 
              if remote_ip in ["127.0.0.1", "::1", "localhost"] do
                case find_pid_by_port(remote_port) do
                  nil -> "remote-#{remote_ip}:#{remote_port}"
                  target_pid -> "pid-node-#{target_pid}"
                end
              else
                "remote-#{remote_ip}:#{remote_port}"
              end
            
            {:communicates_with, target_id, :tcp, 1.0}

          # Just scanning connections here. Listening ports are handled in scan_process.
          true -> nil
        end
      end)
      |> Enum.reject(&is_nil/1)
      |> Enum.uniq()
    rescue
      _ -> []
    end
  end

  defp parse_addr(addr) do
    # Handles IPv4: port and [IPv6]:port
    case Regex.run(~r/^(.*):(\d+)$/, addr) do
      [_, host, port] -> [String.replace(host, ~r/[\[\]]/, ""), String.to_integer(port)]
      _ -> [addr, 0]
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Resolves which PID is listening on a specific port.
  """
  def find_pid_by_port(port) do
    try do
      {out, 0} = System.cmd("lsof", ["-nP", "-i", "TCP:#{port}", "-sTCP:LISTEN", "-t"])
      case String.trim(out) |> String.split("\n", trim: true) do
        [pid_str | _] -> String.to_integer(pid_str)
        _ -> nil
      end
    rescue
      _ -> nil
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Scans for ports being listened on by the process.
  """
  def extract_ports(pid) do
    try do
      {out, 0} = System.cmd("lsof", ["-nP", "-p", to_string(pid), "-i", "-sTCP:LISTEN"])
      
      Regex.scan(~r/:(\d+)\s+\(LISTEN\)/, out)
      |> Enum.map(fn [_, port] -> String.to_integer(port) end)
      |> Enum.uniq()
    rescue
      _ -> []
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Scans for dependencies (e.g., shared libraries).
  Logic: /proc/[pid]/maps or lsof.
  """
  def forge_dependency_edges(pid) do
    # Scans for shared libraries (.so files) mapped to the process memory
    try do
      {out, 0} = System.cmd("lsof", ["-p", to_string(pid)])
      
      out
      |> String.split("\n", trim: true)
      |> Enum.filter(&String.ends_with?(&1, ".so"))
      |> Enum.map(fn line ->
        # Extract the path (usually the last column)
        parts = String.split(line, ~r/\s+/, trim: true)
        path = List.last(parts)
        lib_name = Path.basename(path)
        
        # Spec: {:depends_on, target_id}
        {:depends_on, "lib-#{lib_name}"}
      end)
      |> Enum.uniq()
    rescue
      _ -> []
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Identifies parent-child relationships.
  """
  def get_parent_id(pid) do
    case read_proc_field(pid, "status", "PPid") do
      nil -> nil
      ppid_str -> String.trim(ppid_str) |> String.to_integer()
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Resolves a numeric UID to a user name.
  """
  def resolve_user(uid) do
    try do
      {out, 0} = System.cmd("id", ["-nu", to_string(uid)])
      String.trim(out)
    rescue
      _ -> to_string(uid)
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Retrieves the absolute path to the executable.
  """
  def get_executable_path(pid) do
    case File.read_link("/proc/#{pid}/exe") do
      {:ok, path} -> path
      _ -> nil
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Discovers child processes.
  """
  def get_child_pids(pid) do
    # In Linux, we can find children via 'pgrep -P [pid]' or scanning all /proc/[pid]/status
    # For now, we simulate or use a shell command.
    try do
      {out, 0} = System.cmd("pgrep", ["-P", to_string(pid)])
      out
      |> String.split("\n", trim: true)
      |> Enum.map(&String.to_integer/1)
    rescue
      _ -> []
    end
  end

  # --- Internal ---

  defp read_proc_field(pid, file, field) do
    path = "/proc/#{pid}/#{file}"
    if File.exists?(path) do
      File.read!(path)
      |> String.split("\n")
      |> Enum.find_value(fn line ->
        if String.starts_with?(line, field) do
          [_, val] = String.split(line, ":", parts: 2)
          val
        else
          nil
        end
      end)
    else
      nil
    end
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: modelling_agent.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CREMS.Ingestion.ModellingAgent do
  use Task, restart: :temporary
  require Logger

  alias CREMS.Ingestion.IdentityFactory
  alias CREMS.Ingestion.EdgeForger
  alias CMS.Core.Logic.EdgeGenerator
  alias CMS.Tool.Embedder
  alias CREMS.{Node, NodeHead, NodeBody, NodeAntenna, NodeTail}
  alias CREMS.Agents.{NodeActor, PhysiologySentinel, TrackingAgent, BeamSentinel}

  @moduledoc """
  An ephemeral worker for modelling one node.
  Implements the 7-step sequence: Scan, Identify, Spawn, Wire, Enervate, Reproduce, Terminate.
  """

  # --- Client API ---

  def spawn_agent(pid, context) do
     Task.Supervisor.start_child(CREMS.Ingestion.SwarmSupervisor, fn ->
       run(pid, context)
     end)
  end

  # --- Lifecycle Flow ---

  def run(pid, context) do
    if should_skip_os_process?(pid) do
       # Skip non-essential OS processes (lsof, pgrep, etc) early
       :ok
    else
      # 0. IDEMPOTENCY CHECK
      # We derive the ID first to check if we've already modelled this node.
      {node_id, _link} = derive_node_id(pid)

      if Registry.lookup(CMS.NodeRegistry, node_id) != [] do
         # Node exists. We skip expensive scanning/embedding.
         # CRITICAL FIX: We do NOT trigger reproduction for existing nodes here anymore.
         # This prevents the "Recursive Discovery Storm" where every check triggers a full walk.
         Logger.debug("[SWARM] Node #{node_id} already exists. Skipping.")
         :ok
      else
        Logger.debug("[SWARM] ModellingAgent starting for PID #{pid}")
    
        # 1. SCAN
        case scan_process(pid) do
          {:ok, raw_data} ->
             
             # 2. IDENTIFY
             identity = identify_node(pid, raw_data)
             
             # 3. SPAWN
             case spawn_actor(pid, identity, context) do
               {:ok, node} ->
                  # 4. WIRE
                  wire_node(pid, node, context)
                  
                  # 5. ENERVATE
                  enervate_node(pid, node)
                  
                  # 6. REPRODUCE
                  reproduce(pid, node.id)
                  
                  # 7. TERMINATE
                  Logger.info("[SWARM] ModellingAgent completed for PID #{pid} (Node: #{node.id})")
                  
               {:error, :already_exists} -> 
                  # Race condition catch
                  Logger.debug("[SWARM] Node for PID #{pid} already exists (race). Skipping.")
             end
    
          {:error, reason} ->
             Logger.debug("[SWARM] Could not scan PID #{pid}: #{reason}")
        end
      end
    end
  end

  # --- Step 1: SCAN ---
  defp scan_process(pid) do
     if File.exists?("/proc/#{pid}/status") do
        status = File.read!("/proc/#{pid}/status")
        # Extract binary name, user, etc.
        bin = extract_field(status, "Name")
        uid_str = extract_field(status, "Uid") |> String.split("\t") |> List.first()
        gid_str = extract_field(status, "Gid") |> String.split("\t") |> List.first()
        uid = String.to_integer(uid_str)
        gid = String.to_integer(gid_str)
        user_name = EdgeForger.resolve_user(uid)
        
        args = case File.read("/proc/#{pid}/cmdline") do
          {:ok, content} -> String.replace(content, <<0>>, " ") |> String.trim()
          _ -> ""
        end
        
        # Extracts Ports (Spec requirement)
        ports = EdgeForger.extract_ports(pid)
        exe_path = EdgeForger.get_executable_path(pid)
        
        {:ok, %{
          binary_name: bin,
          user: user_name,
          uid: uid,
          gid: gid,
          args: args,
          ports: ports,
          exe_path: exe_path
        }}
     else
        {:error, :not_found}
     end
  end

  # --- Step 2: IDENTIFY ---
  defp identify_node(_pid, raw_data) do
     fact = IdentityFactory.generate_fact(raw_data)
     tags = IdentityFactory.generate_tags(raw_data)
     
     # CDMS Embedder call using CMS.Tool.Embedder.generate/2
     embedding = 
       case Embedder.generate(fact, "all-MiniLM-L6-v2") do
         {:ok, tensor} -> tensor
         {:error, reason} -> 
           Logger.error("[SWARM] Embedding failed for fact: #{fact}. Reason: #{inspect(reason)}")
           # Fallback: Zero vector
           Nx.broadcast(0.0, {384})
       end

     %{
       fact: fact,
       tags: tags,
       embedding: embedding,
       raw_data: raw_data
     }
  end

  # --- Step 3: SPAWN ---
  defp spawn_actor(pid, identity, _context) do
    # Use shared helper
    {node_id, symbol_link} = derive_node_id(pid)
     
     case Registry.lookup(CMS.NodeRegistry, node_id) do
       [{_pid, _}] -> {:error, :already_exists}
       [] -> 
          node = %Node{
            id: node_id,
            head: %NodeHead{
              embedding: identity.embedding,
              urgency_threshold: 0.5,
              cognitive_state: :steady
            },
            body: %NodeBody{
              natural_language_fact: identity.fact,
              semantic_tags: identity.tags,
              fid_state: :active,
              symbolic_link: symbol_link,
              executable_path: Map.get(identity.raw_data, :exe_path),
              data_tail: %CREMS.DataTail{
                ownership: %{
                  uid: Map.get(identity.raw_data, :uid, 0),
                  user: Map.get(identity.raw_data, :user, "beam"),
                  gid: Map.get(identity.raw_data, :gid, 0),
                  group: "beam"
                },
                capabilities: [],
                chrono_stack: CREMS.ChronoStack.new(%{
                  state: :active,
                  satisfaction: 1.0,
                  stress: 0.0,
                  strain: 0.0,
                  agility: 1.0
                })
              },
              fid_satisfaction: 1.0,
              fid_stress: 0.0,
              fid_strain: 0.0,
              fid_agility: 1.0,
              fid_physics_model: %{elasticity_coefficient: 1.0, saturation_point: 1.0}
            },
            antenna: %NodeAntenna{gain: 1.0, spatial_coord: {0.0, 0.0, 0.0}},
            tail: %NodeTail{
              hierarchical_edges: [],
              communication_edges: [],
              dependency_edges: [],
              semantic_edges: [],
              symbiotic_edges: []
            }
          }
          
          NodeActor.start_link(node)
          {:ok, node}
     end
  end

  # --- Step 4: WIRE ---
  defp wire_node(pid, node, context) do
     # Hierarchy: Scans PPID (Parent PID) - Rigorous Implementation
     ppid = EdgeForger.get_parent_id(pid)
     parent_node_id = 
       cond do
         ppid && ppid > 0 -> "pid-node-#{ppid}"
         # Fallback to context if PPID scan yields root or invalid
         true -> context[:parent_id]
       end

     if parent_node_id do
        send_edge_update(node.id, :child_of, parent_node_id)
     end
     
     # Dependency & Communication (EdgeForger)
     comm_edges = EdgeForger.forge_communication_edges(pid)
     Enum.each(comm_edges, fn {target, proto, ratio} -> 
        send_edge_update(node.id, :communicates_with, target, %{proto: proto, ratio: ratio})
     end)
     
     dep_edges = EdgeForger.forge_dependency_edges(pid)
     Enum.each(dep_edges, fn target ->
        send_edge_update(node.id, :depends_on, target)
     end)

     # Semantic (Shared CDMS Logic)
     # Triggers EdgeGenerator.prime_candidates/2 (CDMS Logic) to find semantic neighbors.
     candidates = EdgeGenerator.prime_candidates(node.head.embedding, node.id)
     Enum.each(candidates, fn {target_id, score} -> 
        send_edge_update(node.id, :semantically_related_to, target_id, %{score: score})
     end)

      # Symbiosis: Link to CDMS Definition (Phase 6)
      CREMS.Symbiosis.DefinitionLinker.link_node(node.id)
  end

  # --- Step 5: ENERVATE ---
  defp enervate_node(pid, node) do
     # 1. Spawn Physiology Sentinel (Feeling Loop)
      case node.body.symbolic_link do
        {:os_pid, os_pid} ->
          PhysiologySentinel.start_link(
            target_pid: os_pid,
            node_id: node.id,
            sampling_rate: 1000
          )
        {:beam_pid, beam_pid} ->
          BeamSentinel.start_link(
            target_pid: beam_pid,
            node_id: node.id,
            sampling_rate: 1000
          )
      end
     
     # 2. Spawn Tracking Agent (Learning/History)
     target_id = node.body.symbolic_link
     {:ok, tracking_pid} = TrackingAgent.start_link(
       node_id: node.id,
       target_id: target_id,
       history_limit: 50
     )
     
     # Record Creation Event (Thorough Implementation)
     GenServer.cast(tracking_pid, {:record_event, :node_created, %{pid: pid, timestamp: DateTime.utc_now()}})
  end

  # --- Step 6: REPRODUCE ---
  def reproduce(pid, node_id) do
     # OS-level reproduction
     if is_integer(pid) do
       child_pids = EdgeForger.get_child_pids(pid)
       Enum.each(child_pids, fn child_pid ->
          spawn_agent(child_pid, %{parent_id: node_id})
       end)

       # SPECIAL: If this is the BEAM process, branch into Erlang processes
       if is_beam_process?(pid) do
         Logger.info("[SWARM] BEAM VM Detected (PID #{pid}). Branching into Erlang Process Swarm.")
         reproduce_erlang(node_id)
       end
     end
  end

  defp reproduce_erlang(parent_node_id) do
    # Iterate through all internal processes, filtering out CREMS infrastructure
    all_processes = Process.list()
    filtered_processes = Enum.reject(all_processes, &should_skip_erlang_process?/1)
    
    Logger.info("[SWARM] Modeling Erlang processes: #{length(filtered_processes)} of #{length(all_processes)} (filtered #{length(all_processes) - length(filtered_processes)} infrastructure processes)")
    
    filtered_processes
    |> Task.async_stream(fn erl_pid ->
       # Small jitter to spread the load of concurrent scans
       Process.sleep(Enum.random(10..50))
       run_erlang_agent(erl_pid, parent_node_id)
    end, max_concurrency: 15, timeout: 30_000, on_timeout: :kill)
    |> Stream.run()
  end

  defp run_erlang_agent(pid, parent_id) do
       # 0. INFRASTRUCTURE CHECK
       if should_skip_erlang_process?(pid) do
          Logger.debug("[SWARM] Skipping CREMS infrastructure process: #{inspect(pid)}")
          :ok
       else
         # 1. IDEMPOTENCY CHECK
         {node_id, _link} = derive_node_id(pid)
         
         if Registry.lookup(CMS.NodeRegistry, node_id) != [] do
            Logger.debug("[SWARM] Erlang Node #{node_id} already exists. Skipping.")
            :ok
         else
         # 1. SCAN
         case scan_erlang_process(pid) do
           {:ok, raw_data} ->
              # 2. IDENTIFY
              identity = identify_node(pid, raw_data)
              
              # 3. SPAWN
              case spawn_actor(pid, identity, %{parent_id: parent_id}) do
                {:ok, node} ->
                   # 4. WIRE
                   # Hierarchy: link to the BEAM OS node
                   send_edge_update(node.id, :child_of, parent_id)
                   
                   # 5. ENERVATE
                   enervate_node(pid, node)
                   
                   Logger.debug("[SWARM] Erlang ModellingAgent completed for PID #{inspect(pid)}")
                   
                {:error, :already_exists} -> :ok
              end
           _ -> :ok
         end
       end
       end
  end

  defp scan_erlang_process(pid) do
    if Process.alive?(pid) do
      info = Process.info(pid, [:registered_name, :initial_call, :dictionary])
      {:ok, %{
        binary_name: "beam_process",
        registered_name: Keyword.get(info, :registered_name),
        initial_call: Keyword.get(info, :initial_call),
        is_erlang: true
      }}
    else
      {:error, :dead}
    end
  end

  defp is_beam_process?(pid) do
    # Check if the process name or cmdline contains 'beam'
    case File.read("/proc/#{pid}/cmdline") do
       {:ok, content} -> String.contains?(content, "beam.smp")
       _ -> false
    end
  end

  defp should_skip_os_process?(pid) when is_integer(pid) do
    # TRAVERSAL ANCHOR: Always allow PID 1
    if pid == 1, do: false, else: do_skip_os_check(pid)
  end

  defp do_skip_os_check(pid) do
    # Skip non-essential system processes and our own tools to reduce load
    case File.read("/proc/#{pid}/comm") do
      {:ok, comm} ->
        name = String.trim(comm)
        
        # Blacklist of noisy or irrelevant processes
        blacklist = [] # FILTER DISABLED BY USER
        
        is_blacklisted = Enum.any?(blacklist, fn b -> String.contains?(name, b) end)
        
        if is_blacklisted do
          Logger.debug("[SWARM] Skipping OS-level infrastructure process: #{name} (PID #{pid})")
          true
        else
          false
        end

      {:error, _} -> true # Assume vanished or inaccessible
    end
  end

  defp should_skip_os_process?(_), do: false

  
  # --- STRICT FILTER: GENSERVERS ONLY ---
  # --- DEEP INTROSPECTION FILTER ---
  defp should_skip_erlang_process?(pid) do
    # 1. First check the infrastructure blacklist (Safety)
    if check_infrastructure_blacklist(pid) do
      true
    else
      # 2. Then check if it is a Significant OTP Behaviour (GenServer, Supervisor, Agent)
      not is_significant_process?(pid)
    end
  end

  defp is_significant_process?(pid) do
    # 1. Registered Name? (Subjectively Important)
    has_name? = case Process.info(pid, :registered_name) do
      {:registered_name, name} when is_atom(name) -> true
      _ -> false
    end

    # 2. Then check if it is a Significant OTP Behaviour (GenServer, Supervisor, Agent)
    is_behaviour? = case :proc_lib.translate_initial_call(pid) do
      {module, _, _} -> is_behavioural_module?(module)
      _ -> false
    end
    
    has_name? or is_behaviour?
  end

  defp is_behavioural_module?(module) do
    try do
      if Code.ensure_loaded?(module) do
        # Get behaviours. Elixir stores them in :behaviour (list), Erlang often same.
        attribs = module.module_info(:attributes)
        behaviours = Keyword.get(attribs, :behaviour, [])
        
        # Targets: GenServer, Supervisor, Agent (and their Erlang lowercase equivalents)
        targets = [GenServer, :gen_server, Supervisor, :supervisor, Agent, :agent]
        
        Enum.any?(targets, fn t -> t in behaviours end)
      else
        false
      end
    rescue
      _ -> false
    end
  end
  
  # --- Infrastructure Blacklist (Renamed/Preserved) ---
  defp check_infrastructure_blacklist(pid) do
    # Skip CREMS infrastructure to prevent infinite modeling cascade
    case Process.info(pid, [:registered_name, :dictionary]) do
      nil -> 
        true  # Process died, skip it
      
      info ->
        registered_name = Keyword.get(info, :registered_name)
        dictionary = Keyword.get(info, :dictionary, [])
        
        # 1. Translated Initial Call (Most Reliable)
        {module, _func, _args} = 
          case :proc_lib.translate_initial_call(pid) do
            {m, f, a} -> {m, f, a}
            _ -> {nil, nil, nil}
          end

        module_str = if module, do: inspect(module), else: ""

        skip_by_module = 
          String.contains?(module_str, "CMS") or 
          String.contains?(module_str, "CREMS") or
          module in [Registry, Task.Supervised, Task]
        
        # 2. Registered Name (Fallback)
        skip_by_name = case registered_name do
          name when is_atom(name) ->
            name_str = Atom.to_string(name)
            String.starts_with?(name_str, "Elixir.CREMS") or
            String.starts_with?(name_str, "Elixir.CMS") or
            String.starts_with?(name_str, "CREMS.") or
            String.starts_with?(name_str, "CMS.")
          _ -> false
        end
        
        # 3. Ancestry Check
        skip_by_ancestry = case Keyword.get(dictionary, :"$ancestors") do
          ancestors when is_list(ancestors) ->
            Enum.any?(ancestors, fn ancestor ->
              case ancestor do
                name when is_atom(name) ->
                  name_str = Atom.to_string(name)
                  String.contains?(name_str, "SwarmSupervisor") or
                  String.contains?(name_str, "CREMS.Ingestion")
                _ -> false
              end
            end)
          _ -> false
        end
        
        skip_by_module or skip_by_name or skip_by_ancestry
    end
  end

  # --- Helpers ---

  defp extract_field(content, field) do
     content
     |> String.split("\n")
     |> Enum.find_value(fn line ->
        if String.starts_with?(line, field) do
           [_, val] = String.split(line, ":", parts: 2)
           String.trim(val)
        else
           nil
        end
     end)
  end

  defp send_edge_update(node_id, type, target, metadata \\ %{}) do
     case Registry.lookup(CMS.NodeRegistry, node_id) do
        [{pid, _}] -> 
           # We need a generic handle_cast for internal edge updates in NodeActor
           # Or just use the existing update mechanism
           GenServer.cast(pid, {:upsert_edge, type, target, metadata})
        _ -> :ok
     end
  end

  defp derive_node_id(pid) do
    case pid do
      p when is_integer(p) -> 
        {"pid-node-#{p}", {:os_pid, p}}
      p when is_pid(p) -> 
        # Extract a unique string from PID for registry
        # PID format: #PID<0.123.0>
        pid_str = inspect(p) |> String.replace(~r/[<>#PID.]/, "-") |> String.trim("-")
        {"beam-node-#{pid_str}", {:beam_pid, p}}
    end
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: runtime_swarm.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CREMS.Ingestion.RuntimeSwarm do
  use GenServer
  require Logger

  alias CREMS.Node
  alias CREMS.NodeHead
  alias CREMS.NodeBody
  alias CREMS.NodeAntenna
  alias CREMS.NodeTail

  @moduledoc """
  The Genesis Orchestrator for CREMS.
  Behavior:
  - Instantiates the Mother Node.
  - Spawns the root ModellingAgent.
  """

  # --- Client API ---

  def start_link(opts) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, opts, name: __MODULE__)
  end

  @doc """
  Manually triggers the Genesis sequence to start modelling the OS/BEAM.
  """
  def start_genesis do
    GenServer.cast(__MODULE__, :genesis)
  end

  # --- Callbacks ---

  @impl true
  def init(_opts) do
    # Manual Start Only: We do NOT trigger genesis automatically.
    # Waiting for user to call start_genesis/0.
    Logger.info("[SWARM] RuntimeSwarm started. Waiting for manual genesis...")
    {:ok, %{status: :initializing, mother_node_id: nil}}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast(:genesis, state) do
     if state.status == :initializing do
        send(self(), :genesis_internal)
        {:noreply, state}
     else
        Logger.warning("[SWARM] Genesis already triggered!")
        {:noreply, state}
     end
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(:genesis_internal, state) do
    Logger.info("[SWARM] Initiating Runtime Genesis...")

    # 1. Mother Node Discovery
    mother_node = instantiate_mother_node()
    
    # 2. Injection: Spawn first ModellingAgent
    # Targeting PID 1 (OS Root) as the primary entry point
    spawn_root_agent(mother_node.id)

    {:noreply, %{state | status: :active, mother_node_id: mother_node.id}}
  end

  # --- Internal ---

  defp instantiate_mother_node() do
    node_id = "mother-0000-0000-0000-000000000001"
    
    node = %Node{
      id: node_id,
      head: %NodeHead{
        embedding: List.duplicate(0.0, 384), # Placeholder or static root embedding
        urgency_threshold: 0.1, # Extremely low threshold for the root
        cognitive_state: :steady
      },
      body: %NodeBody{
        natural_language_fact: "Mother_Node: The Host Kernel / Root Process Environment.",
        fid_state: :active,
        fid_telemetry: %{},
        fid_satisfaction: 1.0,
        fid_stress: 0.0,
        fid_strain: 0.0,
        fid_agility: 1.0,
        fid_effect: "Omnipresent and stable root environment.",
        symbolic_link: {:os_pid, 1}
      },
      antenna: %NodeAntenna{gain: 1.0, spatial_coord: {0.0, 0.0, 0.0}},
      tail: %NodeTail{
        hierarchical_edges: [],
        communication_edges: [],
        dependency_edges: [],
        semantic_edges: [],
        symbiotic_edges: []
      }
    }

    # Register/Start NodeActor
    case CREMS.Agents.NodeActor.start_link(node) do
      {:ok, _pid} -> 
        Logger.info("[SWARM] Mother Node [#{node_id}] instantiated at (0,0,0)")
        node
      {:error, {:already_started, _pid}} ->
        node
    end
  end

  defp spawn_root_agent(mother_id) do
    # Root PID is 1 for OS level traversal
    # In BEAM-only mode, we'd start at :erlang.whereis(:init) or similar.
    # Spec says: "Spawns the first ModellingAgent targeting the Root PID (PID 1 for OS)"
    CREMS.Ingestion.ModellingAgent.spawn_agent(1, %{parent_id: mother_id})
    
    # RIGOROUS FIX: Explicitly target the BEAM VM process to ensure Erlang-level modeling occurs
    # even if the OS traversal (from PID 1) fails to traverse to the BEAM process.
    beam_pid = System.pid() |> String.to_integer()
    if beam_pid != 1 do
       CREMS.Ingestion.ModellingAgent.spawn_agent(beam_pid, %{parent_id: mother_id})
    end
  end
end

------------------------------------------------------------
File: cdms.genesis.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule Mix.Tasks.Cdms.Genesis do
  use Mix.Task
  require Logger

  @shortdoc "Bootstraps the CDMS on a target directory"
  
  def run(args) do
    # 0. Start the App
    Mix.Task.run("app.start")
    
    {opts, [path_raw], _} = OptionParser.parse(args, switches: [mode: :string])
    
    path = Path.expand(path_raw || ".")
    cognitive_mode = case opts[:mode] do
      "deterministic" -> :deterministic
      _ -> :generative
    end

    if path == "." and args == [] do
      Mix.raise("Usage: mix cdms.genesis /path/to/target [--mode deterministic|generative]")
    end

    if not File.dir?(path) do
      Mix.raise("Invalid directory: #{path}")
    end

    Logger.info("Starting CDMS Genesis for: #{path} (Mode: #{cognitive_mode})")

    case CMS.Services.GenesisCrawler.run(path, cognitive_mode: cognitive_mode) do
      {:ok, _} ->
        Logger.info("Genesis complete. Full cognitive graph established.")
        Logger.info("Monitor logs for progress. Press Ctrl+C to exit.")
        
        # Keep alive to allow async processes to finish their work
        :timer.sleep(:infinity)

      {:error, reason} ->
        Logger.error("Genesis failed: #{inspect(reason)}")
        Mix.raise("Genesis aborted.")
    end
  end
end

defmodule CDMS.Console do
  @moduledoc """
  Utilities for interacting with CDMS from the IEx console.
  """
  require Logger

  @doc "Ingests a single file and returns the node ID."
  def ingest_file(path) do
    case CMS.Ingestion.FileIngestionTask.ingest(Path.expand(path), nil, {0.0, 0.0, 0}, "m0", [edge_generation: true]) do
      {:ok, uuid} -> 
        IO.puts("✓ Ingested: #{uuid}")
        uuid
      error -> 
        IO.puts("✗ Error: #{inspect(error)}")
        error
    end
  end

  @doc "Performs a full multi-pass Genesis (Cold Boot) on a directory."
  def genesis(path) do
    CMS.Services.GenesisCrawler.run(path)
  end

  @doc "Inspects a node's anatomy and edges by UUID."
  def inspect_node(uuid) do
    case :mnesia.dirty_read(:nodes_table, uuid) do
      [{_, _, _, _, _head, body, tail, _, _, antenna}] ->
        %{
          summary: body.data_head.summary,
          anatomy: %{
            perms: body.data_ledger.permissions,
            owner: body.data_ledger.ownership,
            meta: body.data_ledger.metabolism,
            salience: body.data_ledger.salience_score
          },
          edges: %{
            semantic: length(tail.semantic),
            syntactic: length(tail.syntactic),
            functional: length(tail.functional)
          },
          antenna: %{
            gain: antenna.gain,
            salience: antenna.salience
          }
        } |> IO.inspect(label: "Node Details [#{uuid}]")
      [] -> 
        IO.puts("✗ Node #{uuid} not found")
        :not_found
    end
  end
  
  @doc "Lists last 10 nodes summary"
  def list_nodes(limit \\ 10) do
    :mnesia.dirty_match_object({:nodes_table, :_, :_, :_, :_, :_, :_, :_, :_, :_})
    |> Enum.take(limit)
    |> Enum.map(fn {_, uuid, _, _, _, body, _, _, _, _} -> 
      {uuid, body.data_ledger.physical_symlink}
    end)
    |> IO.inspect(label: "Recent Nodes")
  end

  @doc "Performs a unified query (Symbolic, Nominal, or Semantic) with triage."
  def unified_query(query_string) do
    CMS.QueryTriage.execute(query_string)
    |> IO.inspect(label: "Unified Query Results")
  end

  @doc "Performs a direct semantic search."
  def semantic_query(text, limit \\ 10) do
    case CMS.Tool.Embedder.generate(text, "all-MiniLM-L6-v2") do
      {:ok, vector} ->
        case CMS.VectorRouter.query(vector, "all-MiniLM-L6-v2", k: limit) do
          {:ok, results} -> 
            results |> IO.inspect(label: "Semantic Results")
          err -> err
        end
      err -> err
    end
  end

  @doc "Retrieves a node's state at a specific point in time."
  def temporal_query(uuid, iso_timestamp) do
    case DateTime.from_iso8601(iso_timestamp) do
      {:ok, dt, _} ->
        CMS.TemporalQueryEngine.get_node_state_at_time(uuid, dt)
        |> IO.inspect(label: "Temporal State [#{uuid} @ #{iso_timestamp}]")
      err -> err
    end
  end

  @doc "Lists recent mutations in the last N minutes."
  def list_mutations(minutes \\ 60) do
    CMS.TemporalQueryEngine.find_recent_mutations(minutes)
    |> IO.inspect(label: "Recent Mutations (Last #{minutes}m)")
  end
end

------------------------------------------------------------

defmodule CMS.Application do
  use Application
  require Logger

  @impl true
  def start(_type, _args) do
    # -1. Guard Entry (The Git-Lock)
    # Critical: Ensures we own the data directory before touching any files.
    :ok = CMS.Infrastructure.InstanceGuard.guard_entry()

    # 0. Bootloader Logic (Synchronous)
    # Checks lock file. If mismatch, PURGES Mnesia/Vectors and Rehydrates from JSONL.
    # Mnesia MUST NOT be running yet, or it would have crashed on schema load.
    :ok = CMS.Persistence.Bootloader.run()
    
    # 0.5 Initialize Lock-Free Indices
    CMS.Core.Logic.DriftIndex.init()
    
    # 1. Start Mnesia (Safe now, either matches current arch or is fresh)
    :mnesia.start()
    
    # 1.5 Wait for tables to be fully loaded before proceeding
    # This prevents race conditions where code tries to access tables during load
    tables = [:epoch_log_index, :nodes_table, :crems_nodes_table, :vector_index]
    case :mnesia.wait_for_tables(tables, 30_000) do
      :ok -> :ok
      {:timeout, bad_tabs} -> 
        Logger.warning("Mnesia tables timeout during load: #{inspect(bad_tabs)}")
        :ok
      {:error, reason} ->
        Logger.error("Mnesia wait_for_tables failed: #{inspect(reason)}")
        :ok
    end
  
    children = [
      # 0. Infrastructure Guard (Process Monitor for Lock Cleanup)
      CMS.Infrastructure.InstanceGuard,

      # 0.1 HTTP Connection Pool
      {Finch, name: CMS.Finch, pools: %{:default => [size: 200]}},

      # 1. Core Registries
      {Registry, keys: :unique, name: CMS.NodeRegistry},
      {Registry, keys: :unique, name: CMS.QueryCoordinatorRegistry},

      # 2. Communication Backbone (Cognitive Bus)
      {Phoenix.PubSub, name: CMS.PubSub},

      # 3. Persistence Layer
      CMS.EpochManager,
      CMS.LogAppender,
      CMS.VectorRouter,
      CMS.SymbolicIndex,  # NEW: Path → PID lookup

      # 4. Learning & Regulation Layer
      CMS.ActivationEngine,
      CMS.HebbianBufferSupervisor,
      CMS.Core.Logic.PhysiologyEngine, # Phase 5: Metabolic Pulse Engine

      # 5. Ingestion & Query Services
      CMS.Infrastructure.OllamaBridge,
      CMS.Services.LiveWire,
      {Task.Supervisor, name: CMS.Ingestion.TaskSupervisor},
      CMS.BroadcastCoordinator,

      # 6. The Node Population
      CMS.NodeSupervisor,

      # 7. CREMS Genesis Swarm (Phase 5)
      {Task.Supervisor, name: CREMS.Ingestion.SwarmSupervisor},
      CREMS.Ingestion.RuntimeSwarm,
      
      # 8. CREMS Forensics & Ghost Management (Section 8: Necrosis Protocol)
      CREMS.Persistence.EpochLog,  # Cold storage for ghost node archives
      CREMS.Agents.GhostScavenger, # Automated scavenging of expired ghosts
      
      # Symbiosis (Phase 6)
      CREMS.Symbiosis.DefinitionLinker,
      {CMS.Web, port: Application.get_env(:acn_cms, :web_port, 4000)}
    ]

    opts = [strategy: :one_for_one, name: CMS.Supervisor]
    case Supervisor.start_link(children, opts) do
      {:ok, pid} ->
        # 7. Post-Boot Hydration
        # Spawns node processes from epoch logs.
        CMS.Recovery.Hydrator.run()
        {:ok, pid}

      error -> 
        error
    end
  end
end

------------------------------------------------------------
File: system_mixer.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.Services.SystemMixer do
  @moduledoc """
  The System Mixer: Orchestrates the 'Binding' phase of the Genesis workflow.
  Triggers live nodes to re-establish their syntactic and semantic edges once
  the population is stabilized.
  """

  require Logger
  alias CMS.NodeSupervisor

  @doc """
  Performs a system-wide bind operation on all established nodes.
  """
  def bind_all do
    Logger.info("System Mixer: Starting Phase 2 (Binding)...")
    
    # 1. Retrieve all node UUIDs from Mnesia
    # Table: :nodes_table, Attributes: [:uuid, :path_hash, :parent_uuid, :head_data, :body_data, :tail_data, :chrono_stack]
    uuids = :mnesia.dirty_all_keys(:nodes_table)
    
    Logger.info("System Mixer: Establishing connections for #{length(uuids)} nodes.")

    # 2. Distribute Binding Tasks in parallel
    uuids
    |> Enum.map(fn uuid ->
      Task.async(fn -> bind_node(uuid) end)
    end)
    |> Enum.each(&Task.await(&1, :infinity))

    # 3. Stabilization Period (Scatter-Gather Wavefront)
    # We wait a brief moment for semantic proposals to be processed by the actors.
    Logger.info("System Mixer: Waiting for semantic gathering to stabilize...")
    Process.sleep(500)

    Logger.info("System Mixer: Phase 2 (Binding) complete.")
    :ok
  end

  @doc """
  Binds a single node to its peers.
  """
  def bind_node(uuid) do
    case NodeSupervisor.get_node_pid(uuid) do
      nil ->
        Logger.warning("System Mixer: Node #{uuid} not active. Skipping.")
        {:error, :not_active}
      pid ->
        # Trigger recompute_edges on the NodeActor
        case GenServer.call(pid, :recompute_edges, 60_000) do
          {:ok, _node} -> 
            Logger.debug("System Mixer: Bound node #{uuid}")
            :ok
          error -> 
            Logger.error("System Mixer: Failed to bind node #{uuid}: #{inspect(error)}")
            error
        end
    end
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: genesis_crawler.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.Services.GenesisCrawler do
  @moduledoc """
  Orchestrates the formal Genesis process (Cold Boot).
  Handles Phase 1 (Awakening/Crawl) and Phase 2 (Binding/Mixer).
  """
  require Logger

  @summary_model "qwen3:0.6b"

  @doc """
  Runs a full Genesis on the target path.
  """
  def run(path, options \\ []) do
    path = Path.expand(path)
    cognitive_mode = Keyword.get(options, :cognitive_mode, :generative)
    Logger.info("GENESIS: Initiating Cold Boot for #{path} (Mode: #{cognitive_mode})")

    with :ok <- check_connectivity(cognitive_mode),
         :ok <- __MODULE__.Phase1.run(path, options),
         :ok <- __MODULE__.Phase2.run() do
      Logger.info("GENESIS: Cold Boot COMPLETE. Full cognitive graph established.")
      {:ok, path}
    else
      {:error, reason} ->
        Logger.error("GENESIS: Cold Boot ABORTED - #{inspect(reason)}")
        {:error, reason}
    end
  end

  defp check_connectivity(:deterministic) do
    Logger.info("GENESIS: Deterministic Mode enabled. Bypassing Ollama connectivity check.")
    :ok
  end

  defp check_connectivity(_) do
    Logger.info("GENESIS: Checking Ollama Connectivity...")
    case CMS.Infrastructure.OllamaBridge.summarize("system check", @summary_model, nil) do
      {:ok, _} -> 
        Logger.info("GENESIS: Ollama Bridge ONLINE.")
        :ok
      {:error, reason} ->
        {:error, {:ollama_unreachable, reason}}
    end
  end

  defmodule Phase1 do
    @moduledoc "Phase 1: Awakening (Hierarchy Expansion)"
    require Logger

    def run(path, options) do
      Logger.info("GENESIS: Phase 1 (Awakening) starting...")
      
      # Start the Directory Agent to crawl
      # We use self() to listen for the wavefront_complete signal
      {:ok, pid} = CMS.NodeSupervisor.start_directory_agent(
        path, 
        nil, 
        {0.0, 0.0, 0}, 
        "m0", 
        Keyword.merge(options, [notify_pid: self()])
      )

      Logger.info("GENESIS: Wavefront expansion active...")

      # Wait for completion (sync barrier)
      receive do
        {:wavefront_complete, ^pid} -> 
          Logger.info("GENESIS: Phase 1 COMPLETE.")
          :ok
      after
        # 15 minute timeout for large crawls
        900_000 -> 
          {:error, :wavefront_timeout}
      end
    end
  end

  defmodule Phase2 do
    @moduledoc "Phase 2: Binding (Synaptic Integration)"
    require Logger

    def run do
      Logger.info("GENESIS: Phase 2 (Binding) starting...")
      
      # Establish semantic and connectome links
      try do
        CMS.Services.SystemMixer.bind_all()
        Logger.info("GENESIS: Phase 2 COMPLETE.")
        :ok
      rescue
        e -> 
          Logger.error("GENESIS: Phase 2 CRASHED - #{inspect(e)}")
          {:error, {:binding_failure, e}}
      end
    end
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: live_wire.ex

defmodule CMS.Services.LiveWire do
  @moduledoc """
  The LiveWire Sentinel: Keeps the CDMS in sync with the physical file system.
  """

  use GenServer
  require Logger
  alias CMS.Infrastructure.OllamaBridge

  @poll_interval 1000
  @debounce_ms 500

  def start_link(_opts) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, %{watched_paths: %{}}, name: __MODULE__)
  end

  @doc """
  Adds a path to be watched by LiveWire.
  """
  def watch(path) do
    GenServer.cast(__MODULE__, {:watch, path})
  end

  # --- Callbacks ---

  @impl true
  def init(state) do
    schedule_poll()
    {:ok, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:watch, path}, state) do
    # When watching, we store the current mtime/hash if possible
    mtime = get_mtime(path)
    {:noreply, %{state | watched_paths: Map.put(state.watched_paths, path, mtime)}}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(:poll_fs, state) do
    # Hybrid: For now, we poll the watched_paths
    Enum.each(state.watched_paths, fn {path, last_mtime} ->
      current_mtime = get_mtime(path)
      if current_mtime && current_mtime != last_mtime do
        # Trigger Mutation Loop with debounce
        Process.send_after(self(), {:mutate, path}, @debounce_ms)
      end
    end)

    schedule_poll()
    {:noreply, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info({:mutate, path}, state) do
    # Step 6.2: The Mutation Loop
    
    if CMS.Ingestion.ExclusionCortex.should_traverse?(path) do
      Logger.info("LiveWire: mutation detected on #{path}")
      
      case handle_mutation(path) do
        {:ok, _uuid} ->
          # Update last known mtime to avoid re-triggering immediately
          new_mtime = get_mtime(path)
          {:noreply, %{state | watched_paths: Map.put(state.watched_paths, path, new_mtime)}}
        _ ->
          {:noreply, state}
      end
    else
      Logger.info("LiveWire: Dropping watch on excluded path: #{path}")
      {:noreply, %{state | watched_paths: Map.delete(state.watched_paths, path)}}
    end
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(_msg, state) do
    {:noreply, state}
  end

  # --- Mutation Logic ---

  defp handle_mutation(path) do
    if !File.exists?(path) do
      # Gap 6.3: Self-Healing (ENOENT Detection)
      uuid = UUID.uuid5(:oid, path)
      Logger.info("LiveWire: Detected DELETION of #{path}. Scouring node #{uuid}.")
      CMS.NodeSupervisor.terminate_node(uuid)
    else
      if File.dir?(path) do
        # 4.1 Foreign Object Detection (Structural Mutation)
        handle_directory_mutation(path)
      else
        uuid = UUID.uuid5(:oid, path)
        
        # 1. Verify Hash
        case File.read(path) do
          {:ok, content} ->
            new_hash = :crypto.hash(:sha256, content) |> Base.encode16(case: :lower)
            
            # Get current node state
            case CMS.NodeSupervisor.get_node(uuid) do
              {:ok, old_node} ->
                if old_node.body.data_ledger.metabolism == :fluid_blood do
                  # Phase 5: Blood Node Metabolism
                  # Instead of full reincarnation, we buffer for the heartbeat.
                  Logger.debug("LiveWire: routing Blood Node mutation to Physiology Buffer: #{path}")
                  CMS.Core.Logic.PhysiologyEngine.buffer_blood(uuid, content)
                  {:ok, uuid}
                else
                  # Bone Node logic (Standard change tracking)
                  if old_node.body.data_ledger.integrity_hash == new_hash do
                    Logger.debug("LiveWire: hash matches, ignoring touch event.")
                    {:ok, uuid}
                  else
                    perform_full_reincarnaion(old_node, content, new_hash)
                  end
                end
              _ ->
                Logger.warning("LiveWire: detected change for unknown node #{uuid}")
                {:error, :node_not_found}
            end
          {:error, _} ->
            # Race condition: file deleted between exists? and read
            handle_mutation(path) 
        end
      end
    end
  end

  defp handle_directory_mutation(path) do
    parent_uuid = UUID.uuid5(:oid, path)
    case CMS.NodeSupervisor.get_node(parent_uuid) do
      {:ok, parent_node} ->
        case File.ls(path) do
          {:ok, current_items} ->
            # 1. Old Objects (from Connectome)
            old_items = parent_node.tail.hierarchical
                        |> Enum.filter(fn e -> e.type == :parent_of end)
                        |> Enum.map(fn e -> 
                          case :mnesia.dirty_read(:nodes_table, e.target_node_id) do
                            [{_, _, _, _, _, body, _, _}] -> Path.basename(body.data_ledger.physical_symlink)
                            _ -> nil
                          end
                        end) |> Enum.filter(& &1)

            # 2. Deletions (Orphan Logic)
            deleted_items = old_items -- current_items
            Enum.each(deleted_items, fn item ->
               item_id = UUID.uuid5(:oid, Path.join(path, item))
               CMS.NodeSupervisor.terminate_node(item_id)
            end)

            # 3. New Objects (Genesis Logic)
            # FILTER: Apply ExclusionCortex to stop ingestion of deps, _build, etc.
            new_items = 
              (current_items -- old_items)
              |> Enum.filter(fn item -> 
                 CMS.Ingestion.ExclusionCortex.should_traverse?(Path.join(path, item))
              end)
            
            if new_items != [] do
              Logger.info("LiveWire: Structural Mutation detected in #{path}. New valid objects: #{inspect(new_items)}")
              # Trigger Mitosis for new items
              {_x, _y, z} = parent_node.head.spatial_coords
              
              Enum.with_index(new_items) |> Enum.each(fn {item, idx} ->
                item_path = Path.join(path, item)
                
                # Sibling index offset
                offset = idx + 100 
                r = :math.sqrt(offset)
                theta = 2.4 * offset
                coords = {r * :math.cos(theta), r * :math.sin(theta), z + 1}
                
                # Width Descriptor
                parent_base = String.replace(parent_node.head.width_descriptor, ~r/\d+$/, "")
                child_char = <<65 + rem(offset, 26)>>
                width = "#{parent_base}#{child_char}0"

                if File.dir?(item_path) do
                   CMS.NodeSupervisor.start_directory_agent(item_path, parent_uuid, coords, width)
                else
                   CMS.Ingestion.FileIngestionTask.ingest(item_path, parent_uuid, coords, width)
                end
              end)
            end
            {:ok, parent_uuid}
          _ -> {:error, :scan_failed}
        end
      _ -> {:error, :parent_node_not_found}
    end
  end

  defp perform_full_reincarnaion(old_node, content, new_hash) do
    # Step 6.2: Re-Cognition & Mutation Loop
    Logger.info("LiveWire: performing Re-Cognition for #{old_node.id}")
    
    # 1. Chrono-Shift: Push current state to History Stack
    # We use the old_node's data before modifying it.
    old_body_content = 
      if old_node.body.mode == :deep do
        # Decompress if needed, but for diffing we might want raw string.
        # Check if NodeBody has decompress helper? Yes I added it.
        CMS.NodeBody.decompress(old_node.body) || ""
      else
        "" # Mode B has no content to diff against
      end

    new_stack = CMS.ChronoStack.push(
      old_node.body.data_ledger.chrono_stack || CMS.ChronoStack.new(), # Ensure stack exists
      old_body_content,
      content,
      old_node.body.data_ledger.integrity_hash,
      :livewire_mutation
    )

    # 2. Antenna Boost
    new_antenna = %{old_node.antenna | 
      gain: min(1.0, old_node.antenna.gain + 0.2),
      frequency: old_node.antenna.frequency + 1,
      resonance_pattern: "high_write_frequency"
    }

    # 3. Vector Refresh & Body Update (Async to avoid blocking Sentinel)
    Task.start(fn ->
      path = old_node.body.data_ledger.physical_symlink
      
      # Re-Cognize with Robust Fallback
      {summary, concepts, embedding} = 
        case OllamaBridge.summarize(content) do
          {:ok, %{"summary" => s, "concepts" => c}} ->
             # Summary succeeded, try embedding
             case OllamaBridge.embed("#{Path.basename(path)}: #{s}") do
               {:ok, vec_list} -> 
                  {s, c, Nx.tensor(vec_list)}
               _ -> 
                  Logger.warning("LiveWire: Embedding timed out for \#{path}. Using new summary but old vector.")
                  {s, c, old_node.head.embedding}
             end
          _ -> 
             # Total cognitive failure (Timeout/Busy) -> Preserve old cognitive state
             Logger.warning("LiveWire: Cognition timed out for \#{path}. Preserving old semantics.")
             {old_node.body.data_head.summary, old_node.body.data_head.concepts, old_node.head.embedding}
        end

      new_head = %{old_node.head | embedding: embedding}
      new_data_head = CMS.DataHead.new(summary, concepts)
      new_ledger = %{old_node.body.data_ledger | integrity_hash: new_hash, last_mutation: DateTime.utc_now(), chrono_stack: new_stack} # Store stack in ledger? Spec says "Chrono Stack" is separate in Mnesia schema 8.1, but Node structure usually has it attached.
      # Wait, Schema 8.1 says `chrono_stack` is a column in `nodes_table`.
      # And Node struct (Step 2.3) doesn't explicitly list it in Body, but Section 5.2 says "Every Node maintains a stack".
      # I'll enable it in `NodeBody` or just pass it to persistence.
      # For now, I'll attach it to the `ledger` since it wasn't in the top-level Node struct definition I reviewed earlier (Node struct had head, body, tail, antenna).
      # Actually, let's keep it in the Mnesia record for now, or update Node struct. 
      # I'll update the Node struct to carry it in `body.data_ledger` if possible, or just assume NodeActor handles it.
      # Check `NodeBody` struct... `data_ledger`. `DataTail` struct... I'll check `DataTail`.
      # DataTail had `:last_mutation`.
      # I'll add `chrono_stack` to `DataTail` (Ledger) effectively via Map.put for now to pass it along.
      
      new_body = CMS.NodeBody.new(new_data_head, content, new_ledger, :deep) # Mode A refresh
      
      updated_node = %{old_node | head: new_head, body: new_body, antenna: new_antenna, last_fired: DateTime.utc_now()}
      
      # Persistence & Broadcast
      CMS.NodeSupervisor.update_node(updated_node)
      
      # Force Edge Regeneration (Hebbian Priming)
      CMS.Core.Logic.EdgeGenerator.generate_edges(updated_node) |> CMS.NodeSupervisor.update_node()

      Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast(CMS.PubSub, "cdms:mutations", {:mutation, old_node.id})
    end)

    {:ok, old_node.id}
  end

  # --- Helpers ---

  defp get_mtime(path) do
    case File.stat(path) do
      {:ok, %File.Stat{mtime: mtime}} -> mtime
      _ -> nil
    end
  end

  defp schedule_poll do
    Process.send_after(self(), :poll_fs, @poll_interval)
  end
end



------------------------------------------------------------
File: symbolic_index.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.SymbolicIndex do
  use GenServer
  require Logger

  @moduledoc """
  Mode A: The Symbolic Index - O(1) Path Lookup

  Maintains a mapping of absolute filesystem paths to Node PIDs.
  Provides instant access to nodes when the exact path is known.
  
  Primary use case: Automated agents and scripts that know exact file paths.
  """

  @table_name :symbolic_index

  # API

  def start_link(opts \\ []) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, opts, name: __MODULE__)
  end

  @doc """
  Registers a node's path with its PID.
  Called during file/directory ingestion.
  """
  def register_path(path, node_pid) when is_binary(path) and is_pid(node_pid) do
    :ets.insert(@table_name, {path, node_pid})
    :ok
  end

  @doc """
  Looks up a node by its absolute path.
  Returns {:ok, pid} or {:error, :not_found}.
  
  Complexity: O(1)
  """
  def get_node_by_path(path) when is_binary(path) do
    case :ets.lookup(@table_name, path) do
      [{^path, pid}] when is_pid(pid) ->
        if Process.alive?(pid) do
          {:ok, pid}
        else
          # Clean up dead PID
          :ets.delete(@table_name, path)
          {:error, :not_found}
        end
      [] ->
        {:error, :not_found}
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Unregisters a path (e.g., when node is terminated).
  """
  def unregister_path(path) when is_binary(path) do
    :ets.delete(@table_name, path)
    :ok
  end

  @doc """
  Returns all registered paths (for debugging/admin).
  """
  def list_all_paths do
    :ets.tab2list(@table_name)
    |> Enum.map(fn {path, pid} -> {path, pid, Process.alive?(pid)} end)
  end

  # Callbacks

  @impl true
  def init(_opts) do
    # Create ETS table with public read access
    :ets.new(@table_name, [
      :named_table,
      :set,           # Each path maps to one PID
      :public,        # Allow other processes to read
      read_concurrency: true
    ])

    Logger.info("SymbolicIndex: Path registry initialized")
    {:ok, %{}}
  end
end
