------------------------------------------------------------
File: query_coordinator.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.QueryCoordinator do
  use GenServer
  require Logger

  alias CMS.LogAppender

  @moduledoc """
  Orchestrates the lifecycle of a single query.

  Implements Gap 1: Global Inhibition / Winner-Takes-Most (WTM).

  It collects results from responding NodeActors.
  Optimization: Implements a "Gathering Window" to finalize queries immediately
  after results start arriving, avoiding fixed timeouts in broadcast mode.
  """

  # Configuration
  @inhibit_threshold_count 100  # If >100 nodes fire, trigger inhibition
  @query_timeout 10_000         # Max time to wait if NO results arrive
  # @gathering_window replaced by dynamic logic (50ms hardcoded per spec)
  @inhibit_check_delay 150      # Check for explosion 150ms after start

  # State
  defstruct [
    :query_id,
    :origin_pid,
    :target_regions,
    :fired_nodes,    # Map: %{node_id => {relevance, node_data}}
    :top_k_list,     # List: [{relevance, node_id}, ...] sorted desc
    :active_regions, # MapSet of regions pending completion
    :inhibited?,     # Boolean
    :timeout_timer,   # Reference to the active timeout timer
    :max_results     # Integer or nil
  ]

  # API

  @doc """
  Starts a coordinator for a specific query.
  """
  def start_link(query_id, target_regions, origin_pid, opts \\ []) do
    max_results = Keyword.get(opts, :max_results)
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, {query_id, target_regions, origin_pid, max_results}, name: via_tuple(query_id))
  end

  @doc """
  Called by a NodeActor when it decides to fire.
  """
  def node_fired(query_id, node_id, relevance, node_data) do
    case Registry.lookup(CMS.QueryCoordinatorRegistry, query_id) do
      [{pid, _}] ->
        GenServer.cast(pid, {:node_fired, node_id, relevance, node_data})
      [] ->
        Logger.warning("QueryCoordinator: Node #{node_id} attempted to fire for Query #{query_id}, but Coordinator not found.")
        :ok
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Called by the BusPipeline when a region has finished processing the initial broadcast.
  """
  def region_complete(query_id, region_id) do
    case Registry.lookup(CMS.QueryCoordinatorRegistry, query_id) do
      [{pid, _}] -> GenServer.cast(pid, {:region_complete, region_id})
      [] -> :ok
    end
  end

  # Callbacks

  @impl true
  def init({query_id, target_regions, origin_pid, max_results}) do
    Logger.debug("Coordinator #{query_id}: Initializing...")

    # Start the failsafe timeout (10s)
    timer_ref = Process.send_after(self(), :timeout, @query_timeout)

    # Rapid inhibition check
    Process.send_after(self(), :check_inhibition, @inhibit_check_delay)

    # CRITICAL FIX 1: Signal the caller (Web.Router) that the coordinator is ready.
    # This prevents the race condition where nodes fire before the coordinator is findable.
    send(origin_pid, {:coordinator_ready, query_id})
    Logger.debug("Coordinator #{query_id}: Ready signal sent to #{inspect(origin_pid)}.")

    {:ok, %__MODULE__{
      query_id: query_id,
      origin_pid: origin_pid,
      target_regions: target_regions,
      active_regions: MapSet.new(target_regions),
      fired_nodes: %{},
      top_k_list: [],
      inhibited?: false,
      timeout_timer: timer_ref,
      max_results: max_results
    }}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:node_fired, node_id, relevance, node_data}, state) do
    # NEW LOGGING: Confirm receipt of fired node
    Logger.debug("Coordinator #{state.query_id}: Received fired node #{node_id} (Score: #{:io_lib.format("~.4f", [relevance])})")

    # CRITICAL FIX: Duplicate Prevention logic
    should_update =
      case Map.get(state.fired_nodes, node_id) do
        {existing_score, _} when existing_score >= relevance -> false
        _ -> true
      end

    if should_update do
      # 1. Store Result
      new_fired = Map.put(state.fired_nodes, node_id, {relevance, node_data})

      # 2. Update Top-K (Insertion Sort / limited list / deduplication)
      new_top_k = update_top_k(state.top_k_list, {relevance, node_id}, state.max_results)

      # 3. Latency Optimization (The Gathering Window)
      new_timer =
        if map_size(state.fired_nodes) == 0 do
          Logger.debug("Coordinator #{state.query_id}: First result received. Switching to fast-path gathering window (5000ms).")
          Process.cancel_timer(state.timeout_timer)
          Process.send_after(self(), :timeout, 5000) # Increased to 500ms for Spreading Activation
        else
          state.timeout_timer
        end

      new_state = %{state | fired_nodes: new_fired, top_k_list: new_top_k, timeout_timer: new_timer}

      # 4. Check Immediate Overload (Global Inhibition)
      if not state.inhibited? and map_size(new_fired) > 100 do # Hardcoded 100 per spec
        Logger.warning("Coordinator #{state.query_id}: Threshold exceeded (#{map_size(new_fired)} nodes). TRIGGERING GLOBAL INHIBITION.")
        
        # A. Broadcast STOP
        trigger_inhibition(state.query_id, state.active_regions)
        
        # B. Log System Congestion
        LogAppender.append_system_event(:system_congestion, %{level: "critical", source: "QueryCoordinator", result_count: map_size(new_fired)})
        
        # C. Lower Factor (Increase Congestion)
        # We set congestion to 1.0 (Critical) to force factor -> 0.1
        CMS.ActivationEngine.set_congestion_level(1.0)

        report_query_failure(state, :cognitive_overload)
        {:noreply, %{new_state | inhibited?: true}}
      else
        {:noreply, new_state}
      end
    else
      # Duplicate or lower score ignored
      {:noreply, state}
    end
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:region_complete, region_id}, state) do
    new_active = MapSet.delete(state.active_regions, region_id)

    if MapSet.size(new_active) == 0 do
      Logger.debug("Coordinator #{state.query_id}: All regions complete. Finalizing.")
      # Cancel any pending timers since we are finishing explicitly
      Process.cancel_timer(state.timeout_timer)
      finalize_query(state)
      {:stop, :normal, state}
    else
      {:noreply, %{state | active_regions: new_active}}
    end
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(:check_inhibition, state) do
    # If we have a lot of results early on, prune the rest of the search.
    if not state.inhibited? and map_size(state.fired_nodes) > (@inhibit_threshold_count / 2) do
      Logger.debug("Coordinator #{state.query_id}: Fast inhibition triggered.")
      trigger_inhibition(state.query_id, state.active_regions)
      report_query_failure(state, :rapid_inhibition)
      {:noreply, %{state | inhibited?: true}}
    else
      {:noreply, state}
    end
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(:timeout, state) do
    # This handler is now dual-purpose:
    # 1. If 10s passed and NO results -> Report Timeout/Failure.
    # 2. If 5000ms passed and RESULTS exist -> Return Results (Fast Path).

    result_count = map_size(state.fired_nodes)

    if result_count > 0 do
      Logger.info("Coordinator #{state.query_id}: Gathering window closed. Returning #{result_count} results.")
      finalize_query(state)
    else
      Logger.warning("Coordinator #{state.query_id} timed out with 0 results.")
      report_query_failure(state, :timeout)
      finalize_query(state)
    end

    {:stop, :normal, state}
  end

  # Internals

  defp trigger_inhibition(query_id, active_regions) do
    Enum.each(active_regions, fn region_id ->
      Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast(CMS.PubSub, "region:#{region_id}", {:inhibit, query_id})
    end)
  end

  defp report_query_failure(state, reason) do
    LogAppender.append_system_event(:query_failure, %{
      query_id: state.query_id,
      reason: reason,
      origin_pid: inspect(state.origin_pid),
      fired_node_count: map_size(state.fired_nodes),
      active_regions: MapSet.to_list(state.active_regions)
    })
  end

  defp finalize_query(state) do
    # Map top_k IDs back to full data
    results =
      state.top_k_list
      |> Enum.map(fn {_score, id} -> Map.get(state.fired_nodes, id) end)
      |> Enum.reject(&is_nil/1)

    Logger.info("Coordinator #{state.query_id}: Finalizing query with #{length(results)} top results.")

    # Send back to requestor
    send(state.origin_pid, {:query_result, state.query_id, results})
  end

  defp update_top_k(list, new_entry, max_results) do
    list = [new_entry | list]
    |> Enum.sort_by(fn {score, _} -> score end, :desc)
    |> Enum.uniq_by(fn {_, id} -> id end) # CRITICAL FIX: Deduplicate by ID
    
    if max_results && max_results > 0 do
      Enum.take(list, max_results)
    else
      list
    end
  end

  defp via_tuple(query_id) do
    {:via, Registry, {CMS.QueryCoordinatorRegistry, query_id}}
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: activation_engine.ex

defmodule CMS.ActivationEngine do
  use GenServer
  require Logger

  @moduledoc """
  Manages the Global Inhibition Mechanism.

  Implements Gap 16: Global Inhibition Signal / System Congestion.

  It maintains a public ETS table containing the current 'inhibition_factor'.
  NodeActors read this factor to modulate their Spreading Activation gain.
  High Congestion -> Low Factor -> Weaker Pulses -> Less Traffic.
  """

  # Constants
  @table_name :cms_global_inhibition_factor_ets
  @key :factor
  @default_factor 1.0 # 1.0 = Full Energy, 0.0 = Total Inhibition
  @congestion_topic "system_congestion"

  # API

  def start_link(_opts) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, :ok, name: __MODULE__)
  end

  @doc """
  Retrieves the current global inhibition factor (0.0 to 1.0).
  This is a high-frequency read operation optimized via ETS.
  """
  @spec get_global_inhibition_factor() :: float()
  def get_global_inhibition_factor do
    case :ets.lookup(@table_name, @key) do
      [{@key, value}] -> value
      _ -> @default_factor
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Injects a simulated congestion level for testing or external monitoring tools.
  Level: 0.0 (Idle) to 1.0 (Critical Load).
  """
  def set_congestion_level(level) do
    GenServer.cast(__MODULE__, {:congestion_level, level})
  end

  # Callbacks

  @impl true
  def init(:ok) do
    # Create public ETS table for read-heavy access by NodeActors
    :ets.new(@table_name, [:set, :public, :named_table, {:read_concurrency, true}])
    :ets.insert(@table_name, {@key, @default_factor})

    # Subscribe to system stats (e.g., from a LiveWire Agent or Telemetry)
    Phoenix.PubSub.subscribe(CMS.PubSub, @congestion_topic)

    Logger.info("CMS.ActivationEngine started. Global Factor: #{@default_factor}")
    {:ok, %{current_congestion: 0.0}}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:congestion_level, level}, state) do
    new_factor = calculate_inhibition(level)
    update_ets(new_factor)

    Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast(CMS.PubSub, @congestion_topic, {:system_congestion, level})
    {:noreply, %{state | current_congestion: level}}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info({:system_congestion, level}, state) do
    new_factor = calculate_inhibition(level)
    update_ets(new_factor)
    Logger.debug("System Congestion #{level} -> Inhibition Factor #{new_factor}")
    {:noreply, %{state | current_congestion: level}}
  end

  # Internals

  defp calculate_inhibition(congestion) do
    # Linear degradation:
    # Congestion 0.0 -> Factor 1.0
    # Congestion 1.0 -> Factor 0.1 (Never fully 0 to allow critical alerts)

    clamped_congestion = max(0.0, min(1.0, congestion))
    max(0.1, 1.0 - (clamped_congestion * 0.9))
  end

  defp update_ets(factor) do
    :ets.insert(@table_name, {@key, factor})
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: node_supervisor.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.NodeSupervisor do
  use DynamicSupervisor
  require Logger

  @moduledoc """
  Manages the lifecycle of all CMS.NodeActor processes.

  Responsibilities:
  1. Starts NodeActors dynamically upon Ingestion or Cold Boot.
  2. Restarts transient failures.
  3. Provides lookup utilities (PID retrieval).
  """

  def start_link(opts) do
    DynamicSupervisor.start_link(__MODULE__, opts, name: __MODULE__)
  end

  @impl true
  def init(_opts) do
    DynamicSupervisor.init(strategy: :one_for_one)
  end

    @doc """
  Spawns a new NodeActor for the given Node struct.
  """
  @spec start_child(CMS.Node.t()) :: {:ok, pid()} | {:error, any()}
  def start_child(%CMS.Node{} = node) do
    child_spec = %{
      id: node.id,
      start: {CMS.NodeActor, :start_link, [node]},
      restart: :transient
    }
    DynamicSupervisor.start_child(__MODULE__, child_spec)
  end

  def spawn_node(node), do: start_child(node)

  @doc """
  Hydrates a single node from Mnesia and starts its process.
  Used for idempotency checks in Genesis.
  """
  def start_node_from_store(uuid) do
    if pid = get_node_pid(uuid) do
      {:ok, pid}
    else
      case :mnesia.dirty_read(:nodes_table, uuid) do
        [{:nodes_table, uuid, _hash, _p_uuid, head, body, tail, stack, provenance, antenna}] ->
          node = %CMS.Node{
            id: uuid,
            head: head,
            body: %{body | data_ledger: %{body.data_ledger | chrono_stack: stack}},
            tail: tail,
            antenna: antenna,
            provenance: provenance
          }
          case start_child(node) do
            {:ok, pid} = result ->
              # Register with LiveWire and SymbolicIndex
              if body.data_ledger && body.data_ledger.physical_symlink do
                CMS.Services.LiveWire.watch(body.data_ledger.physical_symlink)
                CMS.SymbolicIndex.register_path(body.data_ledger.physical_symlink, pid)
              end
              result
            error -> error
          end

        [{:nodes_table, uuid, _hash, _p_uuid, head, body, tail, stack, provenance}] ->
          # Backward compatibility for old records without antenna field
          node = %CMS.Node{
            id: uuid,
            head: head,
            body: %{body | data_ledger: %{body.data_ledger | chrono_stack: stack}},
            tail: tail,
            antenna: CMS.NodeAntenna.new(body.data_ledger.physical_symlink),
            provenance: provenance
          }
          case start_child(node) do
            {:ok, pid} = result ->
              # Register with LiveWire and SymbolicIndex
              if body.data_ledger && body.data_ledger.physical_symlink do
                CMS.Services.LiveWire.watch(body.data_ledger.physical_symlink)
                CMS.SymbolicIndex.register_path(body.data_ledger.physical_symlink, pid)
              end
              result
            error -> error
          end
          
        _ -> {:error, :not_found}
      end
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Retrieves a full Node snapshot by ID.
  """
  def get_node(node_id) do
    case get_node_pid(node_id) do
      nil -> {:error, :not_found}
      pid -> GenServer.call(pid, :get_state_snapshot)
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Updates a running node's state.
  """
  def update_node(%CMS.Node{id: id} = node) do
    case get_node_pid(id) do
      nil -> {:error, :not_found}
      pid -> GenServer.cast(pid, {:update_node, node})
    end
  end

  def start_directory_agent(path, parent_id, spatial_coords \\ {0.0, 0.0, 0}, width_descriptor \\ "m0", opts \\ []) do
    # Corrected module name
    CMS.Ingestion.DirectoryAgent.start_link({path, parent_id, spatial_coords, width_descriptor, opts})
  end

  @doc """
  Terminates a node and recursively terminates its hierarchical children.
  Scrubs all from Mnesia.
  """
  def terminate_node(node_id) do
    case :mnesia.dirty_read(:nodes_table, node_id) do
      [record] when is_tuple(record) and tuple_size(record) >= 7 ->
        tail = elem(record, 6)
        # Hierarchical recursion: terminate all children
        Enum.each(tail.hierarchical, fn edge ->
          if edge.type == :parent_of do
             terminate_node(edge.target_id)
          end
        end)

        # Scour self
        case get_node_pid(node_id) do
          nil -> :ok
          pid -> DynamicSupervisor.terminate_child(__MODULE__, pid)
        end
        
        :mnesia.dirty_delete(:nodes_table, node_id)
        Logger.info("CMS.NodeSupervisor: Terminated node #{node_id} (and potentially children)")
        :ok

      [] -> 
        {:error, :not_found}
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Retrieves the PID of a running NodeActor by its Content-Addressable ID.
  """
  @spec get_node_pid(String.t()) :: pid() | nil
  def get_node_pid(node_id) do
    case Registry.lookup(CMS.NodeRegistry, node_id) do
      [{pid, _}] -> pid
      [] -> nil
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Returns a list of all active Node PIDs.
  """
  def get_all_active_node_pids do
    Registry.select(CMS.NodeRegistry, [{{:"$1", :"$2", :_}, [{:is_binary, :"$1"}], [:"$2"]}])
  end
end



------------------------------------------------------------
File: node_actor.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.NodeActor do
  use GenServer
  require Logger

  alias CMS.Node

  @moduledoc """
  The Atomic Unit of Cognition (The Quad-Partite Actor).
  """

  defstruct [:node, :hydrated?, :last_activation_times, :metabolic_energy, :region_id, :last_query_context]

  # --- Client API ---

  def fire(uuid) do
    case Registry.lookup(CMS.NodeRegistry, uuid) do
      [{pid, _}] -> GenServer.cast(pid, :fire)
      _ -> {:error, :not_found}
    end
  end

  def pulse(uuid), do: fire(uuid)

  def start_link(%Node{} = node) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, node, name: via_tuple(node.id))
  end

  # --- Callbacks ---

  @impl true
  def init(node) do
    # Trap exits to ensure terminate/2 is called for DriftIndex cleanup
    Process.flag(:trap_exit, true)
    
    # Subscribe to the semantic gather bus and query broadcasts
    Phoenix.PubSub.subscribe(CMS.PubSub, "semantic_gather")

    # Pre-compute region_id to avoid O(N) overhead in Scatter-Gather
    region_id = CMS.SemanticRegion.compute_region_hash(node.head.embedding, node.head.embedding_model_version)
    
    # Register with the lock-free index for Scatter-Gather discovery
    CMS.Core.Logic.DriftIndex.register(node.id, node.head.embedding, region_id)

    # Start metabolic decay loop (every 30 seconds)
    Process.send_after(self(), :metabolic_decay, 30_000)

    # We initialize with full energy (1.0).
    {:ok, %__MODULE__{
      node: node, 
      hydrated?: true, 
      last_activation_times: %{}, 
      metabolic_energy: 1.0,
      region_id: region_id,
      last_query_context: nil
    }}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_call(:get_state_snapshot, _from, state) do
    {:reply, {:ok, state.node}, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_call(:get_drift_info, _from, state) do
    # Return info needed for drift management
    # Uses cached region_id for extreme performance
    {:reply, {state.node.id, state.node.head.embedding, state.node.head.embedding_model_version, state.region_id}, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_call(:get_trust_score, _from, state) do
    # Provenance usually contains trust_score
    score = Map.get(state.node.provenance || %{}, "trust_score", 0.5)
    {:reply, score, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_call(:recompute_edges, _from, state) do
    # 1. Syntactic Edges (Synchronous AST Parsing)
    new_node = CMS.Core.Logic.EdgeGenerator.generate_edges(state.node)
    
    # 2. Trigger Semantic Scatter-Gather (Asynchronous)
    GenServer.cast(self(), :scatter_gather_edges)
    
    # Persist to both Mnesia and epoch logs
    persist_node(new_node)
    
    {:reply, {:ok, new_node}, %{state | node: new_node}}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:update_node, new_node}, state) do
    # Check if this is a Blood node and if we should inhibit persistence
    # We allow persistence only if it's a Bone node OR if it's an authorized pulse.
    # Note: We can check new_node's ChronoStack for the latest cause.
    
    last_cause = List.first(new_node.body.data_ledger.chrono_stack.snapshots || []) 
                |> case do nil -> :unknown; s -> s.cause end

    is_blood = new_node.body.data_ledger.metabolism == :fluid_blood
    is_authorized_pulse = last_cause == :metabolic_pulse
    
    should_persist = not is_blood or is_authorized_pulse
    
    if should_persist do
      # Persistence Strategy: Write-Through Cache
      persist_node(new_node)
    else
      # Just update RAM state, wait for Heartbeat pulse to persist
      Logger.debug("NodeActor: Blood node #{new_node.id} state updated in RAM but inhibited from persistence.")
    end

    {:noreply, %{state | node: new_node}}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast(:scatter_gather_edges, state) do
    # Perform direct scatter-gather to establish semantic edges
    # Now uses the unified EdgeGenerator logic
    new_node = CMS.Core.Logic.EdgeGenerator.generate_semantic_edges(state.node)
    
    if new_node.tail != state.node.tail do
      persist_node(new_node)
      {:noreply, %{state | node: new_node}}
    else
      {:noreply, state}
    end
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:update_region, _new_region}, state) do
    {:noreply, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:feedback, :irrelevant, target_id, amount}, state) do
    Logger.info("Node #{state.node.id} routing Anti-Hebbian feedback for target #{target_id} (-#{amount})")
    
    # Use sharded buffer for penalization as well (negative delta)
    region_id = CMS.SemanticRegion.compute_region_hash(state.node.head.embedding, state.node.head.embedding_model_version)
    CMS.RegionalHebbianBuffer.buffer_update(state.node.id, [{target_id, -amount}], region_id)
    
    {:noreply, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:add_edge, edge}, state) do
    new_node = %{state.node | tail: CMS.NodeTail.add_edge(state.node.tail, edge)}
    persist_node(new_node)
    {:noreply, %{state | node: new_node}}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast(:fire, state) do
    # Primary fire (e.g. from query) starts a NEW trace
    trace = MapSet.new([state.node.id])
    ttl = 1
    
    handle_cast({:fire_with_config, trace, ttl}, state)
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:apply_hebbian_updates, adjustments}, state) do
    new_tail = Enum.reduce(adjustments, state.node.tail, fn {target_id, delta}, acc_tail ->
      update_edge_weight(acc_tail, target_id, delta)
    end)

    if new_tail != state.node.tail do
      Logger.debug("Node #{state.node.id} applied #{length(adjustments)} Hebbian updates in real-time.")
      new_node = %{state.node | tail: new_tail}
      persist_node(new_node)
      {:noreply, %{state | node: new_node}}
    else
      {:noreply, state}
    end
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:fire_with_config, trace, ttl}, state) do
    Logger.info("Node #{state.node.id} is firing with trace (TTL: #{ttl})!")
    
    now = DateTime.utc_now()
    new_node = %{state.node | last_fired: now}
    
    # Global inhibition still applies
    inhibition = CMS.ActivationEngine.get_global_inhibition_factor()
    
    # Metabolic Modulation - Penalties removed: Energy is always 1.0
    energy_modulated_gain = state.node.antenna.gain # * 1.0
    base_strength = energy_modulated_gain * inhibition

    # Pulse edges with trace and decremented TTL
    all_edges = state.node.tail.semantic ++ state.node.tail.syntactic ++ state.node.tail.hierarchical
    
    edge_count = length(all_edges)
    Logger.info("Node #{state.node.id} preparing to pulse #{edge_count} edges (Base Strength: #{:io_lib.format("~.4f", [base_strength])}, Energy: #{:io_lib.format("~.4f", [state.metabolic_energy])}, Gain: #{state.node.antenna.gain}, Inhibit: #{inhibition})")

    Enum.each(all_edges, fn edge ->
      target_strength = base_strength * edge.weight
      
      Logger.debug(">>> Node #{state.node.id} Propagation: Pulsing target #{edge.target_node_id} [Type: #{edge.type}, Weight: #{edge.weight}, Strength: #{:io_lib.format("~.4f", [target_strength])}, TTL: #{ttl}]")

      case Registry.lookup(CMS.NodeRegistry, edge.target_node_id) do
        [{pid, _}] -> send(pid, {:activation, state.node.id, target_strength, edge.type, trace, ttl})
        [] -> Logger.warning("Node #{state.node.id} failed to pulse #{edge.target_node_id}: Node not found in Registry.")
      end
    end)

    CMS.LogAppender.append_node_event(state.node.id, :node_fired, %{strength: base_strength})
    persist_node(new_node)

    # Post-fire state: Always maintain full energy
    {:noreply, %{state | node: new_node, metabolic_energy: 1.0}}
  end

  # --- Internal Helpers moved TO CMS.Core.Logic.CognitiveMath ---

  defp update_edge_weight(tail, target_id, delta) do
    case update_weight_in_list(tail.semantic, target_id, delta) do
      {:ok, new_list} -> %{tail | semantic: new_list}
      :not_found ->
        case update_weight_in_list(tail.syntactic, target_id, delta) do
          {:ok, new_list} -> %{tail | syntactic: new_list}
          :not_found -> tail
        end
    end
  end

  defp update_weight_in_list(list, target_id, delta) do
    case Enum.find_index(list, &(&1.target_node_id == target_id)) do
      nil -> :not_found
      idx ->
        edge = Enum.at(list, idx)
        # REFACTOR: Use Shared Math
        new_weight = CMS.Core.Logic.CognitiveMath.calculate_new_hebbian_weight(edge.weight, delta)
        {:ok, List.replace_at(list, idx, %{edge | weight: new_weight})}
    end
  end


  @impl true
  def handle_info({:query, query_context}, state) do
    if is_directory?(state.node) do
      # Directories are structural, they don't participate in cognitive search
      {:noreply, state}
    else
      # Decentralized Path: Every node evaluates its own relevance
      Logger.info("[QUERY] Node #{String.slice(state.node.id, 0, 8)} evaluating query #{String.slice(query_context.query_id, 0, 8)}...")
    
      case autonomous_query_evaluation(query_context, state) do
        {:fire, relevance} ->
          Logger.info("[FIRE] Node #{String.slice(state.node.id, 0, 8)} FIRES! Relevance: #{:io_lib.format("~.4f", [relevance])} | Path: #{state.node.body.data_ledger.physical_symlink}")
          
          # 1. Report result to the coordinator
          CMS.QueryCoordinator.node_fired(
            query_context.query_id, 
            state.node.id, 
            relevance, 
            state.node
          )
          
          # 2. Trigger active pulse (Spreading Activation)
          GenServer.cast(self(), :fire)
          
          # Always maintain full energy
          {:noreply, %{state | metabolic_energy: 1.0, last_query_context: query_context}}

        {:ignore, relevance, t_adj} ->
          Logger.debug("Node #{String.slice(state.node.id, 0, 8)} ignored: R=#{:io_lib.format("~.4f", [relevance])} < T_adj=#{:io_lib.format("~.4f", [t_adj])}")
          # Still update last_query_context so neighbors can pulse us later
          {:noreply, %{state | last_query_context: query_context}}
      end
    end
  end

  # ... (Semantic Scatter code remains same, handled by EdgeGenerator which uses Nx) ...

  # New activation handler with Trace and TTL loop prevention
  # NEW: Activation handler with Refractory Period Guard
  @impl true
  def handle_info({:activation, source_id, strength, type, trace, ttl}, state) do
    if is_directory?(state.node) do
      # Directories do not participate in Spreading Activation
      {:noreply, state}
    else
      # 1. LOOP PREVENTION: Trace (Stop infinite circles)
      if MapSet.member?(trace, state.node.id) do
        Logger.debug("[TRACE] Node #{String.slice(state.node.id, 0, 8)} already in trace, ignoring activation from #{String.slice(source_id, 0, 8)}")
        {:noreply, state}
      else
        # 2. REFRACTORY GUARD (Stop Multipath Explosion)
        now = DateTime.utc_now()
        last = state.node.last_fired || DateTime.add(now, -3600, :second) 
        diff_ms = DateTime.diff(now, last, :millisecond)

        if diff_ms < 2000 do
          {:noreply, state}
        else
          # 3. TTL LOOP PREVENTION
          if ttl <= 0 do
             Logger.debug("[TTL] Node #{String.slice(state.node.id, 0, 8)} received dead pulse (TTL=0), processing locally only")
             process_activation_locally(source_id, strength, type, state)
          else
             # REFACTOR: Use Shared Math
             boost = CMS.Core.Logic.CognitiveMath.calculate_associative_boost(strength, 1.0, type, :default)

             local_relevance = 
               if state.last_query_context do
                  node_text = state.node.head.label || ""
                  query_text = Map.get(state.last_query_context, :query_text, "")
                  # REFACTOR: Use Shared Math
                  CMS.Core.Logic.CognitiveMath.calculate_relevance_score(state.node.head.embedding, state.last_query_context.query_vector, node_text, query_text)
               else
                  0.0
               end

             total_score = local_relevance + boost
             inhibition = CMS.ActivationEngine.get_global_inhibition_factor()
             # REFACTOR: Use Shared Math
             t_adj = CMS.Core.Logic.CognitiveMath.calculate_adjusted_threshold(get_base_threshold(), inhibition, state.node.head.internal_state)

             if total_score >= t_adj do
                Logger.info("Node #{state.node.id} Firing! (S=#{:io_lib.format("~.4f", [total_score])} > T=#{:io_lib.format("~.4f", [t_adj])})")
                
                new_trace = MapSet.put(trace, state.node.id)
                new_ttl = ttl - 1
                new_node = %{state.node | last_fired: now}
                
                GenServer.cast(self(), {:fire_with_config, new_trace, new_ttl})
                
                if is_semantic_type?(type) do
                  send_hebbian_feedback(source_id, state.node.id)
                  delta = boost * 0.1
                  region_id = CMS.SemanticRegion.compute_region_hash(state.node.head.embedding, state.node.head.embedding_model_version)
                  CMS.RegionalHebbianBuffer.buffer_update(source_id, [{state.node.id, delta}], region_id)
                end

                new_times = Map.put(state.last_activation_times, source_id, System.monotonic_time(:millisecond))
                {:noreply, %{state | node: new_node, last_activation_times: new_times, metabolic_energy: 1.0}}
             else
                {:noreply, state}
             end
          end
        end
      end
    end
  end

  # Backward compatibility: Old activation messages without trace/TTL
  @impl true
  def handle_info({:activation, source_id, strength, type}, state) do
    # Default: empty trace, TTL = 1
    handle_info({:activation, source_id, strength, type, MapSet.new(), 1}, state)
  end

  # Backwards compatibility for untagged activations
  @impl true
  def handle_info({:activation, source_id, strength}, state) do
    handle_info({:activation, source_id, strength, :semantic}, state)
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info({:pulse, source_id, strength, type}, state) do
    handle_info({:activation, source_id, strength, type}, state)
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info({:pulse, source_id, strength}, state) do
    handle_info({:activation, source_id, strength, :semantic}, state)
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(:delayed_scatter_gather, state) do
    # Triggered after genesis settles - now safe to do scatter-gather
    GenServer.cast(self(), :scatter_gather_edges)
    {:noreply, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info({:hebbian_reinforce, target_id, amount}, state) do
    Logger.debug("Node #{state.node.id} buffering Hebbian reinforcement for #{target_id} (+#{amount})")
    
    # Route to sharded buffer for aggregation
    region_id = CMS.SemanticRegion.compute_region_hash(state.node.head.embedding, state.node.head.embedding_model_version)
    CMS.RegionalHebbianBuffer.buffer_update(state.node.id, [{target_id, amount}], region_id)
    
    {:noreply, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(:metabolic_decay, state) do
    # User Request: "All nodes must be highly active at all times"
    # Instead of decaying, we maintain max energy (1.0)
    new_energy = 1.0
    Process.send_after(self(), :metabolic_decay, 600_000)
    {:noreply, %{state | metabolic_energy: new_energy}}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(_msg, state) do
    {:noreply, state}
  end

  # --- Private ---

  defp autonomous_query_evaluation(ctx, state) do
    # 1. Relevance Score ($R$) - Refactored
    node_text = state.node.head.label || ""
    query_text = Map.get(ctx, :query_text, "")
    
    relevance = CMS.Core.Logic.CognitiveMath.calculate_relevance_score(state.node.head.embedding, ctx.query_vector, node_text, query_text)

    # 2. Adjusted Threshold ($T_{adj}$) - Refactored
    base_threshold = get_base_threshold()
    inhibition = CMS.ActivationEngine.get_global_inhibition_factor()
    t_adj = CMS.Core.Logic.CognitiveMath.calculate_adjusted_threshold(base_threshold, inhibition, state.node.head.internal_state)

    if relevance >= t_adj do
      {:fire, relevance}
    else
      {:ignore, relevance, t_adj}
    end
  end

  # --- FORMULAS (DELETED - Moved to Module) ---

  defp persist_node(new_node) do
    try do
      if :mnesia.system_info(:is_running) == :yes and :nodes_table in :mnesia.system_info(:tables) do
        path_hash = UUID.uuid5(:oid, new_node.body.data_ledger.physical_symlink)
        parent_uuid = 
          case Enum.find(new_node.tail.hierarchical, fn e -> e.type == :child_of end) do
            %CMS.Edge{target_node_id: pid} -> pid
            nil -> nil
          end
        stack = new_node.body.data_ledger.chrono_stack
        
        # CRITICAL FIX: Persist to both Mnesia (cache) AND Epoch Logs (source of truth)
        :mnesia.dirty_write({:nodes_table, new_node.id, path_hash, parent_uuid, new_node.head, new_node.body, new_node.tail, stack, new_node.provenance, new_node.antenna})
        
        # Append to epoch log for rehydration capability
        CMS.LogAppender.append_node_event(new_node.id, :node_updated, new_node)
      end
    rescue
      _ -> :ok
    end
  end

  defp send_hebbian_feedback(origin_id, my_id) do
    case Registry.lookup(CMS.NodeRegistry, origin_id) do
       [{pid, _}] -> send(pid, {:hebbian_reinforce, my_id, 0.01})
       _ -> :ok
    end
  end

  # Helper: Process activation locally without propagating (for dead pulses)
  defp process_activation_locally(source_id, strength, type, state) do
    # Calculate boost but don't propagate
    # REFACTOR: Use Shared Math
    boost = CMS.Core.Logic.CognitiveMath.calculate_associative_boost(strength, 1.0, type, :default)
    
    # Local relevance check
    local_relevance = 
      if state.last_query_context do
         node_text = state.node.head.label || ""
         query_text = Map.get(state.last_query_context, :query_text, "")
         # REFACTOR: Use Shared Math
         CMS.Core.Logic.CognitiveMath.calculate_relevance_score(state.node.head.embedding, state.last_query_context.query_vector, node_text, query_text)
      else
         0.0
      end

    total_score = local_relevance + boost

    # Calculate dynamic threshold
    inhibition = CMS.ActivationEngine.get_global_inhibition_factor()
    # REFACTOR: Use Shared Math
    t_adj = CMS.Core.Logic.CognitiveMath.calculate_adjusted_threshold(get_base_threshold(), inhibition, state.node.head.internal_state)

    # If score is high enough, just report to coordinator but don't fire/propagate
    if total_score >= t_adj and state.last_query_context do
      Logger.info("Node #{state.node.id} LOCAL activation (dead pulse): S_total=#{:io_lib.format("~.4f", [total_score])} >= T_adj=#{:io_lib.format("~.4f", [t_adj])}")
      
      # Report to coordinator if in query mode
      CMS.QueryCoordinator.node_fired(
        state.last_query_context.query_id, 
        state.node.id, 
        total_score, 
        state.node
      )
      
      # Hebbian feedback but no propagation
      if is_semantic_type?(type) do
        send_hebbian_feedback(source_id, state.node.id)
      end
      
      new_times = Map.put(state.last_activation_times, source_id, System.monotonic_time(:millisecond))
      {:noreply, %{state | last_activation_times: new_times, metabolic_energy: 1.0}}
    else
      Logger.debug("Node #{state.node.id} IGNORED dead pulse: S_total=#{:io_lib.format("~.4f", [total_score])} < T_adj=#{:io_lib.format("~.4f", [t_adj])}")
      {:noreply, state}
    end
  end



  defp is_semantic_type?(type) do
    type in [:relates_to, :semantic, :contradicts, :supersedes, :superseded_by]
  end

  defp via_tuple(id), do: {:via, Registry, {CMS.NodeRegistry, id}}

  @impl true
  def terminate(_reason, state) do
    CMS.Core.Logic.DriftIndex.unregister(state.node.id)
    :ok
  end

  # Helper to fetch threshold dynamically (Defaults to 0.7 if not set)
  defp get_base_threshold do
    Application.get_env(:acn_cms, :node_base_threshold, 0.25)
  end

  defp is_directory?(node) do
    mime = node.body.data_ledger.mime_definition
    type = Map.get(node.provenance || %{}, "type")
    
    mime == "inode/directory" or type == "directory"
  end
end

------------------------------------------------------------
File: regional_hebbian_buffer.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.RegionalHebbianBuffer do
  use GenServer
  require Logger

  @moduledoc """
  A sharded buffer for accumulating Hebbian link weight adjustments.

  Implements Gap 5: Sharded Hebbian Buffer.
  Uses ETS for high-performance in-memory aggregation before flushing to the immutable log.
  """

  # Configuration
  @flush_interval 5_000 # Flush every 5 seconds
  @max_updates_before_flush 1_000 # Or if we hit 1000 pending updates

  defstruct [:shard_id, :ets_table, :update_count]

  # Client API

  def start_link(shard_id) do
    name = via_tuple(shard_id)
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, shard_id, name: name)
  end

  @doc """
  Routes an update to the correct shard based on the node's region ID.

  adjustments: List of {target_node_id, weight_delta}
  e.g., [{"UUID-B", 0.05}, {"UUID-C", -0.01}]
  """
  @spec buffer_update(String.t(), list({String.t(), float()}), integer()) :: :ok
  def buffer_update(source_node_id, adjustments, semantic_region_id) do
    # Route to shard based on region ID (assuming 32 shards)
    shard_id = rem(semantic_region_id, 32)
    GenServer.cast(via_tuple(shard_id), {:buffer, source_node_id, adjustments})
  end

  # Server Callbacks

  @impl true
  def init(shard_id) do
    # Create a named public ETS table for this shard
    table_name = :"hebbian_buffer_#{shard_id}"
    ets_table = :ets.new(table_name, [:set, :named_table, :public])

    Logger.info("Hebbian Shard #{shard_id} started. ETS: #{inspect(table_name)}")

    # Schedule flush
    Process.send_after(self(), :flush, @flush_interval)

    {:ok, %__MODULE__{
      shard_id: shard_id,
      ets_table: ets_table,
      update_count: 0
    }}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:buffer, source_id, adjustments}, state) do
    # Write to ETS.
    # Key: {source_id, target_id}
    # Value: Accumulated Delta

    Enum.each(adjustments, fn {target_id, delta} ->
      key = {source_id, target_id}

      # update_counter is atomic. If key doesn't exist, create it with default.
      # ETS update_counter usually works with integers. For floats, we must use
      # :ets.update_element or read/write inside the GenServer serialization.
      # Since we are inside handle_cast, we are serialized, so read/write is safe here.

      case :ets.lookup(state.ets_table, key) do
        [{^key, current_delta}] ->
          :ets.insert(state.ets_table, {key, current_delta + delta})
        [] ->
          :ets.insert(state.ets_table, {key, delta})
      end
    end)

    new_count = state.update_count + length(adjustments)

    if new_count >= @max_updates_before_flush do
      do_flush(state.ets_table)
      {:noreply, %{state | update_count: 0}}
    else
      {:noreply, %{state | update_count: new_count}}
    end
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(:flush, state) do
    do_flush(state.ets_table)
    Process.send_after(self(), :flush, @flush_interval)
    {:noreply, %{state | update_count: 0}}
  end

  # Internals

  defp do_flush(table) do
    # 1. Drain table
    # This is a critical section. We use tab2list then delete_all.
    # In a ultra-high concurrency scenario, we might swap tables, but this suffices for now.
    entries = :ets.tab2list(table)
    :ets.delete_all_objects(table)

    unless Enum.empty?(entries) do
      Logger.debug("Flushing #{length(entries)} Hebbian updates to LogAppender.")

      # 2. Group by Source Node for efficient logging
      # Transform {{source, target}, delta} -> {source, [{target, delta}]}
      grouped_updates =
        entries
        |> Enum.group_by(
          fn {{source, _target}, _delta} -> source end,
          fn {{_source, target}, delta} -> [target, delta] end
        )

      # 3. Send to LogAppender (Step 7)
      CMS.LogAppender.append_hebbian_updates(grouped_updates)

      # 4. FEEDBACK LOOP: Apply updates to active NodeActors in real-time
      Enum.each(grouped_updates, fn {source_id, updates} ->
        # updates is a list of [target_id, delta]
        formatted_updates = Enum.map(updates, fn [target, delta] -> {target, delta} end)
        
        case Registry.lookup(CMS.NodeRegistry, source_id) do
          [{pid, _}] -> 
            GenServer.cast(pid, {:apply_hebbian_updates, formatted_updates})
          _ -> :ok
        end
      end)

      # --- NEW: Broadcast for Real-time Monitoring ---
      # This proves Neuroplasticity is happening
      total_updates = length(entries)
      sample = Enum.at(grouped_updates, 0)
      Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast(CMS.PubSub, "global:signals",
        {:hebbian_learning, %{update_count: total_updates, sample: sample}}
      )
    end
  end

  defp via_tuple(shard_id) do
    {:global, :"cms_hebbian_shard_#{shard_id}"}
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: hebbian_buffer_supervisor.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.HebbianBufferSupervisor do
  use Supervisor

  @shard_count 32

  def start_link(opts) do
    Supervisor.start_link(__MODULE__, opts, name: __MODULE__)
  end

  @impl true
  def init(_opts) do
    children =
      for i <- 0..(@shard_count - 1) do
        %{
          id: :"hebbian_shard_#{i}",
          start: {CMS.RegionalHebbianBuffer, :start_link, [i]},
          restart: :permanent
        }
      end

    Supervisor.init(children, strategy: :one_for_one)
  end
end

defmodule CMS.Persistence.Identity do
  @moduledoc """
  Manages the identity.lock file, which serves as the "Signature" of the last machine
  that successfully wrote to the data directory.
  
  Used to detect when data has been moved to a new host (Alien Data), triggering rehydration.
  """
  
  @lock_file_name "identity.lock"
  
  defstruct [:node_name, :architecture, :version, :data_dir_path]
  
  @type t :: %__MODULE__{
    node_name: atom(),
    architecture: binary(),
    version: binary(),
    data_dir_path: binary()
  }

  @doc """
  Checks the lock file against the current system identity.
  Returns:
  - :ok -> Identity matches (Fast Boot).
  - :mismatch -> Identity differs (Rehydrate).
  - :missing -> No lock file (Fresh Init).
  """
  def check_lock do
    path = lock_file_path()
    
    if File.exists?(path) do
      case File.read(path) do
        {:ok, binary} ->
           try do
             stored_identity = :erlang.binary_to_term(binary)
             if identities_match?(stored_identity, current_identity()) do
               :ok
             else
               :mismatch
             end
           rescue
             _ -> :mismatch # Corrupt lock file -> treated as mismatch
           end
        {:error, _} -> :missing
      end
    else
      :missing
    end
  end
  
  @doc """
  Writes the current identity to the lock file. All Rehydration/Boot processes must call this
  once they are ready to own the data.
  """
  def sign_lock do
    path = lock_file_path()
    identity = current_identity()
    binary = :erlang.term_to_binary(identity)
    File.write!(path, binary)
    :ok
  end
  
  def delete_lock do
    File.rm(lock_file_path())
  end

  def current_identity do
    %__MODULE__{
      node_name: Node.self(),
      architecture: to_string(:erlang.system_info(:system_architecture)),
      version: Application.spec(:acn_cms, :vsn) |> to_string(),
      # We also track the absolute path. If the folder moves on the SAME machine,
      # Mnesia might still be confused if it uses absolute paths internally.
      data_dir_path: Path.absname(Application.get_env(:mnesia, :dir) || "priv/data/mnesia_store")
    }
  end
  
  defp identities_match?(stored, current) do
    # We strictly check Architecture and Node Name (Mnesia requirement).
    # We can be lenient on Version (Migration logic handles that elsewhere).
    stored.node_name == current.node_name and 
    stored.architecture == current.architecture
  end
  
  defp lock_file_path do
    # Lock file sits at the root of the data store parent (Rehydrator philosophy)
    # If mnesia_dir is "priv/data/mnesia_store", lock is at "priv/data/identity.lock"
    mnesia_dir = Application.get_env(:mnesia, :dir) || "priv/data/mnesia_store"
    root_data_dir = Path.dirname(mnesia_dir)
    Path.join(root_data_dir, @lock_file_name)
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: bootloader.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.Persistence.Bootloader do
  require Logger
  alias CMS.Persistence.Identity
  alias CMS.Recovery.Rehydrator

  # Table Definitions
  @table_name :epoch_log_index
  @nodes_table :nodes_table
  @crems_nodes_table :crems_nodes_table
  @vector_index :vector_index

  def run do
    Logger.info("CMS Bootloader: Checking Persistence Integrity...")
    
    # Pre-flight: Ensure Schema and Tables exist
    ensure_schema_and_tables()

    case Identity.check_lock() do
      :ok ->
        if schema_damaged?() do
          Logger.warning("CMS Bootloader: Identity Verified but Mnesia Schema is DAMAGED or INCOMPATIBLE.")
          rehydrate_and_sign()
        else
          Logger.info("CMS Bootloader: Identity Verified. Fast Boot initiated.")
        end
        :ok
        
      :mismatch ->
        Logger.warning("CMS Bootloader: ALIEN DATA DETECTED! (Architecture/Node Mismatch).")
        rehydrate_and_sign()
        :ok
        
      :missing ->
        if data_exists?() do
          Logger.warning("CMS Bootloader: Data directory exists but identity.lock is missing.")
          rehydrate_and_sign()
        else
          Logger.info("CMS Bootloader: No existing data found. Assuming Fresh Init.")
          Identity.sign_lock()
        end
        :ok
    end
  end

  defp rehydrate_and_sign do
    Logger.warning("CMS Bootloader: Initiating Emergency REHYDRATION.")
    Rehydrator.rehydrate()
    Identity.sign_lock()
  end

  defp schema_damaged? do
    mnesia_dir = Application.get_env(:mnesia, :dir) || "priv/data/mnesia_store"
    schema_file = Path.join(mnesia_dir, "schema.DAT")
    
    if File.exists?(schema_file) do
       # Paranoid check: Can we open this schema as a DETS file?
       # Mnesia schema is internally a DETS file. If this returns :badarg or :error, 
       # Mnesia will crash upon start.
       case :dets.open_file(:boot_probe, [{:file, String.to_charlist(schema_file)}, {:repair, false}, {:access, :read}]) do
         {:ok, _} -> 
            :dets.close(:boot_probe)
            false
         _ -> true
       end
    else
      false
    end
  end

  defp data_exists? do
    mnesia_dir = Application.get_env(:mnesia, :dir) || "priv/data/mnesia_store"
    case File.ls(mnesia_dir) do
      {:ok, files} when files != [] -> true
      _ -> false
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Ensures Mnesia schema and tables exist. 
  Safe to call multiple times (idempotent).
  """
  def ensure_schema_and_tables do
    Logger.info("CMS Bootloader: Ensuring Schema and Tables...")
    
    # 1. Get Directory from Config
    raw_dir = Application.get_env(:mnesia, :dir) || "priv/data/mnesia_store"
    mnesia_dir = if is_binary(raw_dir), do: String.to_charlist(raw_dir), else: raw_dir

    # 2. Ensure directory
    File.mkdir_p!(to_string(mnesia_dir))
    Application.put_env(:mnesia, :dir, mnesia_dir)
    
    nodes = [Node.self()]

    # 3. Stop Mnesia to perform cleanup/creation if needed
    :mnesia.stop()

    # 4. Create Schema
    case :mnesia.create_schema(nodes) do
      :ok -> Logger.info("Mnesia schema created.")
      {:error, {_, {:already_exists, _}}} -> :ok
      error -> Logger.error("Mnesia create_schema failed: #{inspect(error)}")
    end

    # 5. Start Mnesia (temporarily to create tables)
    :mnesia.start()

    # 6. Create Tables
    create_table_if_missing(@table_name, [attributes: [:id, :start_time, :end_time, :file_path], disc_copies: nodes])
    create_table_if_missing(@nodes_table, [attributes: [:uuid, :path_hash, :parent_uuid, :head_data, :body_data, :tail_data, :chrono_stack, :provenance, :antenna_data], disc_copies: nodes])
    create_table_if_missing(@crems_nodes_table, [attributes: [:id, :head, :body, :tail, :antenna], ram_copies: nodes])
    create_table_if_missing(@vector_index, [attributes: [:id, :vector], disc_copies: nodes, type: :ordered_set])

    # 7. Stop Mnesia so Application can start it cleanly
    :mnesia.stop()
  end

  defp create_table_if_missing(name, opts) do
    case :mnesia.create_table(name, opts) do
      {:atomic, :ok} -> Logger.info("Mnesia table #{name} created.")
      {:aborted, {:already_exists, _}} -> :ok
      other -> Logger.error("Mnesia create_table #{name} failed: #{inspect(other)}")
    end
  end
end

------------------------------------------------------------
File: ollama_bridge.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.Infrastructure.OllamaBridge do
  @moduledoc """
  The Ollama Bridge: Manages communication with the local Ollama instance.
  Implements token bucket rate limiting to prevent GPU saturation.
  """

  use GenServer
  require Logger

  @base_url "http://localhost:11434"
  @inference_limit 5
  @embedding_limit 20

  defstruct [:inference_tokens, :embedding_tokens, :inference_queue, :embedding_queue]

  # --- Client API ---

  def start_link(_) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, nil, name: __MODULE__)
  end

  @doc """
  Summarizes file content using the specified LLM.
  Retries on bad responses until we get something usable.
  Pass file_path for better context in prompts.
  """
  def summarize(content, model \\ "qwen3:0.6b", file_path \\ nil, retries \\ 3) do
    if Mix.env() == :test do
      {:ok, %{"summary" => "Test Summary", "concepts" => ["Term1", "Term2", "Term3"]}}
    else
      GenServer.call(__MODULE__, {:request, :inference, fn ->
        attempt_summarize(content, model, retries, file_path)
      end}, :infinity)
    end
  end

  defp attempt_summarize(content, model, retries_left, file_context) do
    # Extract filename and extension for context
    {filename, extension} = if file_context do
      {Path.basename(file_context), Path.extname(file_context)}
    else
      {"unknown", ""}
    end
    
    prompt = """
    You are analyzing source code file: #{filename}
    File type: #{extension}
    
    READ THIS CODE CAREFULLY:
    #{String.slice(content, 0, 3000)}
    
    TASK: Write a CLEAR, SPECIFIC summary (NOT dots or placeholders).
    
    RULES:
    1. Summary MUST be a complete sentence describing what this code does
    2. DO NOT use "..." or ". . ." or ellipsis in your summary
    3. Be specific about the actual functionality
    4. Extract 3 concrete technical concepts from the code
    
    OUTPUT FORMAT (strict JSON):
    {
      "summary": "A clear description of what #{filename} does",
      "concepts": ["Concept1", "Concept2", "Concept3"]
    }
    
    BEGIN YOUR ANALYSIS NOW:
    """
    
    case retry_post("#{@base_url}/api/generate", %{
      model: model,
      prompt: prompt,
      format: "json",
      stream: false,
      temperature: 0.7,
      keep_alive: -1
    }, 120_000) do
      {:ok, %Req.Response{status: 200, body: %{"response" => resp_json}}} ->
        Logger.info("Ollama Response [#{model}]: #{resp_json}")
        
        cleaned_json = extract_json(resp_json)
        case Jason.decode(cleaned_json) do
          {:ok, %{"summary" => summary} = decoded} when is_binary(summary) ->
            # Check if response is actually useful
            if String.length(summary) >= 15 and not String.match?(summary, ~r/^[\.\s]+$/) and not String.contains?(summary, "...") do
              {:ok, decoded}
            else
              # Bad response - retry if we have attempts left
              if retries_left > 0 do
                Logger.warning("Ollama returned minimal/ellipsis response: '#{summary}'. Retrying... (#{retries_left} left)")
                :timer.sleep(1500)
                attempt_summarize(content, model, retries_left - 1, file_context)
              else
                Logger.error("Ollama exhausted retries, last response: '#{summary}'")
                {:error, "Poor quality response after all retries"}
              end
            end
          {:error, _} -> 
            if retries_left > 0 do
              Logger.warning("Invalid JSON from Ollama. Retrying... (#{retries_left} left)")
              :timer.sleep(1500)
              attempt_summarize(content, model, retries_left - 1, file_context)
            else
              {:error, "Invalid JSON after all retries"}
            end
          _ ->
            if retries_left > 0 do
              Logger.warning("Unexpected Ollama response format. Retrying... (#{retries_left} left)")
              :timer.sleep(1500)
              attempt_summarize(content, model, retries_left - 1, file_context)
            else
              {:error, "Unexpected response format after all retries"}
            end
        end
      {:error, reason} -> {:error, reason}
      {:ok, other} -> {:error, "Unexpected HTTP response: #{inspect(other)}"}
    end
  end

  # ... extract_json exists here ...
  defp extract_json(str) do
    # 1. Try to find content between ```json and ```
    case Regex.run(~r/```json\s*(.*?)\s*```/s, str) do
      [_, json] -> json
      nil ->
        # 2. Try to find content between ``` and ```
        case Regex.run(~r/```\s*(.*?)\s*```/s, str) do
          [_, json] -> json
          nil ->
            # 3. Try to find the first { and last }
            case Regex.run(~r/(\{.*\})/s, str) do
              [_, json] -> json
              nil -> str # Fallback to original string
            end
        end
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Generates embedding for the given text.
  """
  def embed(text, model \\ "qwen3:0.6b") do
    if Mix.env() == :test do
       {:ok, List.duplicate(0.1, 384)}
    else
      GenServer.call(__MODULE__, {:request, :embedding, fn ->
        case retry_post("#{@base_url}/api/embeddings", %{
          model: model,
          prompt: text
        }, 30_000) do
          {:ok, %Req.Response{status: 200, body: %{"embedding" => vec}}} ->
            {:ok, vec}
          {:error, reason} -> {:error, reason}
          {:ok, other} -> {:error, "Unexpected response: #{inspect(other)}"}
        end
      end}, :infinity)
    end
  end

  defp retry_post(url, body, timeout, retries \\ 3) do
    case Req.post(url, json: body, receive_timeout: timeout) do
      {:ok, response} -> {:ok, response}
      {:error, %Mint.TransportError{reason: :timeout}} when retries > 0 ->
        Logger.warn("Ollama Bridge: Connection Timeout. Retrying... (#{retries} left)")
        :timer.sleep(2000)
        retry_post(url, body, timeout, retries - 1)
      {:error, reason} -> {:error, reason}
    end
  end

  # --- Server Callbacks ---

  @impl true
  def init(_) do
    {:ok, %__MODULE__{
      inference_tokens: @inference_limit,
      embedding_tokens: @embedding_limit,
      inference_queue: :queue.new(),
      embedding_queue: :queue.new()
    }}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_call({:request, type, func}, from, state) do
    tokens = get_tokens(state, type)
    
    if tokens > 0 do
      # Execute immediately
      new_state = consume_token(state, type)
      execute_request(type, func, from)
      {:noreply, new_state}
    else
      # Queue request
      new_queue = :queue.in({func, from}, get_queue(state, type))
      new_state = update_queue(state, type, new_queue)
      {:noreply, new_state}
    end
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_cast({:release, type}, state) do
    # 1. Replenish Token
    # state = replenish_token(state, type) # Wait, logic is: release means one finished.
    # So we take next from queue if available.
    
    queue = get_queue(state, type)
    
    case :queue.out(queue) do
      {{:value, {func, from}}, remaining_queue} ->
         # Consume the token effectively by NOT incrementing count (we just passed it to the next guy)
         execute_request(type, func, from)
         {:noreply, update_queue(state, type, remaining_queue)}
         
      {:empty, _} ->
         # No one waiting, restore the token count
         {:noreply, replenish_token(state, type)}
    end
  end
  
  defp execute_request(type, func, from) do
     Task.start(fn ->
        result = func.()
        GenServer.cast(__MODULE__, {:release, type})
        GenServer.reply(from, result)
      end)
  end

  defp get_tokens(state, :inference), do: state.inference_tokens
  defp get_tokens(state, :embedding), do: state.embedding_tokens
  
  defp get_queue(state, :inference), do: state.inference_queue
  defp get_queue(state, :embedding), do: state.embedding_queue
  
  defp consume_token(state, :inference), do: %{state | inference_tokens: state.inference_tokens - 1}
  defp consume_token(state, :embedding), do: %{state | embedding_tokens: state.embedding_tokens - 1}

  defp replenish_token(state, :inference), do: %{state | inference_tokens: state.inference_tokens + 1}
  defp replenish_token(state, :embedding), do: %{state | embedding_tokens: state.embedding_tokens + 1}
  
  defp update_queue(state, :inference, q), do: %{state | inference_queue: q}
  defp update_queue(state, :embedding, q), do: %{state | embedding_queue: q}
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: instance_guard.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.Infrastructure.InstanceGuard do
  @moduledoc """
  Ensures only one ACMS instance accesses the Data Directory at a time.
  Implements the "Git-Lock" mechanism using a `running.pid` file.
  """
  use GenServer
  require Logger

  @pid_filename "running.pid"

  def start_link(_opts) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, nil, name: __MODULE__)
  end

  @doc """
  Synchronously checks and acquires the lock.
  Must be called before Mnesia starts.
  """
  def guard_entry do
    data_dir = Application.get_env(:acn_cms, :data_root)
    # Ensure dir exists (Runtime.exs does it, but double check?)
    case acquire_lock(data_dir) do
      :ok -> :ok
      {:error, reason} ->
        Logger.critical("[InstanceGuard] Startup Aborted: #{reason}")
        # Build in a small delay to ensure log flushes if we are about to crash hard?
        :timer.sleep(100)
        raise "INSTANCE GUARD: #{reason}"
    end
  end

  @impl true
  def init(_) do
    Process.flag(:trap_exit, true)
    data_dir = Application.get_env(:acn_cms, :data_root)

    case acquire_lock(data_dir) do
      :ok ->
        Logger.info("[InstanceGuard] Lock acquired on #{data_dir}")
        {:ok, %{data_dir: data_dir}}

      {:error, reason} ->
        Logger.critical("[InstanceGuard] Failed to acquire lock: #{reason}")
        {:stop, reason}
    end
  end

  @impl true
  def terminate(_reason, state) do
    release_lock(state.data_dir)
  end

  defp acquire_lock(dir) do
    pid_path = Path.join(dir, @pid_filename)
    my_pid = System.pid()

    if File.exists?(pid_path) do
      case File.read(pid_path) do
        {:ok, content} ->
           check_existing_lock(String.trim(content), pid_path, my_pid)
        {:error, _} ->
           {:error, :unreadable_lock_file}
      end
    else
      write_lock(pid_path, my_pid)
    end
  end

  defp check_existing_lock(old_pid_str, pid_path, my_pid) do
    case Integer.parse(old_pid_str) do
      {old_pid_int, _} ->
        # Check if it's us (Self-Healing/Re-entrant)
        my_pid_int = String.to_integer(my_pid)

        cond do
          old_pid_int == my_pid_int ->
             # It is us. We already hold the lock (from guard_entry).
             :ok

          process_alive?(old_pid_int) ->
             {:error, "Directory locked by running instance (PID #{old_pid_int})"}

          true ->
             # Dead process
             Logger.warning("[InstanceGuard] Found stale lock from dead PID #{old_pid_int}. Cleaning up.")
             write_lock(pid_path, my_pid)
        end
      :error ->
        Logger.warning("[InstanceGuard] Found invalid PID file content. Overwriting.")
        write_lock(pid_path, my_pid)
    end
  end

  defp write_lock(path, pid) do
    File.write(path, to_string(pid))
  end

  defp release_lock(dir) do
    pid_path = Path.join(dir, @pid_filename)
    # Only delete if it holds OUR pid (race condition safety? minimal locally)
    # For simplicity, we just delete it as we are the guard.
    File.rm(pid_path)
    Logger.info("[InstanceGuard] Lock released.")
  end

  # Cross-platform check is tricky in Elixir standard lib for OS PIDs.
  # System.cmd("ps", ...) or similar?
  # Erlang doesn't have a direct `os:process_exists` for arbitrary OS PIDs easily.
  # But we can try checking via system commands.
  # User is on Windows (WSL) -> essentially Linux.
  defp process_alive?(os_pid) do
    # This is a naive check that works on POSIX.
    # kill -0 <pid> returns 0 if process exists, 1 if not.
    case System.cmd("kill", ["-0", to_string(os_pid)], stderr_to_stdout: true) do
      {_, 0} -> true
      _ -> false
    end
  end
end
