defmodule CMS.Core.Logic.EdgeGenerator do
  @moduledoc """
  The Edge Generator: Forges the links of the Connectome.
  """

  require Logger
  alias CMS.{Edge, NodeTail}

  @doc """
  Generates all edges for a single node.
  """
  def generate_edges(node) do
    # Phase 4 Upgrade: Connectome Scanning
    # We apply this after initial generation phases
    structural_edges = CMS.Core.Logic.ConnectomeScanner.scan_skeleton(node)
    immune_edges = CMS.Core.Logic.ConnectomeScanner.scan_immune(node)
    nervous_edges = CMS.Core.Logic.ConnectomeScanner.scan_nervous(node)
    
    # Merge all edges manually from the previous pipe approach
    # We need to construct the node with valid tails from the previous steps first or just merge results?
    # The previous code piped functions which return 'node'. 
    # But generate_syntactic_edges returns 'node' with updated tail.
    # To correctly integrate, we chain the calls and then inspect/update the final node.
    
    node = node
    |> generate_hierarchical_edges()
    |> generate_syntactic_edges()
    |> generate_semantic_edges()
    
    # Now append Phase 4 edges
    new_tail = node.tail
    
    new_tail = Enum.reduce(structural_edges ++ nervous_edges, new_tail, fn edge, acc ->
      CMS.NodeTail.add_edge(acc, edge)
    end)
    
    # Immune edges might be semantic or own category. Spec calls them Authority.
    # We map them to semantic for generic storage or assume add_edge handles checks.
    # Let's map OWNED_BY/EXECUTABLE_BY to semantic or add support in NodeTail (which defaults generic to syntactic).
    # NodeTail.add_edge handles unknowns by defaulting to syntactic. Spec says Authority Edges.
    # Let's effectively treat them as structural (rigid) or semantic (social)?
    # "Authority Edges describe defense mechanism".
    # Implementation Plan: just add them via standard add_edge.
    
    new_tail = Enum.reduce(immune_edges, new_tail, fn edge, acc ->
      CMS.NodeTail.add_edge(acc, edge)
    end)
    
    %{node | tail: new_tail}
  end

  @doc """
  Generates hierarchical edges (PARENT_OF/CHILD_OF) based on file path.
  Note: This is usually handled during Genesis, but can be re-run.
  """
  def generate_hierarchical_edges(node) do
    # Implementation depends on knowing the directory structure
    # For now, we'll assume the Genesis engine handles this.
    node
  end

  @doc """
  Generates syntactic edges by parsing the file content for imports/dependencies.
  """
  def generate_syntactic_edges(node) do
    if node.body.mode == :deep do
      # Decompress content for analysis
      content = CMS.NodeBody.decompress(node.body) || ""
      path = node.body.data_ledger.physical_symlink
      
      imports = find_imports(content, path)
      
      Enum.reduce(imports, node, fn import_name, acc ->
        # Find node by name
        case CMS.VectorRouter.find_by_name(import_name) do
          {:ok, target_id} ->
            edge = Edge.new(target_id, :imports, 1.0)
            %{acc | tail: NodeTail.add_edge(acc.tail, edge)}
          _ -> 
            # Not found in system. Check if it's a file path to generate a Ghost Node.
            # (Simple heuristic: if it looks like a path or specific system lib)
            if String.contains?(import_name, "/") do
               # Check Bounded Circle
               # NOTE: We need circle_root from node.provenance or opts, but EdgeGenerator 
               # usually runs post-ingest. For Phase 2, we assume we might have circle_root in provenance
               # or just assume specific system paths are external.
               
               # Let's assume for this specific requirement, if we see a path-like import 
               # that isn't in Mnesia, we treat it as external.
               
               {ghost_uuid, _ghost_node, mn_record} = CMS.Ingestion.FractalAperture.create_ghost_node(import_name, node.id)
               
               # Write Ghost to Mnesia (if not exists)
               # Use a safe write or check existence
               case :mnesia.dirty_read(:nodes_table, ghost_uuid) do
                 [] -> :mnesia.dirty_write(mn_record)
                 _ -> :ok
               end
               
               edge = Edge.new(ghost_uuid, :anchored_to, 1.0)
               %{acc | tail: NodeTail.add_edge(acc.tail, edge)}
            else
               acc
            end
        end
      end)
    else
      node
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Generates semantic edges using the "Hebbian Priming" Scatter-Gather protocol.
  Queries all active NodeActors in parallel to find semantic neighbors.
  """
  def generate_semantic_edges(node) do
    # Configuration matches IngestionEngine Spec
    # Differentiate thresholds for CDMS/CREMS
    is_crems = crems_node?(node.id)
    conflict_threshold = if is_crems, do: 0.95, else: 0.8
    
    my_id = node.id

    # Get raw candidates with scores using Scatter-Gather
    candidates = prime_candidates(node.head.embedding, node.id)
    
    Enum.reduce(candidates, node, fn {target_id, score}, acc ->
      if score >= conflict_threshold and not is_crems do
        # --- SPECIAL CASE: DIALECTICAL MERGE ---
        # 1. Add Contradicts Link to Peer
        acc = add_edge_to_node(acc, Edge.new(target_id, :contradicts, 1.0))
        
        # 2. Spawn MetaNode for the conflict
        context = %{node_a: my_id, node_b: target_id, similarity: score}
        case CMS.MetaNode.spawn_conflict("Semantic overlap detected during Genesis", context) do
          {:ok, meta_node} ->
             # Register and spawn MetaNode actor
             CMS.LogAppender.append_node_event(meta_node.id, :node_created, meta_node)
             CMS.NodeSupervisor.spawn_node(meta_node)
             
             # 3. Add Semantic Link to MetaNode
             edge_to_meta = Edge.new(meta_node.id, :semantic, 0.9)
             add_edge_to_node(acc, edge_to_meta)
          _ ->
             acc
        end
      else
        # Normal Semantic Relationship
        edge = Edge.new(target_id, :semantic, score)
        add_edge_to_node(acc, edge)
      end
    end)
  end

  # ... (prime_candidates and cosine_similarity remain) ...

  # --- Helper for Polymorphic Node Support ---

  defp crems_node?(node_id) do
    # CREMS nodes use pid-node-, beam-node-, or mother- prefixes
    String.starts_with?(node_id, ["pid-node-", "beam-node-", "mother-"])
  end

  defp add_edge_to_node(%CMS.Node{} = node, edge) do
    %{node | tail: CMS.NodeTail.add_edge(node.tail, edge)}
  end

  defp add_edge_to_node(%CREMS.Node{} = node, edge) do
    # Map-based Edge Structure for CREMS (Phase 7+)
    # This standardizes CREMS with CDMS for consistency across the Cognitive Graph.
    new_edge = %{
      type: edge.type, 
      target_node_id: edge.target_node_id, 
      weight: edge.weight
    }
    
    # Route to appropriate tail collection based on type
    tail = node.tail
    new_tail = case edge.type do
      t when t in [:parent_of, :child_of] ->
        %{tail | hierarchical_edges: [new_edge | tail.hierarchical_edges]}
      :communicates_with ->
        # Note: communications edges can have extra metadata, but Edge.t() is simplified.
        # NodeActor handles full protocol/ratio via explicit upsert_edge cast.
        %{tail | communication_edges: [new_edge | tail.communication_edges]}
      :depends_on ->
        %{tail | dependency_edges: [new_edge | tail.dependency_edges]}
      t when t in [:semantic, :contradicts, :semantically_related_to] ->
        # Standardize type to :semantically_related_to for CREMS
        standard_edge = %{new_edge | type: :semantically_related_to}
        %{tail | semantic_edges: [standard_edge | tail.semantic_edges]}
      t when t in [:defined_by, :defines] ->
        %{tail | symbiotic_edges: [new_edge | tail.symbiotic_edges]}
      _ ->
        tail
    end
    
    %{node | tail: new_tail}
  end

  @doc """
  Generic Hebbian Priming: Returns a list of semantic candidate pairs {id, score} for a given embedding.
  Queries all active NodeActors in parallel (Scatter-Gather).
  """
  def prime_candidates(embedding, my_id \\ nil) do
    # Configuration matches IngestionEngine Spec
    # Differentiate threshold: 0.9 for CREMS, 0.4 for CDMS
    min_score = if my_id && crems_node?(my_id), do: 0.9, else: 0.4
    max_links = 3
    
    # 1. Fetch all drift info from the lock-free ETS index
    # This eliminates GenServer deadlocks during Scatter-Gather.
    all_drift_info = CMS.Core.Logic.DriftIndex.get_all_drift_info()

    similarities =
      all_drift_info
      |> Enum.reduce([], fn {node_id, remote_embedding, _region_id}, acc ->
        if node_id != my_id and remote_embedding do
           score = cosine_similarity(embedding, remote_embedding)
           if score >= min_score do
             # Logger.debug("EdgeGenerator: Node #{my_id || "NEW"} evaluating #{node_id} -> Score: #{:io_lib.format("~.4f", [score])}")
             [{node_id, score} | acc]
           else
             acc
           end
        else
           acc
        end
      end)

    if length(similarities) > 0 do
       Logger.info("EdgeGenerator: Found #{length(similarities)} semantic candidates for #{my_id || "NEW"} (Min Score: #{min_score})")
    end

    similarities
    |> Enum.sort_by(fn {_, score} -> score end, &>=/2)
    |> Enum.take(max_links)
  end

  @doc """
  Calculates the cosine similarity between two vectors using native Nx operations for maximum performance.
  """
  def cosine_similarity(vec_a, vec_b) do
    try do
      # Directly use tensors if possible, or convert to tensors (much faster than list zip/sum)
      t_a = if is_struct(vec_a, Nx.Tensor), do: vec_a, else: Nx.tensor(vec_a)
      t_b = if is_struct(vec_b, Nx.Tensor), do: vec_b, else: Nx.tensor(vec_b)

      # cosine_similarity = (A . B) / (||A|| * ||B||)
      dot_product = Nx.dot(t_a, t_b) |> Nx.to_number()
      norm_a = Nx.sum(Nx.pow(t_a, 2)) |> Nx.sqrt() |> Nx.to_number()
      norm_b = Nx.sum(Nx.pow(t_b, 2)) |> Nx.sqrt() |> Nx.to_number()

      if norm_a == 0 or norm_b == 0, do: 0.0, else: dot_product / (norm_a * norm_b)
    rescue
      _ -> 0.0
    end
  end

  # --- Import Extraction ---

  defp find_imports(content, path) do
    ext = Path.extname(path)
    cond do
      ext in [".ex", ".exs"] -> 
        find_imports_elixir(content)
      ext == ".py" ->
        # Functional approximation for Python in Phase 1
        (Regex.scan(~r/^import\s+([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)/m, content) |> Enum.map(fn [_, name] -> name end))
        ++ (Regex.scan(~r/^from\s+([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)\s+import/, content) |> Enum.map(fn [_, name] -> name end))
      true ->
        []
    end
  end

  defp find_imports_elixir(content) do
    case Code.string_to_quoted(content) do
      {:ok, quoted} ->
        {_, names} = Macro.prewalk(quoted, [], fn
          {key, _, [{:__aliases__, _, names} | _]}, acc when key in [:alias, :import, :require, :use] ->
             {nil, [Enum.join(names, ".") | acc]}
          node, acc -> {node, acc}
        end)
        Enum.uniq(names)
      _ ->
        []
    end
  end
end

File: drift_index.ex

defmodule CMS.Core.Logic.DriftIndex do
  @moduledoc """
  A lock-free, read-optimized registry for Node embeddings and metadata.
  
  Uses an ETS table to allow NodeActors to perform semantic lookups (Scatter-Gather)
  without blocking other actors or causing circular GenServer deadlocks.
  """

  @table :drift_index

  @doc """
  Initializes the ETS table. Called during application boot.
  """
  def init do
    :ets.new(@table, [:set, :public, :named_table, read_concurrency: true])
  end

  @doc """
  Registers or updates a node's drift information in the index.
  """
  def register(node_id, embedding, region_id) do
    :ets.insert(@table, {node_id, embedding, region_id})
  end

  @doc """
  Removes a node from the index.
  """
  def unregister(node_id) do
    :ets.delete(@table, node_id)
  end

  @doc """
  Retrieves all active drift information records.
  Returns a list of {node_id, embedding, region_id}.
  """
  def get_all_drift_info do
    :ets.tab2list(@table)
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: semantic_region.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.SemanticRegion do
  @moduledoc """
  Manages Semantic Partitioning logic.

  Nodes are assigned to one of 256 regions based on their semantic embedding.
  """

  @num_regions 256

  @doc """
  Computes the region ID (0-255) for a given embedding and model version.
  """
  @spec compute_region_hash(Nx.Tensor.t() | list(), String.t()) :: integer()
  def compute_region_hash(embedding, model_version) do
    # 1. Ensure we have a binary representation (efficient for hashing)
    raw_data = if is_struct(embedding, Nx.Tensor), do: Nx.to_binary(embedding), else: :erlang.term_to_binary(embedding)

    # 2. Hashing and mapping to region space
    :crypto.hash(:sha256, "#{model_version}:" <> raw_data)
    |> :binary.decode_unsigned()
    |> rem(@num_regions)
  end

  @doc """
  Subscribes the current process to the relevant region topics.
  """
  def subscribe(region_id, node_id) do
    Phoenix.PubSub.subscribe(CMS.PubSub, "region:#{region_id}")
    Phoenix.PubSub.subscribe(CMS.PubSub, "node_pulse:#{node_id}")
    Phoenix.PubSub.subscribe(CMS.PubSub, "global:signals")
  end

  @doc """
  Unsubscribes from a region topic (used during Node Migration).
  """
  def unsubscribe(region_id, _node_id) do
    Phoenix.PubSub.unsubscribe(CMS.PubSub, "region:#{region_id}")
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: fid_validator.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.FIDValidator do
  @moduledoc """
  Validates the structural integrity of DataBodyPayloads during Ingestion.
  """

  alias CMS.DataBodyPayload.{Text, Code, Number, Link, Object}

  @spec validate(CMS.DataBodyPayload.t()) :: :ok | {:error, String.t()}

  def validate(%Text{content: c}) when is_binary(c) and byte_size(c) > 0, do: :ok
  def validate(%Text{}), do: {:error, "FID: Text content must be a non-empty string"}

  def validate(%Code{language: l, content: c}) when is_atom(l) and is_binary(c), do: :ok
  def validate(%Code{}), do: {:error, "FID: Code must have an atom language and string content"}

  def validate(%Number{value: v}) when is_number(v), do: :ok
  def validate(%Number{}), do: {:error, "FID: Number value must be integer or float"}

  def validate(%Link{uri: u}) when is_binary(u) do
    if Regex.match?(~r/^(http|https|gm|ipfs):\/\//, u) do
      :ok
    else
      {:error, "FID: Link URI must start with http, https, gm, or ipfs"}
    end
  end
  def validate(%Link{}), do: {:error, "FID: Link URI missing"}

  def validate(%Object{data: d}) when is_map(d), do: :ok
  def validate(%Object{}), do: {:error, "FID: Object data must be a map"}

  def validate(unknown), do: {:error, "Unknown struct type: #{inspect(unknown)}"}
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: fid_parser.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.FIDParser do
  @moduledoc """
  Provides functions for consuming Agents to parse and interpret
  CMS.DataBodyPayloads based on their Format ID (FID) type.

  Implements Gap 12: Polymorphic Consumption Logic.
  """

  alias CMS.DataBodyPayload.{Text, Code, Number, Link, Object}

  @doc """
  Parses a list of DataBodyPayloads, returning a list of raw usable content.
  """
  @spec parse_all([CMS.DataBodyPayload.t()]) :: list(any())
  def parse_all(payloads) when is_list(payloads) do
    Enum.map(payloads, &parse/1)
  end

  @doc """
  Parses a single DataBodyPayload into a usable primitive for an Agent.
  """
  @spec parse(CMS.DataBodyPayload.t()) :: any() | {:error, String.t()}

  # FID: Text -> Returns simple string
  def parse(%Text{content: content}), do: content

  # FID: Code -> Returns tuple {language, content} for the Specialist Agent to compile
  def parse(%Code{language: lang, content: content}), do: {lang, content}

  # FID: Number -> Returns {value, unit} for the Validator Agent (Regression Testing)
  def parse(%Number{value: value, unit: unit}), do: {value, unit}

  # FID: Link -> Returns URI. Agent must then query GM or External Web.
  def parse(%Link{uri: uri}), do: uri

  # FID: Object -> Returns the raw data map. Agent must interpret based on object_type.
  def parse(%Object{data: data}), do: data

  # Fallback for unknown/corrupt payloads
  def parse(other) do
    {:error, "Unknown or Malformed FID Payload: #{inspect(other)}"}
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: node_factory.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.NodeFactory do
  @moduledoc """
  Responsible for enforcing Content-Addressable Memory (CAM) principles.

  The Node ID is derived from a SHA-256 hash of the canonical JSON representation
  of the DataHead, DataBody, and Provenance Metadata. This ensures that
  identical knowledge maps to the same address.
  """

  alias CMS.NodeBody

  @doc """
  Derives a content-addressable ID for a new CMS Node.

  This ID is a hash of the NodeBody (Truth) and Provenance.
  We specifically exclude dynamic fields (like DataTail.salience_score or Edges)
  because the Identity of the node should not change just because it gained a new link.
  """
  @spec derive_content_addressable_id(NodeBody.t(), map()) :: String.t()
  def derive_content_addressable_id(%NodeBody{} = body, provenance_metadata) do
    # 1. Extract the "Truth" components.
    # We strictly use DataHead and DataBody.
    # DataTail is excluded from the ID hash because relationships (Edges) evolve
    # (Neuroplasticity), but the Node's fundamental "Fact" remains constant.
    truth_component = %{
      data_head: body.data_head,
      data_body: body.data_body
    }

    # 2. Canonical Serialization of Truth.
    # sort_keys: true is CRITICAL. {a:1, b:2} must hash strictly equal to {b:2, a:1}.
    canonical_truth_json = Jason.encode!(truth_component, sort_keys: true)

    # 3. Canonical Serialization of Provenance.
    # This ensures that if the same Fact comes from a different Source context,
    # it *might* be treated as a distinct node (depending on ingestion logic),
    # or merged. The ACN Whitepaper suggests Provenance is part of Identity.
    canonical_provenance_json = Jason.encode!(provenance_metadata, sort_keys: true)

    # 4. Concatenate and Hash.
    content_stream = canonical_truth_json <> canonical_provenance_json

    :crypto.hash(:sha256, content_stream)
    |> Base.encode16(case: :lower)
  end
end

------------------------------------------------------------
File: node_antenna.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.NodeAntenna do
  @moduledoc """
  The Antenna: Modulates the signal strength of a Node based on semantic importance and activity.
  """

  @derive Jason.Encoder
  defstruct [
    :gain,
    :salience,
    :frequency,
    :resonance_pattern,
    :dampening_factors
  ]

  @type t :: %__MODULE__{
    gain: float(),
    salience: float(),
    frequency: integer(), # Mutation count or similar
    resonance_pattern: String.t(),
    dampening_factors: [String.t()]
  }

  @doc """
  Creates a new Antenna with initial gain based on salience and file path.
  Gain calculation: Salience * 2.0
  """
  def new(path, salience \\ nil) do
    # Use provided salience or calculate heuristic baseline
    effective_salience = salience || calculate_initial_gain(path)
    
    # Gain = Salience * 2.0 (as per spec)
    gain = effective_salience * 2.0
    
    dampening = calculate_dampening_factors(path)
    dampening = if Enum.empty?(dampening), do: ["signal_established"], else: dampening
    
    %__MODULE__{
      gain: gain,
      salience: effective_salience,
      frequency: 100,
      resonance_pattern: "stable",
      dampening_factors: dampening
    }
  end

  @doc """
  Calculates the initial gain based on file type/extension.
  Spec: Source(0.8), Config(0.8), Doc(0.6), Log/Temp(0.1), Vendor(0.2).
  """
  def calculate_initial_gain(path) do
    cond do
      vendor?(path) -> 0.2
      log_or_temp?(path) -> 0.1
      source_code?(path) -> 0.8
      config?(path) -> 0.8
      documentation?(path) -> 0.6
      true -> 0.5
    end
  end

  defp calculate_dampening_factors(path) do
    factors = []
    factors = if vendor?(path), do: ["vendor_directory" | factors], else: factors
    factors = if generated_code?(path), do: ["generated_code" | factors], else: factors
    factors = if log_or_temp?(path), do: ["noise_file" | factors], else: factors
    factors
  end

  defp source_code?(path), do: String.match?(path, ~r/\.(ex|exs|py|js|ts|go|rs|c|cpp|rb|java)$/)
  defp config?(path), do: String.match?(path, ~r/(\.yaml|\.json|\.toml|\.ini|config|mix\.exs)$/)
  defp documentation?(path), do: String.match?(path, ~r/(\.md|\.txt|LICENSE|README)$/)
  defp vendor?(path), do: String.contains?(path, ["node_modules", "deps", "vendor", "_build", "target"])
  defp log_or_temp?(path), do: String.match?(path, ~r/(\.log|\.tmp|\.lock)$/)
  defp generated_code?(path), do: String.contains?(path, ["generated", "auto-generated"])

  # --- Access Behavior ---
  @behaviour Access

  @impl Access
  def fetch(term, key), do: Map.fetch(term, key)

  @impl Access
  def get_and_update(term, key, fun), do: Map.get_and_update(term, key, fun)

  @impl Access
  def pop(term, key), do: Map.pop(term, key)
end

File: data_body_payload.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.DataBodyPayload do
  @moduledoc """
  Defines the polymorphic structure for elements within a CMS.NodeBody.data_body.

  This implements the 'Format ID' (FID) system (Section 2.2.2), ensuring consuming
  Agents can deterministically parse content (e.g., distinguishing executable code
  from descriptive text).
  """

  # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  # FID: TEXT
  # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  defmodule Text do
    @derive {Jason.Encoder, only: [:type, :content]}
    defstruct type: :text, content: ""
    @type t :: %__MODULE__{type: :text, content: String.t()}
  end

  # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  # FID: CODE
  # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  defmodule Code do
    @derive {Jason.Encoder, only: [:type, :language, :content]}
    defstruct type: :code, language: :elixir, content: ""
    @type t :: %__MODULE__{type: :code, language: atom(), content: String.t()}
  end

  # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  # FID: NUMBER
  # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  defmodule Number do
    @derive {Jason.Encoder, only: [:type, :value, :unit]}
    defstruct type: :number, value: 0.0, unit: nil
    @type t :: %__MODULE__{type: :number, value: number(), unit: atom() | nil}
  end

  # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  # FID: LINK (External/GM Resource)
  # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  defmodule Link do
    @derive {Jason.Encoder, only: [:type, :uri, :description]}
    defstruct type: :link, uri: "", description: nil
    @type t :: %__MODULE__{type: :link, uri: String.t(), description: String.t() | nil}
  end

  # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  # FID: OBJECT (Serialized Data)
  # ----------------------------------------------------------------------------
  defmodule Object do
    @derive {Jason.Encoder, only: [:type, :object_type, :data]}
    defstruct type: :object, object_type: nil, data: %{}
    @type t :: %__MODULE__{type: :object, object_type: atom() | nil, data: map()}
  end

  # Union Type for Dialyzer
  @type t :: Text.t() | Code.t() | Number.t() | Link.t() | Object.t()
end

File: node_tail.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.NodeTail do
  @moduledoc """
  The Connectome: Manages the graph topology and edges for a CDMS Node.
  """

  alias CMS.Edge

  @derive {Jason.Encoder, only: [:hierarchical, :syntactic, :semantic, :functional]}
  defstruct [
    hierarchical: [], # List of CMS.Edge (PARENT_OF, CHILD_OF)
    syntactic: [],    # List of CMS.Edge (IMPORTS, IMPORTED_BY)
    semantic: [],     # List of CMS.Edge (RELATES_TO)
    functional: []    # List of CMS.Edge (Skeletal, Immune, Nervous)
  ]

  @type t :: %__MODULE__{
    hierarchical: [Edge.t()],
    syntactic: [Edge.t()],
    semantic: [Edge.t()],
    functional: [Edge.t()]
  }

  @doc """
  Creates a new empty Connectome.
  """
  def new do
    %__MODULE__{
      hierarchical: [],
      syntactic: [],
      semantic: [],
      functional: []
    }
  end

  @doc """
  Adds an edge to the appropriate category in the connectome.
  Ensures idempotency (no duplicate target/type pairs).
  """
  def add_edge(tail, %Edge{target_node_id: target, type: type} = edge) do
    case type do
      t when t in [:parent_of, :child_of] ->
        if exists?(tail.hierarchical, target, t), do: tail, else: %{tail | hierarchical: [edge | tail.hierarchical]}

      t when t in [:imports, :imported_by] ->
        if exists?(tail.syntactic, target, t), do: tail, else: %{tail | syntactic: [edge | tail.syntactic]}

      t when t in [:relates_to, :semantic, :contradicts, :supersedes, :superseded_by] ->
        if exists?(tail.semantic, target, t), do: tail, else: %{tail | semantic: [edge | tail.semantic]}

      t when t in [:links_dynamically_to, :symlinks_to, :mounts_on, :owned_by, :executable_by, :configures, :logs_to, :defines, :defined_by] ->
        if exists?(tail.functional, target, t), do: tail, else: %{tail | functional: [edge | tail.functional]}

      _ ->
        # Default to syntactic for generic dependencies
        if exists?(tail.syntactic, target, type), do: tail, else: %{tail | syntactic: [edge | tail.syntactic]}
    end
  end

  defp exists?(list, target, type) do
    Enum.any?(list, fn e -> e.target_node_id == target and e.type == type end)
  end

  # --- Access Behavior ---
  @behaviour Access

  @impl Access
  def fetch(term, key), do: Map.fetch(term, key)

  @impl Access
  def get_and_update(term, key, fun), do: Map.get_and_update(term, key, fun)

  @impl Access
  def pop(term, key), do: Map.pop(term, key)
end

defmodule CMS.NodeBody do
  @moduledoc """
  The Vessel of Content: Holds the semantic and physical truth of a file.
  """

  # Custom Jason.Encoder implemented below to handle binary data_body

  defstruct [
    :data_head,   # %CMS.DataHead{} (Summary & Concepts)
    :data_body,   # Binary (Compressed Content) or Nil (Shallow)
    :data_ledger, # %CMS.DataTail{} (The Ledger)
    :mode         # :deep (Mode A) or :shallow (Mode B)
  ]

  @type t :: %__MODULE__{
    data_head: CMS.DataHead.t(),
    data_body: binary() | nil,
    data_ledger: CMS.DataTail.t(),
    mode: :deep | :shallow
  }

  @doc """
  Creates a new NodeBody.
  Stores content in its raw form.
  """
  def new(head, body_content, ledger, mode \\ :deep) do
    %__MODULE__{
      data_head: head,
      data_body: body_content,
      data_ledger: ledger,
      mode: mode
    }
  end

  @doc """
  Returns the data_body as is.
  """
  def decompress(%__MODULE__{data_body: body}), do: body
  def decompress(_), do: nil

  # --- JSON Encoding ---

  defimpl Jason.Encoder, for: __MODULE__ do

    def encode(value, opts) do
      # Convert binary data_body to Base64 for JSON serialization
      {body_data, is_base64} = 
        if is_binary(value.data_body) do
          {Base.encode64(value.data_body), true}
        else
          {value.data_body, false}
        end

      %{
        data_head: value.data_head,
        data_body: body_data,
        data_body_is_base64: is_base64,
        data_ledger: value.data_ledger,
        mode: value.mode
      }
      |> Jason.Encode.map(opts)
    end
  end

  # --- Access Behavior ---
  @behaviour Access

  @impl Access
  def fetch(term, key), do: Map.fetch(term, key)

  @impl Access
  def get_and_update(term, key, fun), do: Map.get_and_update(term, key, fun)

  @impl Access
  def pop(term, key), do: Map.pop(term, key)
end



------------------------------------------------------------
File: json_extensions.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defimpl Jason.Encoder, for: Nx.Tensor do
  def encode(tensor, opts) do
    # Convert the high-performance Tensor binary into a simple list of floats
    # so it can be written to the JSON log file.
    list_data = Nx.to_flat_list(tensor)
    Jason.Encode.list(list_data, opts)
  end
end

defmodule CMS.Node do
  @moduledoc """
  The Quad-Partite Node: The anatomical unit of the CDMS.
  """

  @derive {Jason.Encoder, only: [:id, :head, :body, :tail, :antenna, :created_at, :last_fired]}
  defstruct [
    :id,         # 128-bit persistent UUID
    :head,       # %CMS.NodeHead{} (Cognitive Interface)
    :body,       # %CMS.NodeBody{} (Vessel of Content)
    :tail,       # %CMS.NodeTail{} (The Connectome)
    :antenna,    # %CMS.NodeAntenna{} (Signal Modulation)
    :provenance, # Map (Source Metadata & Trust)
    :created_at, # DateTime of creation
    :last_fired  # DateTime of last activation
  ]

  @type t :: %__MODULE__{
    id: String.t(),
    head: CMS.NodeHead.t(),
    body: CMS.NodeBody.t(),
    tail: CMS.NodeTail.t(),
    antenna: CMS.NodeAntenna.t(),
    provenance: map(),
    created_at: DateTime.t(),
    last_fired: DateTime.t()
  }

  @doc """
  Creates a new Quad-Partite Node.
  """
  def new(head, body, antenna, provenance \\ %{}, created_at \\ nil, last_fired \\ nil) do
    now = DateTime.utc_now()
    %__MODULE__{
      id: head.uuid,
      head: head,
      body: body,
      tail: CMS.NodeTail.new(),
      antenna: antenna,
      provenance: provenance,
      created_at: created_at || now,
      last_fired: last_fired || now
    }
  end

  # --- Access Behavior ---
  @behaviour Access

  @impl Access
  def fetch(term, key), do: Map.fetch(term, key)

  @impl Access
  def get_and_update(term, key, fun), do: Map.get_and_update(term, key, fun)

  @impl Access
  def pop(term, key), do: Map.pop(term, key)
end

defmodule CMS.DataTail do
  @moduledoc """
  The Ledger: The administrative component of the Node Body.
  Holds physical metadata and integrity anchors.
  """

  @derive {Jason.Encoder, only: [:physical_symlink, :integrity_hash, :mime_definition, :last_mutation, :salience_score, :permissions, :ownership, :immutable_flags, :genetic_provenance, :metabolism]}
  defstruct [
    :physical_symlink, # Absolute path to the OS file
    :integrity_hash,   # SHA-256 hash of the content
    :mime_definition,  # Precise file type definition
    :last_mutation,    # DateTime of last detected change
    :chrono_stack,     # CMS.ChronoStack (History)
    :salience_score,   # Float 0.0 - 1.0 (Intrinsic importance)
    # Anatomical Ledger Fields
    :permissions,      # Map: %{user: "rwx", group: "r--", other: "r--"}
    :ownership,        # Map: %{uid: 1000, gid: 1000, owner_name: "gifted"}
    :immutable_flags,  # List: ["readonly"]
    :genetic_provenance, # Map: %{creator: :user | :installer, package: "apt:python"}
    :metabolism        # :static_bone | :fluid_blood
  ]

  @type t :: %__MODULE__{
    physical_symlink: String.t(),
    integrity_hash: String.t(),
    mime_definition: String.t(),
    last_mutation: DateTime.t(),
    chrono_stack: CMS.ChronoStack.t(),
    salience_score: float(),
    permissions: map(),
    ownership: map(),
    immutable_flags: [String.t()],
    genetic_provenance: map()
  }

  @doc """
  Creates a new Ledger.
  """
  def new(path, hash, mime, salience \\ 0.5, permissions \\ %{}, ownership \\ %{}, flags \\ [], provenance \\ %{}, metabolism \\ :static_bone) do
    %__MODULE__{
      physical_symlink: path,
      integrity_hash: hash,
      mime_definition: mime,
      last_mutation: DateTime.utc_now(),
      chrono_stack: CMS.ChronoStack.new(),
      salience_score: salience,
      permissions: permissions,
      ownership: ownership,
      immutable_flags: flags,
      genetic_provenance: provenance,
      metabolism: metabolism
    }
  end

  # --- Access Behavior ---
  @behaviour Access

  @impl Access
  def fetch(term, key), do: Map.fetch(term, key)

  @impl Access
  def get_and_update(term, key, fun), do: Map.get_and_update(term, key, fun)

  @impl Access
  def pop(term, key), do: Map.pop(term, key)
end

defmodule CMS.Edge do
  @moduledoc """
  Represents an associative or structural link between two CMS Nodes.

  Crucial for defining the cognitive graph's topology.
  Implements Gap B: Explicit Link Typing.
  """

  @derive {Jason.Encoder, only: [:target_node_id, :type, :weight, :last_used_at]}
  defstruct [
    :target_node_id, # String: UUID/Hash of the connected node
    :type,           # Atom: :semantic, :syntactic, :dependency, :contradicts, :causes
    :weight,         # Float 0.01 - 1.0: Hebbian strength.
    :last_used_at    # DateTime: For DecayManager to calculate 'Active Forgetting'
  ]

  @type link_type :: :semantic | :syntactic | :dependency | :contradicts | :causes | :similar_to | :defines | :defined_by

  @type t :: %__MODULE__{
    target_node_id: String.t(),
    type: link_type(),
    weight: float(),
    last_used_at: DateTime.t()
  }

  @doc """
  Creates a new edge. Clamps weight between 0.01 and 1.0.
  """
  @spec new(String.t(), link_type(), float()) :: t()
  def new(target_node_id, type, weight \\ 0.5) do
    # Clamp weight to ensure system stability
    safe_weight = max(0.01, min(1.0, weight))

    %__MODULE__{
      target_node_id: target_node_id,
      type: type,
      weight: safe_weight,
      last_used_at: DateTime.utc_now()
    }
  end

  # --- Access Behavior ---
  @behaviour Access

  @impl Access
  def fetch(term, key), do: Map.fetch(term, key)

  @impl Access
  def get_and_update(term, key, fun), do: Map.get_and_update(term, key, fun)

  @impl Access
  def pop(term, key), do: Map.pop(term, key)
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: chrono_stack.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.ChronoStack do
  @moduledoc """
  The Chrono Stack: Maintains a Merkle-Linked history of Node changes.
  Uses Delta Compression (Reverse Diffs) to store history efficiently.
  """

  @derive {Jason.Encoder, only: [:snapshots]}
  defstruct [
    snapshots: [] # List of Snapshot structs
  ]

  # --- Access Behavior ---
  @behaviour Access

  @impl Access
  def fetch(term, key), do: Map.fetch(term, key)

  @impl Access
  def get_and_update(term, key, fun), do: Map.get_and_update(term, key, fun)

  @impl Access
  def pop(term, key), do: Map.pop(term, key)

  defmodule Snapshot do
    @derive {Jason.Encoder, only: [:timestamp, :hash, :diff, :cause]}
    defstruct [:timestamp, :hash, :diff, :cause]

    # --- Access Behavior ---
    @behaviour Access

    @impl Access
    def fetch(term, key), do: Map.fetch(term, key)

    @impl Access
    def get_and_update(term, key, fun), do: Map.get_and_update(term, key, fun)

    @impl Access
    def pop(term, key), do: Map.pop(term, key)
  end

  @doc """
  Creates a new empty ChronoStack.
  """
  def new, do: %__MODULE__{snapshots: []}

  @doc """
  Pushes a new state onto the stack.
  Calculates the DIFF between the NEW content and the OLD content.
  The Stack stores the PREVIOUS state as a Reverse Diff (how to get back to it from current).
  
  So if we have State A. We move to State B.
  NodeBody has State B.
  Stack has [Diff(B -> A)].
  """
  def push(stack, old_content, new_content, old_hash, cause \\ :livewire_mutation, max_depth \\ nil)
  def push([], old_c, new_c, hash, cause, max_depth), do: push(new(), old_c, new_c, hash, cause, max_depth)
  def push(stack, old_content, new_content, old_hash, cause, max_depth) do
    # Calculate Diff: How to turn New -> Old (Reverse Diff)
    # Since we don't have a library for "Patching", we will store the Myers Difference
    # relative to the NEW content to get the OLD content.
    
    diff = if old_content && new_content && String.valid?(old_content) && String.valid?(new_content) do
       List.myers_difference(String.to_charlist(new_content), String.to_charlist(old_content))
       |> inspect()
    else
       if old_content, do: "BINARY_OR_INVALID_UTF8_CHANGE", else: "ROOT_GENESIS"
    end

    snapshot = %Snapshot{
      timestamp: DateTime.utc_now(),
      hash: old_hash,
      diff: diff,
      cause: cause
    }

    snapshots = [snapshot | stack.snapshots]
    
    # Truncate if max_depth is set
    snapshots = if max_depth, do: Enum.take(snapshots, max_depth), else: snapshots

    new_stack = %{stack | snapshots: snapshots}

    new_stack
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: node_head.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.NodeHead do
  @moduledoc """
  Represents the 'Head' of a CMS Node: The Local Cognitive Engine.

  Responsibilities:
  1. Holds the semantic vector embedding for similarity comparisons.
  2. Manages the 'Metabolic State' (internal_state) which acts as a firing inhibitor.
  3. Tracks the embedding model version to handle Model Drift (Gap 7).
  """

  # Custom Jason.Encoder implemented below to handle tuple conversion

  defstruct [
    :uuid,                    # 128-bit persistent identifier
    :embedding,               # Nx.Tensor: The semantic representation
    :embedding_model_version, # String: e.g., "openai_ada_002_v1"
    :relevance_threshold,     # Float (0.0 - 1.0)
    :internal_state,          # Enum [Genesis, Steady, Mutating, Dormant]
    :spatial_coords,          # {x, y, z} tuple
    :width_descriptor,        # String: e.g., "mc0" (Gap 6.3)
    :label                    # String: Label for Jaro fallbacks
  ]

  @type uuid :: String.t()
  @type embedding :: Nx.Tensor.t()
  @type model_version :: String.t()
  @type internal_state :: :genesis | :steady | :mutating | :dormant
  @type spatial_coords :: {float(), float(), integer()}
  @type width_descriptor :: String.t()

  @type t :: %__MODULE__{
    uuid: uuid(),
    embedding: embedding(),
    embedding_model_version: model_version(),
    relevance_threshold: float(),
    internal_state: internal_state(),
    spatial_coords: spatial_coords(),
    width_descriptor: width_descriptor()
  }

  @doc """
  Creates a new NodeHead with default metabolic energy.
  Z-Axis in `coords` represents Hierarchical Depth (Root = 0).
  """
  @spec new(String.t(), Nx.Tensor.t(), String.t(), float(), {float(), float(), integer()}, String.t(), String.t()) :: t()
  def new(uuid, embedding, model_version, threshold \\ 0.35, coords \\ {0.0, 0.0, 0}, width \\ "m0", label \\ "") do
    %__MODULE__{
      uuid: uuid,
      embedding: embedding,
      embedding_model_version: model_version,
      relevance_threshold: threshold,
      internal_state: :genesis,
      spatial_coords: coords,
      width_descriptor: width,
      label: label
    }
  end

  # --- JSON Encoding ---
  defimpl Jason.Encoder, for: __MODULE__ do
    def encode(value, opts) do
      # Convert spatial_coords tuple to list for JSON serialization
      {x, y, z} = value.spatial_coords
      
      %{
        uuid: value.uuid,
        embedding: value.embedding,
        embedding_model_version: value.embedding_model_version,
        relevance_threshold: value.relevance_threshold,
        internal_state: value.internal_state,
        spatial_coords: [x, y, z],  # Convert tuple to list
        width_descriptor: value.width_descriptor
      }
      |> Jason.Encode.map(opts)
    end
  end

  # --- Access Behavior ---
  @behaviour Access

  @impl Access
  def fetch(term, key), do: Map.fetch(term, key)

  @impl Access
  def get_and_update(term, key, fun), do: Map.get_and_update(term, key, fun)

  @impl Access
  def pop(term, key), do: Map.pop(term, key)
end


================================================================================
