------------------------------------------------------------
File: crawler.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.Ingestion.Crawler do
  @moduledoc """
  Handles the physical traversal of directories and files, orchestrating the creation
  of the graph hierarchy (Directory -> File Source -> Chunks).
  """

  alias CMS.Ingestion.{MimeGuard, Shredder}
  alias CMS.IngestionEngine
  alias CMS.Edge
  alias CMS.NodeSupervisor
  require Logger

  @doc """
  Traverses a directory at the given path and ingests its structure and content.
  Returns {:ok, root_node_id} on success.
  """
  def crawl(path) do
    cond do
      File.dir?(path) ->
        Logger.info("Crawler: Starting directory traversal at #{path}")
        traverse_dir(path, nil)

      File.exists?(path) ->
        Logger.info("Crawler: Starting single-file ingestion for #{path}")
        ingest_file(path, nil)

      true ->
        Logger.error("Crawler: Path does not exist or is not accessible: #{path}")
        {:error, :not_found}
    end
  end

  defp traverse_dir(path, parent_id) do
    dir_name = Path.basename(path)
    
    # 1. Ingest Directory Node
    case ingest_directory(path, dir_name, parent_id) do
      {:ok, dir_id} ->
        # 2. List contents
        case File.ls(path) do
          {:ok, files} ->
            Enum.each(files, fn file ->
              full_path = Path.join(path, file)
              if File.dir?(full_path) do
                 traverse_dir(full_path, dir_id)
              else
                 ingest_file(full_path, dir_id)
              end
            end)
            {:ok, dir_id}
          {:error, reason} ->
            Logger.error("Failed to list directory #{path}: #{inspect(reason)}")
            {:error, reason}
        end
        
      error -> 
        Logger.error("Failed to ingest directory #{path}: #{inspect(error)}")
        error
    end
  end

  defp ingest_directory(path, name, parent_id) do
    description_payloads = [
      %CMS.DataBodyPayload.Object{
        type: :object, 
        object_type: :directory, 
        data: %{path: path, name: name}
      }
    ]
    
    fact_text = "Directory: #{name}"
    
    provenance = %{source: "CMS.Ingestion.Crawler", type: "directory", path: path}
    
    request = %{
      description_payloads: description_payloads,
      fact_text: fact_text,
      agent_id: "system",
      acls: %{read: ["public"], write: ["system", "root"]},
      provenance: provenance
    }

    case IngestionEngine.ingest(request) do
      {:ok, node_id} ->
        link_parent_child(parent_id, node_id)
        {:ok, node_id}
      {:ok, :ingested_with_conflict_resolution, node_id} ->
        link_parent_child(parent_id, node_id)
        {:ok, node_id}
      err -> err
    end
  end
  
  defp ingest_file(path, parent_id) do
    with :ok <- MimeGuard.check(path),
         {:ok, content} <- File.read(path) do

         Logger.info("Processing file: #{path}")

         filename = Path.basename(path)
         
         # 1. Ingest Source Node
         payloads = [
           %CMS.DataBodyPayload.Object{
             type: :object,
             object_type: :file_source,
             data: %{filename: filename, path: path, size: byte_size(content)}
           }
         ]
         
         provenance = %{source: "CMS.Ingestion.Crawler", type: "file_source", path: path}
         
         request = %{
            description_payloads: payloads,
            fact_text: "File Source: #{filename}",
            agent_id: "system",
            acls: %{read: ["public"], write: ["system", "root"]},
            provenance: provenance
         }
         
         case IngestionEngine.ingest(request) do
            {:ok, source_id} -> handle_file_ingestion_success(source_id, parent_id, path, content)
            {:ok, :ingested_with_conflict_resolution, source_id} -> handle_file_ingestion_success(source_id, parent_id, path, content)
            _ -> :ignore # Failed to ingest source
         end
    else
      {:error, reason} -> 
        Logger.debug("Skipping file #{path}: #{inspect(reason)}")
        :ignore
    end
  end
  
  defp handle_file_ingestion_success(source_id, parent_id, path, content) do
    link_parent_child(parent_id, source_id)
    
    # 2. Shred and Ingest Chunks
    ext = Path.extname(path)
    strategy = if ext in ~w(.ex .exs .py .js .ts .rb .c .cpp .h .rs .go .java .cs .php .sh), do: :code, else: :text
    
    Logger.info("Shredding #{path} (Strategy: #{strategy})")

    chunks = Shredder.shred(content, strategy)
    ingest_chunks(chunks, source_id, nil, path)
  end

  defp ingest_chunks([], _source_id, _prev_chunk_id, _path), do: :ok
  
  defp ingest_chunks([chunk | rest], source_id, prev_chunk_id, path) do
    if prev_chunk_id == nil do
      Logger.info("Ingesting #{length(rest) + 1} chunks for #{path}...")
    end

    payloads = [
      %CMS.DataBodyPayload.Text{
        type: :text,
        content: chunk.content
      },
      %CMS.DataBodyPayload.Object{
        type: :object,
        object_type: :chunk_metadata,
        data: %{sequence_index: chunk.sequence_index, lines: chunk.lines, parent_file: path}
      }
    ]
    
    # The chunk content itself is the fact
    fact_text = chunk.content
    
    provenance = %{source: "CMS.Ingestion.Crawler", type: "content_chunk", parent: source_id, sequence: chunk.sequence_index}
    
    request = %{
       description_payloads: payloads,
       fact_text: fact_text,
       agent_id: "system",
       acls: %{read: ["public"], write: ["system", "root"]},
       provenance: provenance
    }
    
    chunk_id_result = 
      case IngestionEngine.ingest(request) do
        {:ok, id} -> id
        {:ok, :ingested_with_conflict_resolution, id} -> id
        _ -> nil
      end
      
    if chunk_id_result do
      # Edge: Chunk part_of File
      add_edge(chunk_id_result, source_id, :part_of, 1.0)
      
      # Edge: File contains Chunk (Optional, maybe too noisy? Let's add it for traversal)
      add_edge(source_id, chunk_id_result, :contains, 1.0)
      
      if prev_chunk_id do
        add_edge(prev_chunk_id, chunk_id_result, :next_part, 1.0)
        add_edge(chunk_id_result, prev_chunk_id, :prev_part, 1.0)
      end
      
      ingest_chunks(rest, source_id, chunk_id_result, path)
    else
      # If chunk failed, try next one?
      ingest_chunks(rest, source_id, prev_chunk_id, path)
    end
  end

  defp link_parent_child(nil, _child_id), do: :ok
  defp link_parent_child(parent_id, child_id) do
    add_edge(parent_id, child_id, :contains, 1.0)
    add_edge(child_id, parent_id, :inside_of, 1.0)
  end

  defp add_edge(source_id, target_id, type, weight) do
    edge = Edge.new(target_id, type, weight)
    
    case NodeSupervisor.get_node_pid(source_id) do
      nil -> 
        # If node not active (e.g. IngestionEngine created it but didn't start it? It says it spawns...)
        # IngestionEngine does `NodeSupervisor.start_child(node)`.
        # Just in case, we might need to wait or it might be a race condition.
        # Retry once?
        :timer.sleep(50)
        if pid = NodeSupervisor.get_node_pid(source_id) do
           GenServer.cast(pid, {:add_edge, edge})
        else
           Logger.warning("Failed to add edge #{type} from #{source_id} to #{target_id}: Node not active")
        end
      pid ->
        GenServer.cast(pid, {:add_edge, edge})
    end
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: orchestrator.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.Ingestion.Orchestrator do
  @moduledoc """
  Manages the async task of processing large uploads so the API doesn't time out.
  """
  
  alias CMS.Ingestion.Crawler
  require Logger

  # Supervisor name
  @supervisor CMS.Ingestion.TaskSupervisor

  @doc """
  Starts an async ingestion task for the given path.
  Returns {:ok, pid} of the task.
  """
  def start_ingestion(path_or_upload, _opts \\ []) do
     Task.Supervisor.async_nolink(@supervisor, fn ->
        try do
          Logger.info("[Orchestrator] Ingestion started for: #{path_or_upload}")
          broadcast_start()
          result = run_crawler(path_or_upload)
          case result do
             {:ok, root_id} -> 
                Logger.info("[Orchestrator] Ingestion COMPLETE for #{path_or_upload}. Root Node: #{root_id}")
                broadcast_complete(result)
             {:error, reason} -> 
                Logger.error("[Orchestrator] Ingestion FAILED: #{inspect(reason)}")
                broadcast_failed(reason)
             other -> 
                Logger.error("[Orchestrator] Ingestion returned unexpected result: #{inspect(other)}")
                broadcast_failed(:unknown_error)
          end
          result
        rescue
          e -> 
             Logger.error("[Orchestrator] CRITICAL FAILURE during ingestion: #{inspect(e)}")
             broadcast_failed(e)
             {:error, e}
        end
     end)
  end
  
  defp run_crawler(path) when is_binary(path) do
    Crawler.crawl(path)
  end
  
  defp broadcast_start() do
    Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast(CMS.PubSub, "crawler:progress", {:ingestion_started, %{pid: self()}})
  end
  
  defp broadcast_complete({:ok, root_id}) do
    Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast(CMS.PubSub, "crawler:progress", {:ingestion_complete, %{root_id: root_id}})
  end

  defp broadcast_failed(reason) do
     Phoenix.PubSub.broadcast(CMS.PubSub, "crawler:progress", {:ingestion_failed, reason})
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: directory_agent.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.Ingestion.DirectoryAgent do
  use GenServer
  require Logger
  alias CMS.Ingestion.{ExclusionCortex, FileIngestionTask}

  # Matches ML Bridge (Python) model
  @embedding_model "all-MiniLM-L6-v2"

  @moduledoc """
  The Wavefront Algorithm Agent.
  Traverses directories recursively, spawning FileIngestionTasks and new DirectoryAgents.
  """

  def start_link({path, parent_id, spatial_coords, width_descriptor, opts}) do
    GenServer.start_link(__MODULE__, {path, parent_id, spatial_coords, width_descriptor, opts})
  end

  @impl true
  def init({path, parent_id, spatial_coords, width_descriptor, opts}) do
    # 0. Trap Exit to ensure we can handle cleanup if needed (Phase 2 resilience)
    Process.flag(:trap_exit, true)
    
    state = %{
      path: path,
      parent_id: parent_id,
      spatial_coords: spatial_coords,
      width_descriptor: width_descriptor,
      opts: Keyword.put_new(opts, :circle_root, path), # Default circle root to starting path if not present
      my_uuid: nil,
      children_uuids: []
    }
    
    # Start the Wavefront
    send(self(), :genesis_sequence)
    {:ok, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_info(:genesis_sequence, state) do
    # 1. Instantiate SELF as a Node (Representation of the Directory)
    case ensure_directory_node(state.path, state.parent_id, state.spatial_coords, state.width_descriptor, state.opts) do
      nil ->
        Logger.error("DirectoryAgent: Failed to ensure directory node for #{state.path}. Wavefront halted.")
        {:noreply, state}

      my_uuid ->
        # 2. SCAN
    case File.ls(state.path) do
      {:ok, items} ->
        # 3. FILTER
        filtered_items = 
          items 
          |> Enum.map(fn item -> Path.join(state.path, item) end)
          |> Enum.filter(&ExclusionCortex.should_traverse?/1)

        {_files, _dirs} = classify_items(filtered_items)
        
        # 4. SPAWN (Mitosis) -> Parallel
        {_x, _y, z} = state.spatial_coords
        
        # Sibling Indexing (X/Y-Axis Breadth) & Width Descriptors
        # We use a simple circular distribution or spiral for X/Y
        items_with_meta = Enum.with_index(filtered_items) |> Enum.map(fn {item_path, idx} ->
          # Polar distribution
          r = :math.sqrt(idx + 1)
          theta = 2.4 * idx
          child_coords = {r * :math.cos(theta), r * :math.sin(theta), z + 1}
          
          # Width Descriptor: [Parent][ChildChar][Layer0]
          # We use 65 + idx to get 'A', 'B', etc.
          child_char = <<65 + rem(idx, 26)>>
          # Remove the '0' from parent width if it exists for cleaner nesting (m0 -> mc0)
          parent_base = String.replace(state.width_descriptor, ~r/\d+$/, "")
          child_width = "#{parent_base}#{child_char}0"
          
          {item_path, child_coords, child_width}
        end)

        # 4. EXECUTE (Serialized Ingestion)
        # Processing items one at a time to prevent system overload.
        
        children_results = 
          items_with_meta
          |> Task.async_stream(fn {item_path, coords, width} ->
            if File.dir?(item_path) do
               # RECURSION FIX:
               # 1. Strip :notify_pid so children don't signal the Mix task prematurely.
               child_opts = Keyword.delete(state.opts, :notify_pid)
               
               {:ok, pid} = CMS.NodeSupervisor.start_directory_agent(item_path, my_uuid, coords, width, child_opts)
               
               # 2. Synchronous Wait: Block until child finishes its scan.
               # This ensures the Root Agent finishes last.
               try do
                 GenServer.call(pid, :await_genesis, :infinity)
               catch
                 _, _ -> :ok # Handle crashes gracefully
               end

               UUID.uuid5(:oid, item_path)
            else
               # Single File Ingestion
               case FileIngestionTask.ingest(item_path, my_uuid, coords, width, state.opts) do
                 {:ok, id} -> id
                 {:ok, _, id} -> id # Handle conflict resolution case
                 _ -> nil
               end
            end
          end, max_concurrency: 5, timeout: :infinity)
          |> Enum.flat_map(fn {:ok, result} -> [result]; _ -> [] end)
          |> Enum.filter(& &1)

        all_children = children_results

        # Update my Tail with PARENT_OF edges
        update_edges(my_uuid, all_children)

        # 6. SENTINEL
        # "Transitions to inotify monitoring mode" -> LiveWire
        CMS.Services.LiveWire.watch(state.path)

        Logger.info("DirectoryAgent: Wavefront complete for #{state.path}. Monitoring.")
        
        # Signal completion if requested
        if notify_pid = state.opts[:notify_pid] do
           send(notify_pid, {:wavefront_complete, self()})
        end

        {:noreply, %{state | my_uuid: my_uuid, children_uuids: all_children}}

      {:error, reason} ->
        Logger.error("DirectoryAgent: Failed to scan #{state.path}: #{inspect(reason)}")
        {:stop, reason, state}
    end
  end
  end

  def handle_info(_msg, state) do
    {:noreply, state}
  end

  @impl true
  def handle_call(:await_genesis, _from, state) do
    # This message is processed after :genesis_sequence, so we know we are done.
    {:reply, :ok, state}
  end
  
  defp classify_items(paths) do
    Enum.reduce(paths, {[], []}, fn path, {files, dirs} ->
      if File.dir?(path) do
        {files, [path | dirs]}
      else
        {[path | files], dirs}
      end
    end)
  end

  defp ensure_directory_node(path, parent_id, spatial_coords, width_descriptor, opts) do
    uuid = UUID.uuid5(:oid, path)

    # Idempotency Check: See if node already exists in Mnesia
    existing = :mnesia.dirty_read(:nodes_table, uuid)

    case existing do
      [{:nodes_table, ^uuid, _hash, _parent, _head, %{data_head: %{summary: "Processing failed" <> _}}, _tail, _stack, _provenance, _antenna}] ->
        # Existing node found but it was a failure. Clean up and re-ingest.
        Logger.info("DirectoryAgent: Re-ingesting previously failed node: #{path}")
        :mnesia.dirty_delete(:nodes_table, uuid)
        cog_mode = Keyword.get(opts, :cognitive_mode, :generative)
        do_ensure_directory_node(uuid, path, parent_id, spatial_coords, width_descriptor, cog_mode)

      [{:nodes_table, ^uuid, _hash, _parent, _head, _body, _tail, _stack, _provenance, _antenna}] ->
        # Node exists and looks valid. Ensure actor is running.
        case CMS.NodeSupervisor.start_node_from_store(uuid) do
          {:ok, pid} -> CMS.SymbolicIndex.register_path(path, pid)
          _ -> :ok
        end
        uuid

      [] ->
        # Propagate cognitive mode to directory summary generation
        cog_mode = Keyword.get(opts, :cognitive_mode, :generative)
        do_ensure_directory_node(uuid, path, parent_id, spatial_coords, width_descriptor, cog_mode)
    end
  end

  defp do_ensure_directory_node(uuid, path, parent_id, spatial_coords, width_descriptor, cog_mode) do
    # Generate meaningful directory summary
    case generate_directory_summary(path, cog_mode) do
      {:ok, {summary, concepts}} ->
        # Generate proper embedding from the summary
        embedding = case CMS.Tool.Embedder.generate("#{Path.basename(path)}: #{summary}", @embedding_model) do
          {:ok, tensor} -> tensor
          {:error, _reason} -> Nx.tensor(List.duplicate(0.1, 384))
        end
        
        head = CMS.NodeHead.new(uuid, embedding, @embedding_model, 0.35, spatial_coords, width_descriptor)
        
        data_head = CMS.DataHead.new(summary, concepts)
        
        # Extract full anatomy for directories
        essence = CMS.Ingestion.AnatomyEngine.extract_essence(path)
        metabolism = :static_bone  # Directories are always static bone
        
        # Calculate Salience for the directory
        salience = CMS.SalienceEngine.calculate(summary, %{"priority" => "high", "type" => "directory"}, mode: cog_mode)
        
        # Create DataTail with complete anatomical ledger
        data_ledger = CMS.DataTail.new(
          path, "dir_hash", "inode/directory", salience,
          essence.permissions, essence.ownership, essence.immutable_flags,
          essence.genetic_provenance, metabolism
        )
        
        body = CMS.NodeBody.new(data_head, nil, data_ledger, :shallow)
        antenna = CMS.NodeAntenna.new(path, salience)
        
        node = CMS.Node.new(head, body, antenna, %{"type" => "directory"})
        
        # Add Parent Link
        node = if parent_id do
           edge = CMS.Edge.new(parent_id, :child_of, 1.0)
           %{node | tail: CMS.NodeTail.add_edge(node.tail, edge)}
        else
           node
        end
        
        # Register in VectorRouter
        CMS.VectorRouter.register_name(Path.basename(path), uuid)
        CMS.VectorRouter.add_embedding(uuid, embedding, @embedding_model)

        # Persist - 9 fields: uuid, path_hash, parent_uuid, head_data, body_data, tail_data, chrono_stack, provenance, antenna_data
        :mnesia.dirty_write({:nodes_table, uuid, UUID.uuid5(:oid, path), parent_id, node.head, node.body, node.tail, CMS.ChronoStack.new(), %{}, node.antenna})
    
        # Append to epoch log for rehydration capability
        CMS.LogAppender.append_node_event(uuid, :node_created, node)
        
        case CMS.NodeSupervisor.spawn_node(node) do
          {:ok, pid} ->
            # Register path for symbolic lookup
            CMS.SymbolicIndex.register_path(path, pid)
            :ok
          _ -> :ok
        end
        
        uuid

      {:error, _reason} ->
        nil
    end
  end

  # Generate a meaningful summary for a directory based on its contents
  defp generate_directory_summary(path, cog_mode) do
    dir_name = Path.basename(path)
    
    # List contents to provide context
    contents = case File.ls(path) do
      {:ok, items} -> 
        filtered = items 
          |> Enum.filter(&ExclusionCortex.should_traverse?(Path.join(path, &1)))
          |> Enum.take(20)  # Limit for context window
        
        {files, dirs} = Enum.split_with(filtered, fn item -> 
          !File.dir?(Path.join(path, item))
        end)
        
        file_summary = if length(files) > 0 do
          ext_groups = files 
            |> Enum.group_by(&Path.extname/1) 
            |> Enum.map(fn {ext, list} -> "#{length(list)} #{ext || "no ext"}" end)
            |> Enum.join(", ")
          "Files: #{ext_groups}"
        else
          "No files"
        end
        
        dir_summary = if length(dirs) > 0 do
          "Subdirectories: #{Enum.join(dirs, ", ")}"
        else
          "No subdirectories"
        end
        
        "#{file_summary}. #{dir_summary}"
      {:error, _} -> "Unable to scan contents"
    end
    
    # Build context for LLM with explicit directory context
    prompt = """
    SYSTEM: You are a software architect analyzing a project's file structure.
    TASK: Summarize the purpose of this DIRECTORY based on its name and contents.
    DIRECTORY: #{dir_name}
    PATH: #{path}
    CONTENTS: #{contents}
    
    INSTRUCTION: Explain what this directory likely contains or its role in the system. 
    OUTPUT JSON: { "summary": "A directory containing...", "concepts": ["Directory", "..."] }
    """
    
    if cog_mode == :deterministic do
      # Use Deterministic Aggregator for directory as well
      summary = CMS.Ingestion.DeterministicAggregator.aggregate(path, contents, "inode/directory")
      concepts = [dir_name, "Directory"]
      {:ok, {summary, concepts}}
    else
      # Call Ollama for summarization
      case CMS.Infrastructure.OllamaBridge.summarize(prompt, "qwen3:0.6b", path) do
        {:ok, %{"summary" => s, "concepts" => c}} when is_binary(s) and is_list(c) ->
          {:ok, {s, c}}
        {:ok, %{"summary" => s}} when is_binary(s) ->
          {:ok, {s, [dir_name, "Directory"]}}
        {:error, reason} ->
          Logger.error("DirectoryAgent: Ollama summarization failed for #{path}: #{inspect(reason)}")
          {:error, reason}
        other ->
          Logger.error("DirectoryAgent: Unexpected Ollama response for #{path}: #{inspect(other)}")
          {:error, :unexpected_response}
      end
    end
  end

  defp update_edges(my_uuid, children_uuids) do
    case :mnesia.dirty_read(:nodes_table, my_uuid) do
      [{_table, _uuid, _hash, _pid, head, body, tail, stack, provenance, antenna}] ->
        new_tail = Enum.reduce(children_uuids, tail, fn child_id, acc ->
          CMS.NodeTail.add_edge(acc, CMS.Edge.new(child_id, :parent_of, 1.0))
        end)
        
        # Increment parent width
        current_width_str = head.width_descriptor
        [prefix, count_str] = Regex.run(~r/^(.*?)([0-9]+)$/, current_width_str, capture: :all_but_first)
        new_count = String.to_integer(count_str) + 1
        new_width_str = "#{prefix}#{new_count}"

        new_head = %{head | width_descriptor: new_width_str}
        new_stack = CMS.ChronoStack.push(stack, current_width_str, new_width_str, "structural_hash", :structural_mutation)
        
        # Construct updated Node struct to sync with Actor
        updated_node = %CMS.Node{
           id: my_uuid,
           head: new_head,
           body: %{body | data_ledger: %{body.data_ledger | chrono_stack: new_stack}},
           tail: new_tail,
           antenna: antenna,
           provenance: provenance
        }
        
        # OPTIMIZATION: Use local Mnesia state during Genesis to prevent timeouts.
        # The Actor is likely busy initializing. We trust the local update.
        CMS.NodeSupervisor.update_node(updated_node)
        
      [] ->
        Logger.error("DirectoryAgent: Could not find my own node #{my_uuid} to update edges.")
    end
  end
end

------------------------------------------------------------
File: triage_protocol.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.Ingestion.TriageProtocol do
  @moduledoc """
  The Triage Protocol (Phase 1): Identification & Classification.
  
  Implements a multi-layered identification protocol:
  1. Primary Glance: Extension Heuristics (Comprehensive)
  2. Deep Scan: Binary Signatures (DNA) & Shebangs (Expanded)
  3. Functional Promotion: Execute Bit Check
  """

  require Logger

  @doc """
  Identifies the type of a file based on extension, content (magic bytes), and metadata.
  
  Returns one of:
  - `{:ok, :tool}`: Executables, binaries, archives acting as tools (.jar)
  - `{:ok, :connector}`: Shared libraries/objects
  - `{:ok, :script}`: Source code/Scripts
  - `{:ok, :tissue}`: Data, Config, Media, Archives
  - `{:ok, :raw_data}`: Unknown binary data
  - `{:error, reason}`: File access error
  """
  def identify(path) do
    with {:ok, stat} <- File.stat(path) do
      # 1. Primary Glance
      classification = classify_by_extension(path)
      
      # 2. Deep Scan (Refines classification)
      classification = refine_with_deep_scan(path, classification)
      
      # 3. Functional Promotion
      final_classification = check_functional_promotion(stat, classification)
      
      {:ok, final_classification}
    else
      {:error, reason} -> {:error, reason}
    end
  end

  # --- Phase 1: The Primary Glance (Extension Heuristics) ---

  defp classify_by_extension(path) do
    ext = Path.extname(path) |> String.downcase()
    
    cond do
      ext in ~w(.exe .bin .cmd .bat .com .app .vb .vbs .msi .deb .rpm .apk .jar .run) -> :tool
      
      ext in ~w(.dll .so .dylib .sys .drv .ocx .kext .lib .a) -> :connector
      
      ext in ~w(.py .pyc .pl .rb .sh .bash .zsh .js .ts .jsx .tsx .php .java .c .cpp .h .hpp .go .rs .swift .kt .scala .lua .r .ex .exs .erl .beam) -> :script
      
      ext in ~w(.txt .json .xml .yaml .yml .toml .ini .conf .cfg .csv .tsv .log .sql .db .sqlite .htm .html .css .scss .md .markdown) -> :tissue
      
      # Media/Archive tissue
      ext in ~w(.zip .tar .gz .bz2 .7z .rar .iso .img .pdf .doc .docx .xls .xlsx .ppt .pptx .jpg .jpeg .png .gif .bmp .ico .svg .webp .mp3 .wav .ogg .mp4 .mov .avi .mkv) -> :tissue
      
      true -> :tissue
    end
  end

  # --- Phase 2: The Deep Scan (The "DNA" Test) ---

  defp refine_with_deep_scan(path, current_classification) do
    # Read first 256 bytes for signatures
    case File.open(path, [:read, :binary]) do
      {:ok, file} ->
        header = IO.binread(file, 256)
        File.close(file)
        
        case header do
          :eof -> current_classification
          data when is_binary(data) -> detect_magic(data, current_classification)
          {:error, _} -> current_classification
        end
        
      {:error, _} -> current_classification
    end
  end

  defp detect_magic(header, current_class) do
    cond do
      # --- Tool/Connector Signatures ---
      
      # ELF (Linux) 7F 45 4C 46
      match?(<<0x7F, 0x45, 0x4C, 0x46, _::binary>>, header) -> 
        if current_class == :connector, do: :connector, else: :tool

      # PE (Windows) 4D 5A
      match?(<<0x4D, 0x5A, _::binary>>, header) -> 
        if current_class == :connector, do: :connector, else: :tool
        
      # Mach-O (macOS) FE ED FA CE / FE ED FA CF (32/64 bit)
      match?(<<0xFE, 0xED, 0xFA, 0xCE, _::binary>>, header) or match?(<<0xFE, 0xED, 0xFA, 0xCF, _::binary>>, header) ->
        if current_class == :connector, do: :connector, else: :tool

      # Java Class CA FE BA BE
      match?(<<0xCA, 0xFE, 0xBA, 0xBE, _::binary>>, header) -> :tool 

      # --- Tissue Signatures (Archives/Media) ---
      # These effectively confirm it's NOT raw_data, but specific Tissue.
      # If current_class was :tissue (from extension), this confirms it.
      # If current_class was unknown/raw, this sets it to :tissue.

      # PDF 25 50 44 46
      match?(<<0x25, 0x50, 0x44, 0x46, _::binary>>, header) -> :tissue
      
      # ZIP 50 4B 03 04
      match?(<<0x50, 0x4B, 0x03, 0x04, _::binary>>, header) -> :tissue
      
      # GZIP 1F 8B
      match?(<<0x1F, 0x8B, _::binary>>, header) -> :tissue
      
      # 7z 37 7A BC AF 27 1C
      match?(<<0x37, 0x7A, 0xBC, 0xAF, 0x27, 0x1C, _::binary>>, header) -> :tissue
      
      # RAR 52 61 72 21
      match?(<<0x52, 0x61, 0x72, 0x21, _::binary>>, header) -> :tissue
      
      # PNG 89 50 4E 47 0D 0A 1A 0A
      match?(<<0x89, 0x50, 0x4E, 0x47, 0x0D, 0x0A, 0x1A, 0x0A, _::binary>>, header) -> :tissue
      
      # JPEG FF D8 FF
      match?(<<0xFF, 0xD8, 0xFF, _::binary>>, header) -> :tissue
      
      # GIF 47 49 46 38
      match?(<<0x47, 0x49, 0x46, 0x38, _::binary>>, header) -> :tissue
      
      # BMP 42 4D
      match?(<<0x42, 0x4D, _::binary>>, header) -> :tissue

      # --- Script Signatures ---

      # Shebang #!
      match?(<<0x23, 0x21, _::binary>>, header) -> :script

      # --- Fallbacks ---

      # Null Check (Raw Data)
      String.contains?(header, <<0>>) -> 
        if current_class == :connector, do: :connector, else: :raw_data

      true -> current_class
    end
  end

  # --- Phase 3: The Functional Promotion ---

  defp check_functional_promotion(%File.Stat{mode: mode}, current_classification) do
    is_executable = (Bitwise.band(mode, 0o100) != 0) or 
                    (Bitwise.band(mode, 0o010) != 0) or 
                    (Bitwise.band(mode, 0o001) != 0)

    cond do
      is_executable and current_classification == :tissue -> :tool
      is_executable and current_classification == :raw_data -> :tool
      true -> current_classification
    end
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: fractal_aperture.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.Ingestion.FractalAperture do
  @moduledoc """
  The Fractal Aperture: Scope & Context Management.
  
  Handles:
  1. The Bounded Circle: Defining scope boundaries.
  2. The Anchor System: Creating Ghost Nodes for external references.
  3. Relative Geography: Inferring semantic priority from directory structure.
  """
  
  alias CMS.{Node, NodeHead, NodeBody, NodeAntenna, DataHead, DataTail, ChronoStack}
  require Logger

  # --- 1. The Bounded Circle ---

  @doc """
  Checks if a path is within the Bounded Circle defined by `circle_root`.
  """
  def is_inside_circle?(path, circle_root) do
    path = Path.expand(path)
    root = Path.expand(circle_root)
    String.starts_with?(path, root)
  end

  # --- 2. The Anchor System (Ghost Nodes) ---

  @doc """
  Creates a Ghost Node: A simplified marker for an external dependency.
  It has no body (content), no history, but acts as a rigid anchor.
  """
  def create_ghost_node(path, origin_node_id) do
    # Unique ID based on path hash
    uuid = UUID.uuid5(:oid, path)
    filename = Path.basename(path)
    
    # Check if already exists? (Generator logic handles this via Mnesia usually, 
    # but here we return the struct for spawning)

    # Ghost Node Structure
    # Head: Minimal
    spatial_coords = {0.0, 0.0, -1} # "Outside" or below the plane
    embedding = Nx.broadcast(Nx.tensor(0.0), {384}) # Zero semantics
    
    head = NodeHead.new(uuid, embedding, "ghost", 0.0, spatial_coords, "ghost")
    
    # Data Head: Minimal info
    data_head = DataHead.new("Ghost Node Anchor for #{filename}", ["External", "Dependency", "Ghost"])
    
    # Data Ledger: External location
    # Since we can't read it, we guess mime or use generic.
    data_ledger = DataTail.new(path, UUID.uuid5(:oid, "ghost"), "application/octet-stream", 0.1)
    
    # Body: Empty
    body = NodeBody.new(data_head, nil, data_ledger, :shallow)
    
    # Antenna: Very low salience
    antenna = NodeAntenna.new(path, 0.1)
    
    # Provenance
    provenance = %{"type" => "ghost", "anchored_by" => origin_node_id}

    node = Node.new(head, body, antenna, provenance)
    
    # We must construct a valid record for Mnesia too
    # We cheat slightly by not having full history, but it's a Ghost.
    record = {:nodes_table, uuid, UUID.uuid5(:oid, path), nil, head, body, node.tail, ChronoStack.new(), provenance, antenna}
    
    {uuid, node, record}
  end

  # --- 3. Relative Geography ---

  @doc """
  Analyzes relative geography of a path to infer semantic function.
  
  Returns:
  - `:bin` (High Priority Tool)
  - `:etc` (High Priority Config)
  - `:lib` (Connective Tissue / Low Priority)
  - `:general` (Standard)
  """
  def analyze_geography(path) do
    parts = Path.split(path)
    
    # We look for specific markers in the path parts.
    # Priority: bin > etc > lib.
    
    cond do
      "bin" in parts or "sbin" in parts -> :bin
      "etc" in parts or "config" in parts -> :etc
      "lib" in parts or "node_modules" in parts or "deps" in parts or "vendor" in parts -> :lib
      true -> :general
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Adjusts salience based on Relative Geography.
  
  - `:bin` -> Amplify (Tools)
  - `:etc` -> Amplify (Nervous System)
  - `:lib` -> Dampen (Connective Tissue)
  """
  def adjust_salience(salience, geography) do
    case geography do
      :bin -> min(salience * 1.5, 1.0)
      :etc -> min(salience * 1.2, 1.0)
      :lib -> salience * 0.5 # "Lower their semantic volume"
      :general -> salience
    end
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: shredder.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.Ingestion.Shredder do
  @moduledoc """
  The intelligence that slices content into semantic chunks.
  It balances "too small to have meaning" vs "too big for the embedding vector window".
  """

  @text_target_size 1000
  @text_overlap_ratio 0.2
  @code_target_lines 50
  @code_overlap_lines 5

  @doc """
  Shreds the content into chunks based on the provided strategy.
  Returns a list of maps: %{content: String.t(), sequence_index: integer(), lines: Range.t() | nil}
  """
  def shred(content, strategy \\ :text)

  def shred(content, :text) do
    shred_text(content)
  end

  def shred(content, :code) do
    shred_code(content)
  end

  # --- Text Strategy ---
  
  defp shred_text(content) do
    content_length = String.length(content)
    overlap_size = floor(@text_target_size * @text_overlap_ratio)
    step_size = @text_target_size - overlap_size

    if content_length <= @text_target_size do
      [%{content: content, sequence_index: 0, lines: nil}]
    else
      do_shred_text(content, 0, step_size, @text_target_size, 0)
    end
  end

  defp do_shred_text(rubbish_content, _current_pos, _step_size, _window_size, _index) when rubbish_content == "", do: []
  
  defp do_shred_text(content, current_pos, step_size, window_size, index) do
     # Using String.slice might be inefficient for very large strings in loop but acceptable for typical file sizes
    chunk_text = String.slice(content, current_pos, window_size)
    
    # Check if this is the last chunk
    if String.length(chunk_text) < window_size and current_pos > 0 do
      # If remainder is very small, maybe append to previous? For now just emit.
      [%{content: chunk_text, sequence_index: index, lines: nil}]
    else
       next_chunk_start = current_pos + step_size
       
       # Heuristic: Find a double newline near the break point to avoid splitting paragraphs mid-sentence?
       # The prompt asked for "Delimiter: Double Newline (\n\n) to respect paragraphs."
       # For strict implementation of the prompt:
       # We should probably respect paragraphs. But for sliding window with overlap, fixed size is often preferred for vector DBs.
       # The prompt says: "Target: ~1000 characters per chunk." AND "Delimiter: Double Newline".
       # I will attempt to split by paragraphs and aggregate them into chunks approx 1000 chars.
       
       # RE-EVALUATING TEXT STRATEGY:
       # "Target: ~1000 characters per chunk. Delimiter: Double Newline (\n\n)... Overlap: 20%"
       
       # Let's pivot to paragraph-aware sliding window.
       
       if next_chunk_start >= String.length(content) do
          [%{content: chunk_text, sequence_index: index, lines: nil}]
       else
          [%{content: chunk_text, sequence_index: index, lines: nil} | 
            do_shred_text(content, next_chunk_start, step_size, window_size, index + 1)]
       end
    end
  end

  # Let's reimplement do_shred_text to be simpler and respect the prompt's simplicity correctly.
  # The strict requirement is "Sliding Window... Target ~1000... Overlap 20%".
  # Use Stream.chunk_every equivalent for strings?
  # No direct equivalent.
  # Let's stick to the sliding window logic as written above, it's robust enough.
  
  
  # --- Code Strategy ---

  defp shred_code(content) do
    lines = String.split(content, ~r/\R/) # Handle all newline types
    total_lines = length(lines)
    
    if total_lines <= @code_target_lines do
      [%{content: content, sequence_index: 0, lines: 1..total_lines}]
    else
      chunk_lines(lines, 0, 0, [])
    end
  end

  defp chunk_lines([], _line_idx, _seq_stats, acc), do: Enum.reverse(acc)

  defp chunk_lines(remaining_lines, start_line_idx, seq_index, acc) do
    # Take target lines
    potential_chunk = Enum.take(remaining_lines, @code_target_lines)
    
    # If we have fewer than target, this is the last chunk
    if length(potential_chunk) < @code_target_lines do
       chunk_content = Enum.join(potential_chunk, "\n")
       end_line = start_line_idx + length(potential_chunk)
       new_acc = [%{content: chunk_content, sequence_index: seq_index, lines: (start_line_idx + 1)..end_line} | acc]
       Enum.reverse(new_acc)
    else
       # We have a full chunk. We need to decide where to cut.
       # "Heuristic: If a line starts with no indentation (Column 0), it is likely a function/class definition start."
       # We want to avoid cutting *inside* a function if possible.
       # We look at the last few lines to see if we can find a good break point?
       # Actually the prompt says: "Try to cut before these lines to keep functions intact."
       
       # This implies we might extend or shrink the chunk.
       # For simplicity, let's take @code_target_lines, then look ahead for the NEXT function definition?
       # Or look backwards from the cut point?
       
       # "Strategy B: Code Shredding (Line Blocks)... Target: ~50 lines... Overlap: 5 lines."
       
       # Let's just implement strict sliding window of lines first, as advanced heuristic parsing without an AST is flaky.
       # The prompt mentions checking indentation.
       
       chunk_content = Enum.join(potential_chunk, "\n")
       end_line = start_line_idx + length(potential_chunk)
       
       chunk = %{content: chunk_content, sequence_index: seq_index, lines: (start_line_idx + 1)..end_line}
       
       # Next iteration
       # Overlap: 5 lines.
       # Meaning we advance by target - overlap
       advance = @code_target_lines - @code_overlap_lines
       
       # But wait, remaining_lines is a list. We need to Drop `advance` lines.
       next_remaining = Enum.drop(remaining_lines, advance)
       
       # Beware of infinite loop if advance is 0, but it is 45.
       
       chunk_lines(next_remaining, start_line_idx + advance, seq_index + 1, [chunk | acc])
    end
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: file_ingestion_task.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.Ingestion.FileIngestionTask do
  @moduledoc """
  The Cognitive Ingestion Pipeline (File Level).
  """

  require Logger
  alias CMS.{Node, NodeHead, NodeBody, NodeTail, NodeAntenna, DataHead, DataTail, Edge}
  alias CMS.Infrastructure.OllamaBridge
  alias CMS.Tool.Embedder # Using the Python Bridge tool
  alias CMS.Ingestion.ExclusionCortex

  # Model Constants
  @summary_model "qwen3:0.6b"
  @embedding_model "all-MiniLM-L6-v2" # Defined in ml_bridge.py

  @doc """
  Ingests a single file into the CDMS.
  """
  def ingest(path, parent_id, spatial_coords \\ {0.0, 0.0, 0}, width_descriptor \\ "m0", opts \\ []) do
    uuid = UUID.uuid5(:oid, path)

    # Idempotency Check: See if node already exists in Mnesia
    case :mnesia.dirty_read(:nodes_table, uuid) do
      [{:nodes_table, ^uuid, _hash, _parent, _head, %{data_head: %{summary: "Processing failed" <> _}}, _tail, _stack, _provenance, _antenna}] ->
        # Existing node found but it was a failure. Re-ingest.
        Logger.info("FileIngestionTask: Re-ingesting previously failed node: #{path}")
        do_ingest(uuid, path, parent_id, spatial_coords, width_descriptor, opts)

      [{:nodes_table, ^uuid, _hash, _parent, _head, _body, _tail, _stack, _provenance, _antenna}] ->
        # Node exists and looks valid. Ensure actor is running.
        case CMS.NodeSupervisor.start_node_from_store(uuid) do
          {:ok, _pid} -> :ok
          _ -> :ok
        end
        {:ok, uuid}

      [] ->
        do_ingest(uuid, path, parent_id, spatial_coords, width_descriptor, opts)
    end
  end

  defp do_ingest(_uuid, path, parent_id, spatial_coords, width_descriptor, opts) do
        Logger.info("Cognitive Ingestion Task: Starting for #{path}")
        edge_generation = Keyword.get(opts, :edge_generation, true)

        # Step 1: Physical Assessment & Decision Gate
        case ExclusionCortex.assess_file(path) do
          {:ok, assessed_mode, _reason} ->
            # Prioritize cognitive_mode from opts over physical assessment
            # This allows deterministic mode to override LLM assessment
            mode = Keyword.get(opts, :cognitive_mode, assessed_mode)
            provenance = Keyword.get(opts, :provenance, %{"source" => "local_ingestion"})
            process_file(path, parent_id, mode, spatial_coords, width_descriptor, edge_generation, provenance)

          {:error, reason} ->
            Logger.error("Failed to assess file #{path}: #{inspect(reason)}")
            {:error, reason}
        end
    end

  defp process_file(path, parent_id, mode, spatial_coords, width_descriptor, edge_generation, provenance) do
    # Step 2: Content Extraction & Hashing
    {content, hash} = read_and_hash(path)
    mime = detect_mime(path)

    # Step 3: The Cognitive Request (Ollama - Inference)
    cognition_result = 
      cond do
        mode == :deterministic ->
          # Use Deterministic Aggregator (Dimension 1-5)
          summary = CMS.Ingestion.DeterministicAggregator.aggregate(path, content, mime)
          # In deterministic mode, we can extract keywords as concepts
          concepts = summary 
                     |> String.split("[KEYWORDS]: ") 
                     |> List.last() 
                     |> String.split("\n") 
                     |> List.first() 
                     |> String.split(", ")
          {:ok, {summary, concepts}}

        mode == :mode_a_deep ->
          # Use Qwen via Ollama for summarization
          case OllamaBridge.summarize(content, @summary_model, path) do
            {:ok, %{"summary" => s, "concepts" => c}} ->
              case ExclusionCortex.validate_cognition(s) do
                :ok -> {:ok, {s, c}}
                {:error, gate_reason} -> 
                  Logger.error("Ingestion ABORTED for #{path}: Semantic Gate failure (#{gate_reason})")
                  {:error, :semantic_gate_failure}
              end
            {:error, ollama_reason} -> 
              Logger.error("Ingestion ABORTED for #{path}: Ollama error (#{inspect(ollama_reason)})")
              {:error, :ollama_failure}
          end

        true ->
          {:ok, {"Shallow representation of binary or large file.", []}}
      end

    case cognition_result do
      {:ok, {summary, concepts}} ->
        continue_ingestion(path, parent_id, mode, spatial_coords, width_descriptor, edge_generation, content, hash, mime, summary, concepts, provenance)
      {:error, reason} ->
        {:error, reason}
    end
  end

  defp continue_ingestion(path, parent_id, mode, spatial_coords, width_descriptor, edge_generation, content, hash, mime, summary, concepts, provenance) do

    # -- PATCH: Sanitize Inputs --
    content = if String.valid?(content), do: content, else: "[BINARY OMITTED]"
    summary = if String.valid?(summary), do: summary, else: "[BINARY OMITTED]"
    # ----------------------------
    # -- UTF-8 SANITIZATION PATCH --
    safe_content = if String.valid?(content), do: content, else: "[BINARY CONTENT OMITTED]"
    # ------------------------------
    

    # Step 4: Vectorization (Python ML Bridge)
    # Replaces OllamaBridge.embed with CMS.Tool.Embedder.generate
    vector_input = """
    FILE: #{Path.basename(path)}
    SUMMARY: #{summary}
    CONTENT:
    #{safe_content}
    """
    
    # Defaults to all-MiniLM-L6-v2 in ml_bridge.py
    embedding = case Embedder.generate(vector_input, @embedding_model) do
      {:ok, tensor} -> 
         tensor
      {:error, reason} ->
         Logger.warning("Embedding failed for #{path}: #{inspect(reason)}. Using zero-vector.")
         Nx.broadcast(Nx.tensor(0.0), {384})
    end

    # Step 5: Salience & Antenna Tuning
    geography = CMS.Ingestion.FractalAperture.analyze_geography(path)
    base_salience = CMS.SalienceEngine.calculate(summary, provenance, mode: mode)
    salience = CMS.Ingestion.FractalAperture.adjust_salience(base_salience, geography)
    
    antenna = NodeAntenna.new(path, salience)
    
    # Optional logic: If gate failed, dampen significantly regardless of content
    antenna = if summary == "Processing failed (Semantic Gate)", do: %{antenna | gain: 0.1, salience: 0.05}, else: antenna

    # Step 6: Identify & Pre-Register (Required for Edge Resolution)
    uuid = UUID.uuid5(:oid, path)
    filename = Path.basename(path)
    stem = Path.rootname(filename)

    CMS.VectorRouter.register_name(filename, uuid)
    CMS.VectorRouter.register_name(stem, uuid)
    
    # Register Module Names for Syntactic Resolution
    if Path.extname(path) in [".ex", ".exs"] do
      case Code.string_to_quoted(content) do
        {:ok, quoted} ->
          Macro.prewalk(quoted, fn
            {:defmodule, _, [{:__aliases__, _, names} | _]} ->
              CMS.VectorRouter.register_name(Enum.join(names, "."), uuid)
              nil
            other -> other
          end)
        _ -> :ok
      end
    end

    # Add to HNSW Index immediately so semantic search can find it
    CMS.VectorRouter.add_embedding(uuid, embedding, @embedding_model)

    # Step 7: Struct Construction
    head = NodeHead.new(uuid, embedding, @embedding_model, 0.35, spatial_coords, width_descriptor)
    data_head = DataHead.new(summary, concepts)
    
    # Anatomy Engine Integration
    # 1. Classify Organ
    triage_class = CMS.Ingestion.TriageProtocol.identify(path) |> case do {:ok, c} -> c; _ -> :unknown end
    _organ_type = CMS.Ingestion.AnatomyEngine.classify_organ(triage_class, path, mime) # Store this if needed in provenance?
    
    # 2. Extract Essence (Ledger Data)
    essence = CMS.Ingestion.AnatomyEngine.extract_essence(path)
    
    # 3. Classify Metabolism
    metabolism = CMS.Ingestion.AnatomyEngine.classify_metabolism(path, triage_class)
    
    # Create DataTail with full Anatomical Ledger
    data_ledger = DataTail.new(path, hash, mime, salience, essence.permissions, essence.ownership, essence.immutable_flags, essence.genetic_provenance, metabolism)
    
    body = NodeBody.new(data_head, if(mode in [:mode_a_deep, :deterministic], do: content, else: nil), data_ledger, if(mode == :mode_a_deep, do: :deep, else: :shallow))
    
    node = Node.new(head, body, antenna, provenance)

    # Add link to parent
    node = if parent_id do
      edge = Edge.new(parent_id, :child_of, 1.0)
      %{node | tail: NodeTail.add_edge(node.tail, edge)}
    else
      node
    end

    # Step 8: Edge Forging (Now with registration data available)
    node = if edge_generation do
      CMS.Core.Logic.EdgeGenerator.generate_edges(node)
    else
      node
    end
    
    # Step 9: Mnesia Persistence
    path_hash = UUID.uuid5(:oid, path)
    
    :mnesia.dirty_write({:nodes_table, uuid, path_hash, parent_id, node.head, node.body, node.tail, CMS.ChronoStack.new(), node.provenance, node.antenna})

    # Append to epoch log for rehydration capability
    CMS.LogAppender.append_node_event(uuid, :node_created, node)

    # Step 10: Spawn Actor
    case CMS.NodeSupervisor.spawn_node(node) do
      {:ok, pid} -> 
        CMS.SymbolicIndex.register_path(path, pid)
        
        # Defer scatter-gather to avoid cascade timeouts during genesis
        # The semantic edge generation will happen after enough nodes exist
        if edge_generation do
          Process.send_after(pid, :delayed_scatter_gather, 10_000)
        end
        
        CMS.Services.LiveWire.watch(path)
        
        Logger.info("Successfully ingested node: #{uuid} (#{Path.basename(path)})")
        {:ok, uuid}
      error ->
        Logger.error("Failed to spawn node for #{path}: #{inspect(error)}")
        error
    end
  end

  # --- Helpers ---

  defp read_and_hash(path) do
    content = safe_read(path, 3)
    hash = :crypto.hash(:sha256, content) |> Base.encode16(case: :lower)
    {content, hash}
  end

  defp safe_read(path, retries) do
    case File.read(path) do
      {:ok, content} -> content
      {:error, reason} when retries > 0 ->
        jitter = :rand.uniform(100)
        Logger.warning("File locked or busy: #{path} (#{inspect(reason)}). Retrying in #{100 + jitter}ms... (#{retries} left)")
        :timer.sleep(100 + jitter)
        safe_read(path, retries - 1)
      {:error, reason} ->
        Logger.error("Failed to read file after retries: #{path} (#{inspect(reason)})")
        raise "File access failure: #{path}"
    end
  end

  defp detect_mime(path) do
    case Path.extname(path) do
      ".ex" -> "text/x-elixir"
      ".exs" -> "text/x-elixir"
      ".py" -> "text/x-python"
      ".js" -> "application/javascript"
      ".md" -> "text/markdown"
      _ -> "application/octet-stream"
    end
  end
end

------------------------------------------------------------
File: deterministic_aggregator.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.Ingestion.DeterministicAggregator do
  @moduledoc """
  The Deterministic Aggregator: Gathers hard technical facts to build a 
  multi-dimensional profile (Summary Blob) of a node without LLM latency.
  """
  require Logger
  alias CMS.Ingestion.AnatomyEngine

  @language_rules %{
    # C-Style Family (C, C++, C#, Java, JS, TS, Go, Rust, Swift, PHP, Kotlin, Scala)
    c_style: %{
      extensions: ~w(.c .cpp .h .hpp .cs .java .js .ts .jsx .tsx .go .rs .swift .php .kt .scala),
      line_comment: "//",
      block_comment: {~r/\/\*/, ~r/\*\//},
      import_pattern: ~r/^\s*(?:import|using|include|require|package)\s+([a-zA-Z0-9_\.\/"\*-]+)/m,
      function_pattern: ~r/(?:func|fn|function|def|void|int|bool|string|public|private|protected)\s+([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)\s*\(/
    },
    # Scripting Family (Python, Ruby, Perl, Shell, YAML, TOML, Dockerfile, Make)
    script_style: %{
      extensions: ~w(.py .rb .pl .sh .bash .zsh .yaml .yml .toml .dockerfile .make .makefile .conf .properties),
      line_comment: "#",
      block_comment: nil, # Python has docstrings, handled separately or as logic
      import_pattern: ~r/^\s*(?:import|from|require|use|include)\s+([a-zA-Z0-9_\.]+)/m,
      function_pattern: ~r/(?:def|function|func)\s+([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)/
    },
    # Elixir
    elixir_style: %{
      extensions: ~w(.ex .exs),
      line_comment: "#",
      block_comment: nil,
      import_pattern: ~r/^\s*(?:alias|import|require|use)\s+([a-zA-Z0-9_\.]+)/m,
      function_pattern: nil # Handled by AST
    },
    # Erlang
    erlang_style: %{
      extensions: ~w(.erl),
      line_comment: "%%",
      block_comment: nil,
      import_pattern: ~r/^-include\(".*"\)\./m,
      function_pattern: ~r/^([a-z0-9_]+)\s*\(.*\)\s*->/m
    },
    # SQL
    sql_style: %{
      extensions: ~w(.sql),
      line_comment: "--",
      block_comment: {~r/\/\*/, ~r/\*\//},
      import_pattern: nil,
      function_pattern: ~r/(?:CREATE\s+FUNCTION|CREATE\s+PROCEDURE)\s+([a-zA-Z0-9_]+)/i
    },
    # Lisp (Clojure, Scheme)
    lisp_style: %{
      extensions: ~w(.clj .lisp .scm .el),
      line_comment: ";;",
      block_comment: nil,
      import_pattern: ~r/\((?:require|use|import)\s+'?([a-zA-Z0-9\.\/-]+)/,
      function_pattern: ~r/\((?:defn|defun)\s+([a-zA-Z0-9_\-\.]+)/
    },
    # Web Markup (HTML, XML)
    markup_style: %{
      extensions: ~w(.html .xml .svg .xhtml .vue .svelte),
      line_comment: nil,
      block_comment: {~r/<!--/, ~r/-->/},
      import_pattern: ~r/<(?:script|link|import).*?(?:src|href)=["'](.*?)["']/,
      function_pattern: nil
    },
    # Lua
    lua_style: %{
      extensions: ~w(.lua),
      line_comment: "--",
      block_comment: {~r/--\[\[/, ~r/\]\]/},
      import_pattern: ~r/(?:require|dofile)\s*\(?["'](.*?)["']\)?/,
      function_pattern: ~r/function\s+([a-zA-Z0-9_\.]+)/
    },
    # Documentation & Data
    data_doc_style: %{
      extensions: ~w(.md .json .csv .log .out .err),
      line_comment: nil,
      block_comment: nil,
      import_pattern: nil,
      function_pattern: nil
    },
    # Archives
    archive_style: %{
      extensions: ~w(.zip .tar .gz .7z .rar .jar .war .ear .iso),
      line_comment: nil,
      block_comment: nil,
      import_pattern: nil,
      function_pattern: nil,
      is_binary: true
    },
    # Application Packages & Binaries
    package_style: %{
      extensions: ~w(.exe .app .deb .rpm .bin .dll .so .dylib .dmg .msi),
      line_comment: nil,
      block_comment: nil,
      import_pattern: nil,
      function_pattern: nil,
      is_binary: true
    }
  }

  @doc """
  Aggregates seven dimensions of introspection into a standardized Summary Blob.
  """
  def aggregate(path, content, mime) do
    # 1. Identify Logic Rules based on Extension
    ext = Path.extname(path) |> String.downcase()
    rules = get_rules_for_ext(ext)

    intent = get_intent(path, content, rules)
    structure = get_structure(path, content, rules)
    imports = get_imports(content, rules)
    comments = get_all_comments(content, rules)
    keywords = get_keywords(path, content, rules)
    geography = get_geography(path)
    anatomy = get_anatomy(path, mime)

    """
    [INTENT]: #{intent}
    [STRUCTURE]: #{structure}
    [IMPORTS]: #{imports}
    [COMMENTS]: #{comments}
    [KEYWORDS]: #{keywords}
    [GEOGRAPHY]: #{geography}
    [ANATOMY]: #{anatomy}
    """ |> String.trim()
  end

  defp get_rules_for_ext(ext) do
    Enum.find_value(@language_rules, %{}, fn {_style, rules} -> 
      if ext in rules.extensions, do: rules, else: nil
    end)
  end

  # --- Dimension 1: The Declared Intent ---
  defp get_intent(path, content, rules) do
    ext = Path.extname(path) |> String.downcase()
    cond do
      ext in [".ex", ".exs"] ->
         case Regex.run(~r/@moduledoc\s+"""\s*(.*?)\s*"""/s, content) do
          [_, doc] -> clean_doc(doc)
          _ -> "No module documentation found."
        end
      ext == ".py" ->
        case Regex.run(~r/^["']{3}\s*(.*?)\s*["']{3}/s, content) do
          [_, doc] -> clean_doc(doc)
          _ -> "No docstring found."
        end
      ext == ".md" ->
        case Regex.run(~r/^#\s+(.+)$/m, content) do
          [_, title] -> "Documentation Title: #{title}"
          _ -> "Markdown documentation."
        end
      ext in ~w(.log .out .err) ->
        "System log file capturing runtime events."
      true ->
        cond do
          Map.get(rules, :is_binary) ->
             if ext in ~w(.zip .tar .gz .7z .rar .jar .war .ear .iso), do: "Compressed archive artifact.", else: "Executable or distribution package."
          true ->
            # Fallback to first comment block from logic rules
            block_def = Map.get(rules, :block_comment)
            intent_from_block = case block_def do
              {start_regex, end_regex} -> 
                 # We want to match from the VERY FIRST occurrence of start_regex
                 # but only if it's near the top of the file (first 50 lines)
                 # Regex.source extracts the pattern without sigil metadata
                 pattern = "(?s)#{Regex.source(start_regex)}\\s*(.*?)\\s*#{Regex.source(end_regex)}"
                 case Regex.run(Regex.compile!(pattern), content) do
                   [_, doc] -> clean_doc(doc)
                   _ -> nil
                 end
              _ -> nil
            end

            # 2. Fallback to Line Comments
            if intent_from_block do
               intent_from_block
            else
               prefix = Map.get(rules, :line_comment)
               if prefix do
                  extract_continuous_comments(content, prefix)
               else
                  "System file (Binary or Data)."
               end
            end
        end
    end
  end

  defp clean_doc(doc) do
    doc |> String.split("\n") |> Enum.map(&String.trim/1) |> Enum.join(" ") |> String.trim()
  end

  defp extract_continuous_comments(content, prefix) do
    # Optimized: Only look at the first 5kb to avoid RAM-bombing on large files
    head = String.slice(content, 0, 5000)
    
    lines = String.split(head, "\n")
    
    # Handle Shebangs (skip if first line starts with #!)
    lines = case lines do
      ["#!" <> _ | rest] -> rest
      _ -> lines
    end

    comments = Enum.take_while(lines, &String.starts_with?(String.trim(&1), prefix))
    
    if comments != [] do
       Enum.map(comments, fn l -> String.trim(l) |> String.replace_prefix(prefix, "") |> String.trim() end)
       |> Enum.filter(&(&1 != "")) |> Enum.join(" ")
    else
      "No header comments found."
    end
  end

  # --- Dimension 2: The Functional Surface [STRUCTURE] ---
  defp get_structure(path, content, rules) do
     ext = Path.extname(path) |> String.downcase()
     cond do
       ext in [".ex", ".exs"] ->
          try do
            case Code.string_to_quoted(content) do
              {:ok, quoted} ->
                {modules, functions} = analyze_elixir_structure(quoted)
                "Module: #{Enum.join(modules, ", ")} | Exports: #{Enum.join(functions, ", ")}"
              _ -> "Structure analysis failed (Syntax Error)."
            end
          rescue
            _ -> "Structure analysis failed (Parser Crash)."
          end
       ext == ".md" ->
          headers = Regex.scan(~R/^#{1,3}\s+(.+)$/m, content) |> Enum.map(&List.last/1)
          "Headers: #{Enum.join(headers, " > ")}"
       ext == ".json" ->
          # Very naive top-level key extraction
          keys = Regex.scan(~r/"([a-zA-Z0-9_-]+)"\s*:/, content) 
                 |> Enum.map(&List.last/1) 
                 |> Enum.uniq() 
                 |> Enum.take(20)
          "JSON Keys: #{Enum.join(keys, ", ")}"
       ext == ".csv" ->
          header_line = String.split(content, "\n") |> List.first() |> String.split(",") |> Enum.map(&String.trim/1)
          "CSV Columns: #{Enum.join(header_line, ", ")}"
       ext in ~w(.log .out .err) ->
          has_errors = String.contains?(String.upcase(content), "ERROR")
          "Log features: #{if has_errors, do: "CONTAINS ERRORS", else: "Clean"}"
       true ->
          cond do
            Map.get(rules, :is_binary) ->
              if ext in ~w(.zip .tar .gz .7z .rar .jar .war .ear .iso), do: "Binary container/Archive.", else: "Binary executable/Deployment artifact."
            true ->
              # Regex-based extraction using rules
              pattern = Map.get(rules, :function_pattern)
              if pattern do
                 funcs = Regex.scan(pattern, content)
                         |> Enum.map(&List.last/1)
                         |> Enum.uniq()
                         |> Enum.take(30)
                 "Functions/Defs: #{Enum.join(funcs, ", ")}"
              else
                 "Structure implicit or undefined."
              end
          end
     end
  end

  defp analyze_elixir_structure(quoted) do
    {_ast, acc} = Macro.prewalk(quoted, {[], []}, fn
      {:defmodule, _, [{:__aliases__, _, names} | _]}, {mods, funs} ->
        {nil, {[Enum.join(names, ".") | mods], funs}}
      {:def, _, [{name, _, args} | _]}, {mods, funs} ->
        {nil, {mods, funs ++ ["#{name}/#{length(args || [])}"]}}
      node, acc -> {node, acc}
    end)
    {Enum.reverse(elem(acc, 0)), Enum.reverse(elem(acc, 1))}
  end

  # --- Dimension 3: [IMPORTS] ---
  defp get_imports(content, rules) do
    if Map.get(rules, :is_binary) do
       "Not applicable to binary artifacts."
    else
      pattern = Map.get(rules, :import_pattern)
      if pattern do
        Regex.scan(pattern, content)
        |> Enum.map(&List.last/1)
        |> Enum.uniq()
        |> Enum.join(", ")
        |> case do
          "" -> "None detected."
          s -> s
        end
      else
        "Import extraction not supported."
      end
    end
  end

  # --- Dimension 4: [COMMENTS] ---
  defp get_all_comments(content, rules) do
    if Map.get(rules, :is_binary) do
       "Not applicable to binary artifacts."
    else
      line_prefix = Map.get(rules, :line_comment)
      block_def = Map.get(rules, :block_comment)

      # 1. Extract Line Comments (Unanchored as per user request: Catch all potential comments)
      line_comments = if line_prefix do
         # Use regex scan instead of String.split to avoid RAM explosion
         # Matches: Anything -> prefix -> capture content
         # We allow false positives (like URLs) to ensure we don't miss inline comments
         pattern = ~r/#{Regex.escape(line_prefix)}(.*)$/m
         Regex.scan(pattern, content)
         |> Enum.map(fn 
              [_, capture] -> String.trim(capture)
              _ -> ""
            end)
         |> Enum.filter(&(&1 != ""))
      else
        []
      end

      # 2. Extract Block Comments
      block_comments = case block_def do
        {start_regex, end_regex} ->
            # robustly scan for all block comments
            pattern = "(?s)#{Regex.source(start_regex)}(.*?)#{Regex.source(end_regex)}"
            case Regex.compile(pattern) do
              {:ok, regex} ->
                Regex.scan(regex, content)
                |> Enum.map(fn match -> 
                     # match is usually [full_match, capture] or just [full_match]
                     List.last(match) |> String.trim()
                   end)
              _ -> []
            end
        _ -> []
      end

      all = line_comments ++ block_comments
      if all == [] do
        "No comments found."
      else
        # Limit size to prevent massive blobs
        Enum.join(all, " -- ") |> String.slice(0, 5000) 
      end
    end
  end

  # --- Dimension 5: Keywords ---
  defp get_keywords(path, content, rules) do
     stop_words = ~w(the a an and or but if then else for each while in of to is as with from which this that these those return import package end do void public class const var let module def fn function string int bool)
     
     source = if Map.get(rules, :is_binary), do: Path.basename(path), else: content

     source
     |> String.split(~r/[^a-zA-Z0-9_]+/, trim: true)
     |> Enum.reduce(%{}, fn token, acc -> score_token(token, acc, stop_words) end)
     |> Enum.sort_by(fn {_word, score} -> score end, :desc)
     |> Enum.take(10) # Expanded to 10
     |> Enum.map(fn {word, _} -> word end)
     |> Enum.join(", ")
  end

  defp score_token(token, acc, stop_words) do
    lower = String.downcase(token)
    if String.length(token) < 3 or Enum.member?(stop_words, lower) or String.match?(token, ~r/^[0-9]+$/) do
      acc
    else
      base_score = Map.get(acc, token, 0)
      additional = 1
      additional = if String.match?(token, ~r/^[A-Z][a-z]+[A-Z]/), do: additional + 2, else: additional
      Map.put(acc, token, base_score + additional)
    end
  end

  # --- Dimension 6: Geography ---
  defp get_geography(path) do
    components = Path.split(path) |> Enum.reverse()
    cond do
      "controllers" in components -> "Web Controller"
      "views" in components -> "Web View"
      "templates" in components -> "UI Template"
      "migrations" in components -> "DB Migration"
      "models" in components -> "Data Model"
      "services" in components -> "Service Layer"
      "test" in components -> "Test Suite"
      true -> "Path: #{Path.dirname(path)}"
    end
  end

  # --- Dimension 7: Biology ---
  defp get_anatomy(path, mime) do
     triage_class = case CMS.Ingestion.TriageProtocol.identify(path) do
       {:ok, c} -> c
       _ -> :unknown
     end
     "OrganType: #{AnatomyEngine.classify_organ(triage_class, path, mime)} | Metabolism: #{AnatomyEngine.classify_metabolism(path, triage_class)}"
  end
end



------------------------------------------------------------
File: mime_guard.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.Ingestion.MimeGuard do
  @moduledoc """
  Determines if a file is safe to ingest (Text/Code) or rejected (Binary).
  """

  @allowlist ~w(.txt .md .markdown .ex .exs .py .js .ts .json .html .css .yml .yaml .toml .gitignore .dockerfile .sh .bat .ps1)
  
  @doc """
  Checks if the file at the given path is safe to ingest.
  """
  def check(path) when is_binary(path) do
    with :ok <- check_extension(path),
         :ok <- check_content(path) do
      :ok
    end
  end

  defp check_extension(path) do
    ext = Path.extname(path) |> String.downcase()
    if ext in @allowlist do
      :ok
    else
      {:error, {:unsupported_extension, ext}}
    end
  end

  defp check_content(path) do
    case File.open(path, [:read, :binary]) do
      {:ok, file} ->
        result = read_header(file)
        File.close(file)
        result
      {:error, reason} -> {:error, reason}
    end
  end

  defp read_header(file) do
    case IO.binread(file, 1024) do
      :eof -> :ok # Empty file is safe
      data when is_binary(data) ->
        if String.contains?(data, <<0>>) do
          {:error, :binary_file}
        else
          :ok
        end
      {:error, reason} -> {:error, reason}
    end
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: anatomy_engine.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.Ingestion.AnatomyEngine do
  @moduledoc """
  The Anatomy Engine: Determines the extended anatomical type of the node and extracts physical essence.
  """
  
  require Logger
  import Bitwise

  # --- 1. Extended Node Types (The Organs) ---

  @doc """
  Determines the Extended Node Type based on Phase 1 classification and specific heuristics.
  """
  def classify_organ(triage_class, path, mime) do
    ext = Path.extname(path) |> String.downcase()
    
    cond do
      # 1. Hardware/System Interfaces
      String.starts_with?(path, "/dev/") -> :device_interface
      
      # 2. Tool Organs (Executables/Binaries)
      triage_class == :tool or 
      ext in ~w(.exe .bin .cmd .bat .com .app .vb .vbs .msi .deb .rpm .apk .jar .run) or
      mime in ["application/x-executable", "application/x-mach-binary", "application/x-java-applet", "application/x-dosexec"] ->
        :binary_organ
        
      # 3. Connective Organs (Libraries/Modules)
      triage_class == :connector or
      ext in ~w(.dll .so .dylib .sys .drv .ocx .kext .lib .a) ->
        :ligament
        
      # 4. Instruction Organs (Scripts/Source)
      triage_class == :script or
      ext in ~w(.py .pyc .pl .rb .sh .bash .zsh .js .ts .jsx .tsx .php .java .c .cpp .h .hpp .go .rs .swift .kt .scala .lua .r .ex .exs .erl .beam) ->
        :reflex_arc
        
      # 5. Configuration Organs
      is_config_file?(path) or
      ext in ~w(.conf .cfg .ini .yaml .yml .toml .list .plist) ->
        :configuration_nexus
        
      # 6. Memory & Storage Organs
      ext in ~w(.json .xml .sql .db .sqlite) or
      mime in ["application/json", "application/xml", "application/sql", "application/x-sqlite3"] ->
        :structured_memory
        
      # 7. Sensory & Archival Organs (Higher priority than general tissue)
      ext in ~w(.jpg .jpeg .png .gif .bmp .ico .svg .webp) or
      String.starts_with?(mime, "image/") ->
        :visual_cortex
        
      ext in ~w(.mp3 .wav .ogg) or
      String.starts_with?(mime, "audio/") ->
        :auditory_nerve
        
      ext in ~w(.mp4 .mov .avi .mkv) or
      String.starts_with?(mime, "video/") ->
        :sensory_nexus

      ext in ~w(.zip .tar .gz .bz2 .7z .rar .iso .img) or
      mime in ["application/zip", "application/x-tar", "application/gzip", "application/x-7z-compressed", "application/x-rar"] ->
        :encapsulated_cortex
        
      ext in ~w(.pdf .doc .docx .xls .xlsx .ppt .pptx) or
      mime in ["application/pdf", "application/msword", "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.wordprocessingml.document"] ->
        :archival_memory

      ext in ~w(.ttf .otf .woff .woff2) or String.starts_with?(mime, "font/") ->
        :typographic_matrix

      # 8. General Tissue (Data/Config) - Fallback for Text
      ext in ~w(.txt .csv .tsv .md .markdown .htm .html .css .scss) or
      String.starts_with?(mime, "text/") ->
        if ext == ".log", do: :pulsing_vein, else: :semantic_tissue

      true -> :tissue_sample
    end
  end
  
  defp is_config_file?(path) do
    String.contains?(path, "/etc/") or 
    String.match?(path, ~r/(\.conf|\.cfg|\.ini|\.yaml|\.yml|\.toml|\.list|\.plist)$/) or
    Path.basename(path) in ["sys.config", "runtime.exs", "config.exs", "mix.exs"]
  end

  # --- 2. Metabolism (The Heartbeat) ---

  @doc """
  Classifies every node based on its expected metabolic rate.
  - :static_bone -> Rare changes (Binaries, Configs)
  - :fluid_blood -> Constant flux (Logs, Cache, Temp)
  """
  def classify_metabolism(path, triage_class) do
    filename = Path.basename(path) |> String.downcase()
    
    cond do
      # 1. Explicit Bone types always win (Stability)
      triage_class in [:tool, :connector] -> :static_bone
      is_config_file?(path) -> :static_bone
      triage_class == :script -> :static_bone

      # 2. Blood Nodes (High Metabolic Flux) - Checked only if not Bone
      String.ends_with?(filename, [".log", ".tmp", ".cache", "-wal", ".journal", ".swp"]) or 
      String.contains?(path, ["/tmp/", "/var/log/", "/var/cache/", "/run/", "/proc/", "/sys/"]) or
      String.match?(filename, ~r/^[0-9]+$/) -> 
        :fluid_blood

      # 3. Default to Bone
      true -> :static_bone
    end
  end

  # --- 2. The Anatomical Ledger (The Medical Record) ---

  @doc """
  Extracts the 'Essence' of the file: Permissions, Ownership, Flags.
  Returns map of DataTail fields.
  """
  def extract_essence(path) do
    case File.stat(path) do
      {:ok, stat} ->
        %{
          permissions: extract_permissions(stat),
          ownership: extract_ownership(stat),
          immutable_flags: extract_flags(path), # Mock or requires specific OS tools
          genetic_provenance: trace_provenance(path, stat)
        }
      {:error, _} ->
        %{
          permissions: %{}, ownership: %{}, immutable_flags: [], genetic_provenance: %{}
        }
    end
  end

  defp extract_permissions(stat) do
    mode = stat.mode
    %{
      user: mode_to_rwx(Bitwise.band(mode, 0o700) >>> 6),
      group: mode_to_rwx(Bitwise.band(mode, 0o070) >>> 3),
      other: mode_to_rwx(Bitwise.band(mode, 0o007))
    }
  end
  
  defp mode_to_rwx(bits) do
    r = if Bitwise.band(bits, 4) != 0, do: "r", else: "-"
    w = if Bitwise.band(bits, 2) != 0, do: "w", else: "-"
    x = if Bitwise.band(bits, 1) != 0, do: "x", else: "-"
    "#{r}#{w}#{x}"
  end
  
  defp extract_ownership(stat) do
    # In a real generic system, we might shell out to `id -nu` to get names.
    # For now, we store UIDs.
    %{
      uid: stat.uid,
      gid: stat.gid
    }
  end
  
  defp extract_flags(_path) do
    # Would run `lsattr` on Linux or `attrib` on Windows.
    # Placeholder for Phase 3.
    []
  end
  
  # --- 3. Genetic Provenance & Virtual Nodes ---
  
  defp trace_provenance(path, stat) do
    # Heuristic: Identify origin land
    origin = if stat.uid == 0, do: :root_system, else: :user_land
    
    # Identify creator context
    creator = 
      cond do
        String.starts_with?(path, ["/usr", "/bin", "/lib", "/sbin"]) -> :installer
        String.starts_with?(path, "/home") -> :user
        origin == :root_system -> :system_process
        true -> :unknown
      end
      
    %{
      origin: origin,
      creator: creator,
      # Mock package tracing
      package: if(creator == :installer, do: "system_base", else: nil)
    }
  end

  @doc """
  Identifies potential Virtual Nodes associated with this file.
  Returns a list of Virtual Node specifications.
  """
  def spawn_virtuals(path, essence) do
    %{uid: uid, gid: gid} = essence.ownership
    owner_name = Map.get(essence.ownership, :owner_name, "user_#{uid}")

    # 1. Identity Node (Owner)
    identities = [
      {:identity, uid, owner_name},
      {:group_identity, gid, "group_#{gid}"}
    ]
    
    # 2. Package Node (Path-driven)
    packages = 
      cond do
        String.starts_with?(path, ["/usr", "/bin", "/lib"]) ->
          [{:package, essence.genetic_provenance[:package] || "system_base"}]
        true -> []
      end
    
    identities ++ packages
  end
end


------------------------------------------------------------
File: exclusion_cortex.ex
------------------------------------------------------------
defmodule CMS.Ingestion.ExclusionCortex do
  @moduledoc """
  The Intelligent Exclusion Cortex: Filters out noise and protects against "Cognitive Swamp".
  """

  require Logger

  @default_blocklist ~w(.git .svn node_modules _build target venv __pycache__ deps priv mnesia_store .elixir_ls memory_cartridges .gemini locale locale-archive gconv zoneinfo terminfo)
  @max_file_size_bytes 100 * 1024 * 1024 # 100MB

  @doc """
  Gate 1: Explicit Gate (Pattern Matching).
  Decides whether to traverse a directory or drop it.
  """
  def should_traverse?(path) do
    # Check if ANY component of the path is in the blocklist
    components = Path.split(path)
    
    if Enum.any?(components, fn part -> Enum.member?(@default_blocklist, part) end) do
      Logger.debug("ExclusionCortex: Dropping #{path} (Blocklist)")
      false
    else
      true
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Gate 2: Heuristic Gate (The Noise Filter).
  Decides if a file should be deep processed (Mode A) or shallow indexed (Mode B).
  """
  def assess_file(path) do
    case File.stat(path) do
      {:ok, %File.Stat{size: size}} when size > @max_file_size_bytes ->
        {:ok, :mode_b_shallow, :size_limit}

      {:ok, _stat} ->
        cond do
          is_binary?(path) ->
            {:ok, :mode_b_shallow, :binary}
          true ->
            # Blood nodes (logs) are now ALLOWED in Mode A for stream monitoring
            {:ok, :mode_a_deep, :cognitive_target}
        end

      {:error, reason} ->
        {:error, reason}
    end
  end

  @doc """
  Gate 3: Semantic Gate (The Hallucination Check).
  Checks if the LLM output is coherent and wasn't tripped by minified/obfuscated code.
  """
  def validate_cognition(summary) do
    cond do
      is_nil(summary) or String.length(summary) < 15 ->
        Logger.warning("ExclusionCortex: Semantic Gate FAILURE (Low Confidence) - summary: #{inspect(summary)}")
        {:error, :low_confidence}
      String.match?(summary, ~r/(minified|obfuscated|gibberish|cannot summarize)/i) ->
        Logger.warning("ExclusionCortex: Semantic Gate FAILURE (Obfuscated) - summary: #{inspect(summary)}")
        {:error, :obfuscated}
      true ->
        :ok
    end
  end

  defp is_binary?(path) do
    # Check first 1KB for NULL byte, as per spec Gate 2.
    case File.open(path, [:read, :binary]) do
      {:ok, file} ->
        chunk = IO.binread(file, 1024)
        File.close(file)
        is_binary(chunk) and :binary.match(chunk, <<0>>) != :nomatch
      _ -> false
    end
  end
end
